## PDF p5｜簡介

### English — saved correction

Introduction

Modern industrial and commercial organisations rely heavily on the safe and secure supply of electricity. Electricity has many uses within these organisations, but it is essential for lighting, heating, information technology systems and the operation of important machinery and processes.

Electricity is dangerous and without the appropriate training, equipment, or precautions in place working on or near electrical systems can have severe consequences, such as the loss of life, severe injury, damage to property and considerable financial loss.

Whenever electrical work is to be carried out on extensive and complex systems,managers are required to establish a system of written rules and procedures which embody a methodical approach so that the safety principles involved can be clearly understood by everyone. This is especially true in relation to the use, maintenance, and operation of high-voltage switchgear.

High-voltage switchgear must only be operated by people who are competent to do so. Being competent means having sufficient knowledge or experience to prevent danger or injury; or being under such degree of supervision as may be appropriate for the nature of the work. Switchgear operated incorrectly can result in danger both to those operating it and others.

People with the necessary competence to operate switchgear are often referred to as 'Authorised Persons'.



### 繁體中文學習譯文

引言
現代工業與商業組織高度依賴安全且可靠的電力供應。電力在這些組織中有許多用途，而對於照明、供暖、資訊科技系統，以及重要機械與製程的運作而言，更是不可或缺。
電力具有危險性。如果沒有適當的訓練、設備或安全預防措施，在電氣系統上或其附近工作可能造成嚴重後果，例如死亡、嚴重人身傷害、財產損失以及重大的經濟損失。
當需要在大型且複雜的系統上進行電氣工作時，管理人員必須建立一套書面的規則與程序，並在其中採用有系統的方法，使所有人都能清楚理解相關的安全原則。對於高壓開關設備的使用、維護與操作而言，這一點尤其重要。
高壓開關設備只能由具備勝任能力的人員操作。所謂具備勝任能力，是指具有足夠的知識或經驗，能夠防止危險或傷害；或者依工作性質，在適當程度的監督下進行工作。若開關設備操作不當，可能對操作人員以及其他人員造成危險。
具備操作開關設備所需能力的人員，通常稱為「授權人員（Authorised Persons，AP）」。

nature = 工作性質 / 工作本身的特性

## PDF p6｜本手冊使用方式

### English OCR

Using this Workbook

To help you understand how to undertake the roles and responsibilities of an Authorised Person,a training course has been developed in conjunction with this workbook. This is not a self-teaching document. You must attend a training course and have received instructions from a qualified trainer before working assumingthe responsibilities of an Authorised Person working in an industrial or commercial environment.

The amount of training you will require will depend largely upon your previous experience, but there are many aspects involved in the role of Authorised Person and many skills that must be mastered.

When you have completed the training course, you will be assessed for your level of performance and knowledge.

The contents of this workbook have been carefully researched and are believed to be correct but Develop Training Ltd cannot guarantee the accuracy or completeness of any interpretation or statement made in this guide and does not accept liability for such statements or for any information given.

This workbook has been produced to accompany a Develop training course and to inform delegates of current safe working practices.

This workbook contains public sector information published by the Health and Safety Executive (HSE) and licensed under the Open Government Licence v3.0.

Indicates a danger or hazard that may result in serious injury

### 繁體中文學習譯文

為協助你理解授權人員的角色與責任，訓練課程與本手冊配合編製。本手冊並非自學檔案。在工業或商業環境承擔授權人員責任前，必須參加訓練課程並接受合資格講師指導。
所需訓練程度主要取決於過往經驗，但授權人員工作涵蓋許多面向，也有許多必須掌握的技能。完成課程後，將評估你的表現與知識程度。

### English OCR

HV Working - Health and Safety Rules and Regulations

ACOPs

The Health and Safety at Work Act 1974 (HASAWA)

HASAWA is the primary piece of legislation covering occupational health and safety in Great Britain. It sets out general duties for:

Employers

• Provide and maintain safe plant and systems of work

• Reducethe risks to health and safety relating to the use, handling, storage and transport of articles and substances

• Provide information, instruction, training and supervision to ensure the health and safety at work of employees

• Maintain any place of work in a condition that is safe and avoids risks to health, including safe means of access and egress

• Provide and maintain a working environment for employees that is safe, avoids risks to health and has adequate welfare facilities

• Prepare, maintain and communicate a written health and safety policy.

Employees

• Take reasonable care for the health and safety of themselves and others who may be affected by their actions or omissions at work

• Co-operate with the employer as far as necessary to enable them to carry out their duty

• Not to intentionally or recklessly interfere with or misuse anything provided in the interests of health, safety and welfare.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

《工作健康與安全法》（HASAWA）由下位法規（statutory instruments，法定文書）及核准實務守則（ACOPs）配合支援。圖中層級：國會法案 HASAWA → 次級／附屬法規（法定文書）→ ACOPs（詳述法規如何落實）→ 指導檔案（補充 ACOPs）。
《1974 年工作健康與安全法》是大不列顛職業健康與安全的主要法律，訂出一般責任。
僱主：
• 提供並維持安全的裝置與工作制度。
• 降低物品與物質在使用、搬運、儲存及運輸時的健康安全風險。
• 提供資訊、指導、訓練與監督，確保員工工作時的健康與安全。
• 維持安全且不危害健康的工作場所，包括安全的出入通道。
• 提供並維持安全、不危害健康且有足夠福利設施的工作環境。
• 制訂、維護並傳達書面健康安全政策。
員工：
• 合理照顧自己及可能受其工作行為或疏漏影響之他人的健康與安全。
• 在必要範圍內配合僱主履行責任。
• 不故意或魯莽地幹擾、濫用為健康、安全與福利提供的物品。

## PDF p8｜健康安全管理法規與風險評估

### English OCR

The Management of Health and Safety at Work Regulations (MHSWR) 3 “3

MHSWR requires employers to put arrangements in place to control health and safety risks. As a minimum, employers should have the processes and procedures needed to meet the legal requirements, including:

• Complete a formal risk assessment and record significant risks (Regulation 3).

• Record arrangements for health and safety, including planning, organisation, control, monitoring, and review of the preventive and protective measures (Regulation 5).

• Appoint competent persons to assist in meeting the "requirements and prohibitions" (Regulation 7).

• Established procedures for serious or imminent danger (Regulation 8).

• Provide relevant and comprehensible information to employees on risks, precautions, and procedures (Regulation 8).

• Co-operate with other employers who share a workplace (Regulation 11).

• Make arrangements for the safety of employees of other companies working on the premises (Regulation 12).

• Ensure that employees are given safety training at induction or on being transferred to new work (Regulation 13).

• Employees will work in accordance with instructions and training and report shortcomings in any arrangements made about health and safety. (Regulation 14).

Risk assessment

Every employer is required to make a suitable and sufficient assessment of the risks to the health and safety of: {

a) Employees who are exposed whilst at work

b) People who are not employed but may be affected.

A risk assessment is a systematic process of identifying hazards relating to activities or tasks, evaluating the likelihood that someone could be harmed and implementing controls to reduce those risks:

Risk the likelihood

that someone could be harmed by the hazard(s)

### 繁體中文學習譯文

《工作健康與安全管理法規》（MHSWR）要求僱主安排控制健康安全風險的措施。僱主至少應建立符合下列法律要求的流程與程式：
• 完成正式風險評估並記錄重大風險（第 3 條）。
• 記錄健康安全安排，包括預防及保護措施的規劃、組織、控制、監測與檢討（第 5 條）。
• 指派具能力的人員，協助符合各項要求與禁止事項（第 7 條）。
• 建立嚴重或迫切危險的應變程式（第 8 條）。
• 向員工提供相關且易懂的風險、預防措施與程式資訊（原教材標為第 8 條）。
• 與共用工作場所的其他僱主合作（第 11 條）。
• 安排在場內工作的其他公司員工之安全措施（第 12 條）。
• 確保員工入職或轉調新工作時接受安全訓練（第 13 條）。
• 員工應依指導與訓練工作，並通報健康安全安排的缺失（第 14 條）。
風險評估：每位僱主都必須對下列人員的健康安全風險作適當且充分的評估：a）工作時暴露於風險的員工；b）並非其員工但可能受影響的人。
風險評估是一套有系統的程式：辨識活動或任務的危害、評估人員受害的可能性，再實施控制措施以降低風險。圖中 risk 指人員因危害而受傷害的可能性。

> 法規條號按教材保留；本頁為教材翻譯，未將其改寫為現行法律核對結果。

## PDF p9｜風險評估五步驟與安全工作制度

### English OCR

Five steps of risk assessment

Enforce and

monitor the control measures.

Establish and

record the control measures.

Regularly

review risk assessment.

Identify and

record the hazards.

Establish and

record the risks.

V

Safe systems of work

A safe system of work is a formal procedure that results from systematic examination of a task in order that all the hazards are identified. It further explains how safe methods are developed to ensure that hazards are eliminated, and risks are minimised.

A safe system of work will be needed when hazards cannot be physically eliminated, and jome elements of risk remain.

Management Systems HV Switchgear

Establishing and implementing a formal system for the management and control of an electrical distribution system is an effective and practical way of demonstrating compliance with several legal requirements. When correctly implemented, a management system can help address the safety issues identified with the operation, use and maintenance of HV switchgear.

Operational Safety Documents

The implementation of a safety document scheme, generally referred to as a permit-to-work /stem, is recommended as part of a safe system of work for the use, care, and maintenance of high-voltage switchgear. The naming of documents may vary between organisations, but most schemes will include three types of control document:

• Limitation of access- issued to define the physical limits within which a work activity may be carried out, specifying any precautions necessary and hazards that may be present within the work area.

• Permit-to-work - when issued, clearly identifies the equipment to be worked on, the work to be carried out and the actions taken to achieve the conditions which safeguard people working from the dangers which are inherent in an HV system. A permit-to-work should never be issued on equipment that is still live; it should only be issued when the measures required for safety have been completed.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

風險評估五步驟（圖中迴圈）：辨識並記錄危害 → 確定並記錄風險 → 確定並記錄控制措施 → 執行及監測控制措施 → 定期檢討風險評估。
安全工作制度（Safe system of work）是經有系統檢查任務、辨識所有危害後形成的正式程式；也說明如何建立安全方法，消除危害並使風險降至最低。若無法從實體上消除危害，仍有殘餘風險，就需要安全工作制度。
高壓開關裝置管理制度：建立並實施正式的電力配電系統管理與控制制度，是展現符合多項法定要求的有效實際方法。正確實施時，可協助處理高壓開關裝置操作、使用與維護的安全問題。
操作安全檔案：建議把安全檔案制度（通常稱為工作許可制度，permit-to-work system）納入高壓開關裝置使用、照護與維護的安全工作制度。不同組織的檔名稱可能不同，但多數包括三類控制檔案：
• 進入限制（Limitation of access）：界定可進行工作的實際區域，列明必要預防措施及區內可能存在的危害。
• 工作許可（Permit-to-work）：清楚辨識工作裝置、工作內容，以及為保護工作人員免受高壓系統固有危害而已採取的措施。絕不可對仍帶電的裝置發出工作許可；必須完成安全所需措施後才可發出。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p9。


## PDF p10｜測試許可與工作許可責任

### English OCR

• Sanction for test - issued to allow specified HV equipment to be tested, identifying the conditions under which the testing is to be carried out and the actions taken to safeguard the people performing the test, and anyone else who may have access to the area. A sanction for test may allow temporary earth connections applied as part of the protective measures specified for a work activity to be removed for the purposes of testing.

A A permit-to-work should only be issued by someone who is competent and / • \ authorised to do so.

Permits-to-work should only be given to people capable of understanding the precautions and limitations of the activity described on the permit.

Ideally, a permit should be issued at the work location and the task clearly explained to the recipient before the permit is handed over.

DEVELOP

PwitrfthrJTLGrove

AP15- AUTHORISEDPERSON:HV

PERMITTOWORK

The recipient of a permit is responsible for ensuring that the safety precautions identified are adhered to, and that only the permitted work is completed, confined to the area defined in the permit. It is important that the recipient understands these responsibilities and is, to the best of their knowledge, satisfied that the precautions are adequate for the work they or their work group are about to complete when the permit is received.

When work is to be carried out on HV switchgear or equipment, it is likely that those doing the work will need to be designated 'authorised person'.

More information on the operational safety documents specific to this coursecan befound in the "Develop High-voltageSafety Rules and Procedures" section of this workbook.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

• 測試許可（Sanction for test）：允許對指定高壓裝置進行測試，指出測試條件，以及保護測試人員與可能進入區域之其他人員的措施。為測試目的，SFT 可能允許移除原先作為工作保護措施而加設的臨時接地。
工作許可只能由具能力且獲授權的人發出，也只能交給能理解許可所述預防措施與活動限制的人。理想上應在工作地點發出，在交付前向接收人清楚說明工作。
接收人負責確保遵守檔案列明的安全預防措施，只執行許可範圍內的工作，且不超出指定區域。接收時，接收人必須理解這些責任，並依自己所知，確認措施足以保障自己或工作小組即將執行的工作。
在高壓開關裝置或其他高壓裝置工作的人員，可能需要被指定為「授權人員」。
圖示為 AP15 高壓工作許可表（Permit to Work）。
本課程操作安全檔案的更多說明，見本手冊「Develop 高壓安全規則與程式」章。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p10。


## PDF p11｜工作場所電氣法規

### English OCR

The Electricity at Work Regulations

The Electricity at Work Regulations represent a genuine initiative to improve electrical safety. They attempt to ensure that all systems are constructed in such a way to prevent (as far as is reasonably practical) a risk of injury, and that every work activity, including the operation, use, and maintenance of a system should be carried out in such a manner as not to give rise to danger. All places of work are covered by these regulations.

They are detailed, precise and applicable in the most up-to-date work systems.Ignoring them will be regarded as a criminal offence. A thorough knowledge of the Regulations as they apply in any particular workplace, and a genuine desire never to abuse them will significantly reduce the number of injuries caused by the most common source of energy - Electricity.

The regulations cover:

• All systems,plant, and equipment to be designed to ensure maximum practical level of safety.

• Installation and maintenance to reflect specific safety requirements.

• Access, Light and Working Space to be adequate.

• Means of cutting off power and isolating equipment to be available.

• Precautions to be taken to prevent charging.

• No live working unless absolutely essential.

• Specific precautions to be taken where live working is essential. ' All persons to be effectively trained and supervised. “• Responsibility for observing safety policy to be clearly defined.

• All equipment and tools to be appropriate for safe working.

The regulations apply to all operational voltages, electrical systems,and equipment within the workplace. Some of the systems and equipment to which these regulations apply may have to conform with additional standards, for example BS7671,but must ultimately comply with the Electricity at Works Regulations1989.

The HSEguidance document, HSR 25, accompanies the regulation to provide plain speakingguidance on the regulations and gives technical and legal guidance on them

### 繁體中文學習譯文

《工作場所電氣法規》（Electricity at Work Regulations）旨在改善電氣安全，要求所有系統的構造在合理可行範圍內防止傷害風險；所有工作活動，包括系統操作、使用與維護，也應以不產生危險的方式進行。法規涵蓋所有工作場所。
本教材形容法規詳細、明確，適用於現代工作系統；忽視法規會被視為刑事犯罪。充分了解適用於特定工作場所的規定並確實遵守，可大幅減少電氣傷害。
涵蓋事項：
• 所有系統、裝置與裝置的設計，應達到實際可行的最高安全程度。
• 安裝及維護應反映特定安全要求。
• 進出、照明及工作空間應充分。
• 提供切斷電源及隔離裝置的方法。
• 採取措施避免裝置帶有電荷。
• 除非確有必要，不得帶電作業；必要時須採特別預防措施。
• 所有人員都應接受有效訓練與監督。
• 清楚界定遵守安全政策的責任。
• 所有裝置與工具均應適合安全使用。
規定適用於工作場所內所有操作電壓、電氣系統及裝置。部分系統或裝置可能還須符合 BS 7671 等標準，但最終仍須符合《1989 年工作場所電氣法規》。HSE 指南 HSR 25 以白話配合法規，提供技術與法律指引。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p11。


## PDF p12｜法規中的用語定義

### English OCR

Interpretations used in the Regulations:

1. Circuit Conductor means any conductor in a system which is intended to carry electric current in normal conditions, or to be energised in normal conditions and includes a combined neutral and earth conductor but does not include a conductor provided solely to perform a protective function by connection to earth or other reference point.

2. Conductor means a conductor of electrical energy.

3. Danger means risk of injury.

4. Electrical Equipment includes anything used, intended to be used or installed for use, to generate, provide, transmit, transform, rectify, convert, conduct, distribute, control, store, measure or use electrical energy.

5. Injury means death or personal injury from electric shock, electric burn, electrical explosion, or arcing, and from fire or explosion initiated by electrical energy, where any such death or injury is associated with the generation, provision, transmission, transformation, rectification, conversion, conduction, distribution, control, storage, measurement, or use of electrical energy.

6. System means an electrical system in which all the electrical equipment is, or may be, electrically connected to a common source of electrical energy, and includes such source and such equipment.

c

7. Charged/Live - The terms 'charged' and live' have different meanings; they are not defined in the Regulations, so they take their ordinary meaning. 'Live' means that the item in question is at a voltage, by being connected to a source of electricity for example as in normal use. 'Charged' means that the item has acquired a charge either because it is live or because it has become charged by other means such as by static or induction charging or has retained or regained a charge due to capacitance effects - even though it may be disconnected from the rest of the system.

8. Dead - The term 'dead' is not defined in the Regulations so it takes its ordinary meaning. Thus, in the context of the Regulations, for a conductor to be 'dead' means that it is neither 'live' nor 'charged'.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

1. 電路導體（Circuit conductor）：系統內正常情況下預定承載電流或帶電的導體，包括中性與接地合用導體；不包括僅藉連線大地或其他參考點執行保護功能的導體。
2. 導體（Conductor）：傳導電能的物體。
3. 危險（Danger）：傷害風險。
4. 電氣裝置（Electrical equipment）：任何用於、預定用於或安裝供發電、供電、輸電、變壓、整流、轉換、傳導、配電、控制、儲存、量測或使用電能的物品。
5. 傷害（Injury）：與上述電能活動相關，由觸電、電灼傷、電氣爆炸或電弧，以及由電能引起的火災或爆炸造成的死亡或人身傷害。
6. 系統（System）：所有電氣裝置已經或可能連線至共同電源的電氣系統，包括該電源及裝置。
7. 帶電荷／帶電（Charged／Live）：兩詞意義不同，法規未特別定義，故採一般意義。Live 指物體處於某電壓，例如正常使用時接到電源。Charged 指物體已帶有電荷，原因可能是接上電源、靜電、感應，或因電容效應而保留或重新產生電荷；即使已與系統其他部分斷開，也可能如此。
8. 無電（Dead）：法規未特別定義，採一般意義；在此指導體既非 live，也非 charged。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p12。


## PDF p13｜第 3 條：責任人；絕對義務

### English OCR

Regulation3 - Personson whom duties are imposed by these Regulations It is the duty of every employer and self-employed person to comply with the provisions of these Regulations in so far as they relate to matters which are within their control.

While at work every employee has the duty to:

a) to co-operate with their employer, so far as is necessary,to allow the employer to comply with the duties placed on them by the Regulations

b) to comply with the provisions of the Regulations for matters which are within their control.

A person may find themselves responsible for causingdanger to arise elsewhere in an electrical system, at a point beyond their own installation. Because such circumstances are \thin their control, the effect of regulation 3 is to bring responsibilities for compliance with the rest of the Regulations to that person, thus making them a dutyholder.

Managing Director

Authorising

Engineer

Facilities

Management

A SafeSystem of

Work

Authorised

Person

Authorised

Person

Authorised

Person

Competent Person Competent Person

Example of Dutyholder within the Workplace

What ismeant by absolute and reasonablypracticable? Duties in some of the Regulations are subject to the qualifying term 'reasonably practicable'. Where qualifying terms are absent the requirement in the regulation is said to be absolute.

Absolute

If the requirement in a regulation is 'absolute', for example if the requirement is not qualified by the words 'so far as is reasonably practicable', the requirement must be met regardless of cost or any other consideration. Regulations making such absolute requirements are subject to the defence provision of regulation 29.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 3 條：法規施加責任的人員。每位僱主及自僱人士，都有義務就其控制範圍內的事項遵守規定。員工工作時有義務：a）在必要範圍內與僱主合作，使僱主能履行法規責任；b）就自身控制範圍內事項遵守規定。
一個人可能使電氣系統的其他位置產生危險，即使該位置已超出自己的裝置邊界。只要相關情況仍在其控制範圍內，第 3 條就使其成為 dutyholder（法定責任承擔者），須承擔遵守其他條文的責任。
工作場所責任架構示例圖：董事總經理（Managing Director）、授權工程師（Authorising Engineer）、設施管理、安全工作制度、各授權人員（AP）與具能力人員（CP）。連線關係請對照原圖。
「絕對」與「合理可行」是什麼？部分條文附有 reasonably practicable 限定；若未附此類限定，要求即稱為絕對要求。
絕對義務：若要求未以「在合理可行範圍內」等語句限制，就必須遵守，不論成本或其他考量。此類要求仍受第 29 條的抗辯規定所規範。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p13。


## PDF p14｜合理可行；第 4 條系統與工作活動

### English OCR

Reasonably practicable

Generally, you should do everything 'reasonably practicable' to protect people from harm. This means balancing the level of risk against the measuresneeded to control the real risk in terms of money, time, or trouble.

In the context of the Regulations, where the risk is very often that of death and where the precautions are so often simple and affordable the level of duty to prevent that danger approaches that of an absolute duty.

Regulation 4 - Systems, work activities and protective equipment

Regulation 4(1)

"All systems shall at all times be of such construction as to prevent, so far as is reasonably practicable, clanger."

When assessingthe suitability of the construction of an electrical system, consideration should be given to the conditions of actual application or use of the electrical equipment in the system. This will include the testing, commissioning, operation, and maintenance of the equipment throughout the life of the system.

The safety of a system depends upon the proper selection of all the electrical equipment in the system and the proper consideration of the inter-relationship between the individual items of equipment.

Regulation 4(2)

"As may be necessary to prevent danger, all systems shall be maintained so as to prevent, so far as is reasonably practicable, such danger."

This regulation is concerned with the need for maintenance to be carried out to ensure safety of the system, rather than the activity of doing the maintenance safely.

Maintenance should be sufficient to prevent danger so far as reasonably practicable.

Regulation 4(3)

"Every work activity, including operation, use and maintenance of a system and work near a system, shall be carried out in such a manner as not to give rise, sofar as is reasonably practicable, to danger."

### 繁體中文學習譯文

合理可行（Reasonably practicable）：通常應盡一切合理可行措施保護人員免受傷害，即衡量風險程度與控制實際風險所需的金錢、時間及麻煩。電氣法規涉及的風險往往是死亡，而防護通常簡單且負擔得起，因此防止危險的義務常接近絕對義務。
第 4 條：系統、工作活動與保護裝置。
4(1)：所有系統在任何時候，其構造都應在合理可行範圍內防止危險。
評估構造是否適當，須考量裝置實際應用／使用條件，包括系統全生命週期的測試、試運轉、操作及維護。系統安全取決於正確選擇所有裝置，以及妥善考慮裝置間的相互關係。
4(2)：為防止危險，應視需要維護所有系統，使其在合理可行範圍內避免該危險。
本款關注的是藉維護確保系統安全的必要性，而非如何安全地執行維護。維護程度應足以在合理可行範圍內防止危險。
4(3)：每項工作活動，包括系統操作、使用、維護及在系統附近工作，都應以在合理可行範圍內不產生危險的方式進行。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p14。


## PDF p15｜第 4 條：無電作業與保護裝置

### English OCR

This regulation requires that work activities of any sort, whether directly or indirectly associated with an electrical system, must be carried out in a way which, as far as is reasonably practicable, does not give rise to danger.

In the case of electrical work, it is preferable that the conductors are made dead before work starts. In such cases,it is essential that the equipment is isolated, and the conductors proved dead at the point of work before the work starts. Where a test instrument or voltage indicator is used for this purpose, this device should itself be proved immediately before and immediately after testing the conductors.

Safe systems of work incorporating safety isolation procedures are important for work on equipment which is to be made dead before work starts. Particular consideration is needed where multiple sources of supply can exist. Some work, such as fault finding and testing or ( /e jointing by the electricity supply industry, may require electrical equipment to remain energised during the work.

The operation, maintenance, and testing of electrical systems and equipment must only be carried out by those people who are competent for that work.

Regulation4(4)

"Any equipment provided under these Regulationsfor the purpose of protecting persons at work on or near electrical equipment shall be suitablefor the usefor which it is provided, be maintained in a condition suitablefor that use, and be properly used."

, 'Protective equipment' typically refers to those specialtools, protective clothing, and \nsulating screening materials necessary to do work safely on live electrical equipment.

The regulation requires the protective equipment to be:

a) suitable for use

b) maintained in that condition

c) properly used.

Advice on safe working practices is given in HSEguidance.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

任何直接或間接涉及電氣系統的工作，都必須以在合理可行範圍內不產生危險的方式進行。
電氣工作宜在開始前使導體無電。此時必須隔離裝置，並在工作位置確認導體無電。若使用測試儀器或電壓指示器，應在測試導體的緊接之前與之後，確認儀器本身正常。
對於工作前必須無電的裝置，納入安全隔離程式的安全工作制度非常重要；存在多重電源時尤其要注意。部分工作，例如故障查詢、測試，或供電業者的帶電接續，可能需要裝置在工作時保持通電。
電氣系統與裝置的操作、維護及測試，只能由具備該項工作能力的人員執行。
4(4)：依本法規為保護在電氣裝置上或附近工作的人員而提供的裝置，必須適合其用途、維持適用狀態，並正確使用。
保護裝置通常指為安全執行帶電電氣工作所需的特殊工具、防護衣及絕緣遮蔽物。規定要求它們：a）適合使用；b）維持該適用狀態；c）正確使用。HSE 指南提供安全工作實務建議。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p15。


## PDF p16｜第 5、6 條：裝置能力與不利環境

### English OCR

Regulation 5 - Strength & capability of electrical equipment "No electrical equipment shall be put into use where its strength and capability may be exceeded in such a way as may give rise to danger."

Before equipment is energised,the characteristics of the system to which the equipment is connected must be considered. This should include those existing under normal conditions, possible transient conditions, and prospective fault conditions, so that the equipment is not subjected to stress which it is not capable of handling without giving rise to danger. The effects to be considered include voltage stress and the heating and electromagnetic effects of current.

The term 'strength and capability' of electrical equipment refers to the ability of the equipment to withstand the thermal, electromagnetic, electrochemical, or other effects of the electrical currents which might be expected to flow when the equipment is part of a system. These currents include:

• load currents

• transient overloads

• fault currents

• pulses of current

• currents at various power factors and frequencies (ac circuits).

Insulation must be effective to enable the equipment to withstand the applied voltage and any likely transient over-voltages.

Regulation 6 - Adverse or hazardous environments

"Electrical equipment which may reasonably foreseeably be exposed to -

a) mechanical damage;

b) the effects of the weather, natural hazards, temperature or pressure;

c) the effects of wet, dirty, dusty or corrosive conditions; or

d) any flammable or explosive substance, including dusts, vapours or gases,

shall be of such construction or as necessary protected as to prevent, so far as is reasonably practicable, danger arising from such exposure."

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 5 條：電氣裝置的強度與能力。若使用裝置可能超出其強度及能力並產生危險，就不得投入使用。
裝置送電前，必須考量所連線系統在正常、可能的暫態及預期故障條件下的特性，避免裝置承受無法安全應付的應力；包括電壓應力，以及電流的熱效應與電磁效應。
「強度與能力」指裝置承受系統中預期電流所造成之熱、電磁、電化學及其他效應的能力。電流包括負載電流、暫態過載、故障電流、脈衝電流，以及交流電路中不同功率因數與頻率的電流。絕緣必須有效，使裝置承受施加電壓及可能出現的暫態過電壓。
第 6 條：不利或危險環境。若合理可預見裝置會暴露於 a）機械損傷；b）天候、自然危害、溫度或壓力；c）潮濕、髒汙、粉塵或腐蝕環境；d）任何可燃或爆炸性物質，包括粉塵、蒸氣或氣體，則其構造或必要保護應在合理可行範圍內防止這些暴露造成危險。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p16。


## PDF p17｜第 7–11 條：絕緣、接地及過電流保護

### English OCR

Regulation 7 Insulation,protectionandplacingof conductors

"All conductors in a system which may give rise to danger shall either -

a) be suitably covered with insulating material and as necessary protected so as to prevent, sofar as is reasonably practicable, danger; or

b) have such precautions taken in respect of them (including, where appropriate, their being suitably placed) as will prevent, sofar as is reasonably practicable, danger."

Regulation8

"Precautions shall be taken, either by earthing or by other suitable means, to prevent danger arising when any conductor (other than a circuit conductor) which may reasonably foreseeably become charged as a result of either the useof a system, or afault in a system, becomes so charged; and,for the purposes of ensuring compliance with this regulation, a ( inductor shall be regarded as earthed when it is connected to the general mass of earth by conductors of sufficient strength and current-carrying capability to discharge electrical energy to earth."

Regulation9

"If a circuit conductor is connected to earth or to any other reference point, nothing which might reasonably be expected to give rise to danger by breaking the electrical continuity or introducing high impedanceshall be placed in that conductor unless suitable precautions are taken to prevent that danger."

Regulation 10

"Where necessary to prevent danger, every joint and connection in a system shall be \nechanically and electrically suitablefor use".

Regulation11- Means for protectingfrom excessof current "Efficient means,suitably located, shall be providedfor protectingfrom excess of current every part of a system as may be necessary to prevent danger."

This regulation requires that systems and parts of systems are protected against the effects of short circuits and overloads if these would result in currents which would otherwise result in danger. The means of protection is likely to be in the form of fuses or circuit breakers controlled by relays etc, or it may be provided by some other means capable of interrupting the current or reducing it to a safe value.

Attention should also be given to where the protection is placed within the system.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 7 條：導體的絕緣、保護與配置。系統中可能產生危險的所有導體，必須 a）以適當絕緣材料覆蓋，並視需要加以保護；或 b）採取適當預防措施，包括適當配置位置；兩者目的皆是在合理可行範圍內防止危險。
第 8 條：對於非電路導體，若可合理預見會因系統使用或故障而帶電荷，應以接地或其他適當方法防止危險。為符合本條，導體須藉具有足夠強度與載流能力的導體連線至大地，能將電能洩放入地，才視為已接地。
第 9 條：若電路導體連線大地或其他參考點，除非已採適當預防措施，不得在該導體中設定可能因中斷電氣連續性或引入高阻抗而造成危險的物件。
第 10 條：為防止危險，系統每一處接頭與連線，都必須在機械與電氣方面適合使用。
第 11 條：過電流保護。應在適當位置提供有效措施，保護系統各必要部分免受過電流危險。
若短路或過載電流會造成危險，就必須提供相應保護。常見方式是熔絲、由繼電器控制的斷路器，或其他可中斷電流／將電流降至安全值的方法。也必須注意保護裝置在系統中的設定位置。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p17。


## PDF p18｜第 12 條：切斷電源與隔離

### English OCR

Regulation 12 - Means for cutting off the supply and for isolation

1. "Subject to paragraph (3), where necessary to prevent danger, suitable means (including, where appropriate, methods of identifying circuits) shall be availablefor -

a) cutting off the supply of electrical energy to any electrical equipment; and

b) the isolation of any electrical equipment.

2. In.paragraph (1), "isolation" means the disconnection and separation of the electrical equipmentfrom every source of electrical energy in such a way that this disconnection and separation is secure.

3. Paragraph (1) shall not apply to electrical equipment which is itself a source of electrical energy but, in such a case as is necessary, precautions shall be taken to prevent, so far as is reasonably practicable, danger."

Regulation 12(l)(a)

The objective of this part of the regulation is to ensure that, where necessary to prevent danger, suitable means are available to switch off the electricity supply to any piece of equipment. Switching can be, for example, by direct manual operation or by indirect operation via 'stop' buttons in the control circuits of contactors or circuit breakers.

Regulation 12(l)(b)

This part of the regulation requires that there will be available suitable means of ensuring that the supply will remain switched off and inadvertent reconnection prevented. This is isolation. Thisprovision, in conjunction with safe working practices, will enable work to be carried out on electrical equipment without risk of it becoming live during the course of that work.

DO NOT

OPERATE

EQUIPMENT LOCKEDOUT

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 12 條：
1. 除第 3 款情況外，為防止危險，應具備適當方法（必要時包括電路識別方法），以 a）切斷任何電氣裝置的電能供應；b）隔離任何電氣裝置。
2. 第 1 款的隔離，指將裝置與每一個電能來源斷接並分離，且此斷接和分離必須穩固可靠。
3. 第 1 款不適用於本身就是電源的裝置；但必要時仍須採預防措施，在合理可行範圍內防止危險。
12(1)(a)：確保必要時可切斷任何裝置的電源。可直接以手動方式操作，或透過接觸器／斷路器控制電路的停止按鈕間接操作。
12(1)(b)：要求具備適當方法，確保電源保持關閉，並防止意外重新接通，這才是「隔離」。配合安全工作實務，可避免工作期間裝置意外帶電。
圖示標牌：禁止操作（DO NOT OPERATE）；裝置已上鎖隔離（EQUIPMENT LOCKED OUT）。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p18。


## PDF p19｜隔離與無電裝置工作的預防措施

### English OCR

The two functions of switching off and isolation are not the same,even though in some circumstances they are performed by the same action or by the same equipment.

Regulation12(3)

This part of the regulation recognises the impracticability in some cases of switching off or isolating that equipment which is itself an integral part of a source of electrical energy. The regulation requires precautions to be taken in these circumstances so that danger is prevented so far as is reasonably practicable.

Regulation13 - Precautions for work on equipment made dead "Adequate precautions shall be taken to prevent electrical equipment, which has been made dead in order to prevent danger while work is carried out on or near that equipment,from becoming electrically charged during that work if danger may thereby arise."

c

This regulation may apply during any work, be it electrical or non-electrical.

The precautions must be effective in preventing the electrical equipment from becoming charged in any way which would give rise to danger.

These precautions will prevent the equipment from becoming charged by connection to its own or normal sources of electrical energy but may not, alone, be sufficient to prevent charging. The presence of electrical energy because of electromagnetic induction, mutual capacitance or stored electrical energy may have to be guarded against.

The precautions may need to include means of preventing further accumulation of electrical charge, following initial discharge,because latent energy may be stored in the system.

For work on high-voltage power distribution circuits, isolation procedures should include the application of circuit main earths (primary earths) at points of isolation and additional earthing around the point of work.

Where work is to be done on or near conductors that have been isolated, the conductors must be proved dead at the point of work before the task starts. Where a test instrument or voltage indicator is used for this purpose, this should itself be proved before and immediately after testing the conductor.

I r

Lv

### 繁體中文學習譯文

切斷（switching off）與隔離（isolation）不是相同功能，即使某些情況由同一動作或同一裝置完成。
12(3)：部分裝置本身就是電源的組成部分，可能無法實際切斷或隔離。本款承認此限制，但要求採措施，在合理可行範圍內防止危險。
第 13 條：對已使其無電的裝置工作之預防措施。為避免裝置上或附近工作的危險而使裝置無電後，若工作期間裝置重新帶電荷會產生危險，就必須採取充分措施防止此事。
本條可能適用於任何工作，不限電氣工作。措施必須有效防止裝置以任何方式帶有會造成危險的電荷。
避免裝置重新接至本身或正常電源，未必已足夠；還可能需要防範電磁感應、互電容或儲存電能。首次放電後，系統潛存的能量可能使電荷再度累積，因此也可能需要防止再次充電的措施。
高壓配電迴路工作時，隔離程式應包含在隔離點施加主迴路接地（circuit main earths／primary earths），以及工作點周圍的附加接地。
在已隔離導體上或附近工作，開始前必須在工作點驗明無電。使用的測試儀器或電壓指示器，也必須在測試導體之前及緊接之後確認功能正常。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p19。


## PDF p20｜無電作業程式；第 14、15 條

### English OCR

The safety isolation procedures should be formalised in written instructions or house rules. Safety documentation, including 'permits-to-work', may form part of the written procedures and their use is considered essential to ensuring a safe system of work where this involves work on the conductors or equipment of high-voltage power distribution systems or where the system is very complex.

Properly formulated and regulated 'permit-to-work' procedures focus the minds of those issuing and of those receiving the permits, both on the way the work is to be done and on how the equipment has been made safe.

Before electrical equipment is decommissioned, dismantled, or abandoned for any reason, it must be disconnected from all sources of supply and effective steps taken to ensure that it is dead and cannot inadvertently become re-energised or dangerously charged. It may be necessary to securely mark or otherwise suitably label equipment, circuits, switches etc to( guard against inadvertent re-energisation.

Regulation 14 - Work on or near live conductors "No person shall be engaged in any work activity on or so near any live conductor (other than one suitably covered with insulating material so as to prevent danger) that danger may arise unless -

a) it is unreasonable in all the circumstancesfor it to be dead; and

b) it is reasonable in all the circumstancesfor him to be at work on or near it while it is live;

and

c) suitable precautions (including where necessary the provision of suitable protective equipment) are taken to prevent injury."

The regulation is concerned only with those situations where people are at work on or near live electrical conductors which may foreseeably give rise to danger. Such work is only permitted if conditions (a), (b) and (c) are satisfied.

'Work' is not confined to electrical work but includes any work activity, e.g. electrical testing.

Regulation 15 - Working space, access & lighting

"For the purposes of enabling injury to be prevented, adequate working space, adequate means of access, and adequate lighting shall be provided at all electrical equipment on which or near which work is being done in circumstances which may give rise to danger."

### 繁體中文學習譯文

安全隔離程式應以書面指示或內部規則正式建立。工作許可等安全檔案可構成書面程式的一部分；對高壓配電導體／裝置或非常複雜的系統工作，這些檔案被視為確保安全工作制度不可或缺的措施。
適當制訂並受控的工作許可程式，會讓發出人與接收人同時聚焦於工作如何進行，以及裝置如何達到安全狀態。
裝置除役、拆卸或廢棄前，必須與所有電源斷開，並有效確保其無電、不會意外重新通電或帶有危險電荷。可能須牢固標記或適當標示裝置、迴路及開關，防止誤送電。
第 14 條：在帶電導體上或附近工作。若工作可能因接近帶電導體而產生危險（不包括已以適當絕緣覆蓋而避免危險的導體），除非下列三項同時成立，否則不得工作：
a）綜合所有情況，要求其無電並不合理；且
b）綜合所有情況，讓該人於帶電狀態下在其上或附近工作是合理的；且
c）已採適當措施防止傷害，必要時包括適當防護裝置。
本條針對可合理預見會產生危險的帶電導體作業。只有 a、b、c 全數滿足才允許。Work 不限電氣施工，也包括電氣測試等任何工作活動。
第 15 條：工作空間、通道與照明。在電氣裝置上或附近進行可能有危險的工作時，應提供足夠工作空間、安全通道與照明，以防傷害。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p20。


## PDF p21｜第 16 條：人員能力；第 29 條抗辯

### English OCR

The purpose of the regulation is to ensure that sufficient space,access,and adequate illumination are provided while people are working on, at or near electrical equipment so that they may work safely. The requirement is not restricted to those circumstances where live conductors are exposed but applies where any work is being done in circumstances which may give rise to danger.

Regulation 16 - Persons to be competent to prevent danger and injury "No person shall be engaged in any work activity where technical knowledge or experience is necessary to prevent danger or, where appropriate, injury, unless he possesses such knowledge or experience, or is under such degree of supervision as may be appropriate having regard to the nature of the work."

The object of the regulation is to ensure that people are not placed at risk due to their own ( ck of competence in dealing with electrical equipment, or that of others.

The scope of 'technical knowledge or experience' should include:

a) adequate knowledge of electricity

b) adequate experience of the electrical work being carried out

c) adequate understanding of the system to be worked on and practical experience of that class of system

d) understanding of the hazards which may arise during the work and the precautions which need to be taken

e) the ability to recognise at all times whether it is safe for work to continue.

employees must be trained and instructed to ensure that they understand the safety procedures which are relevant to their work and must work in accordance with any instructions or rules laid down by their employer and directed at ensuring safety.

The regulation recognises that, in many circumstances, people will require some degree of supervision where they do not have sufficient technical knowledge or experience to ensure that they can undertake the work safely.

Regulation 29 - Defence

"In any proceedings for an offence consisting of a contravention of [regulations 4(4), 5, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15 or 16] it shall be a defencefor any person to prove that he took all reasonable steps and exercised all due diligence to avoid the commission of that offence."

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 15 條的目的是讓裝置上、裝置處或附近的作業具有足夠空間、進出條件與照明。不僅適用於帶電導體裸露的情況，也適用於任何可能產生危險的工作。
第 16 條：人員必須具備防止危險與傷害的能力。若工作需要技術知識或經驗以防止危險，或在適用時防止傷害，執行者必須具備該知識／經驗，或依工作性質接受適當程度的監督。
目的在避免因自己或他人處理電氣裝置的能力不足而使人員陷入風險。技術知識與經驗範圍應包括：a）充分電學知識；b）足夠的相關電氣工作經驗；c）瞭解欲操作的系統，且具該類系統的實務經驗；d）理解工作可能出現的危害與所需措施；e）隨時判斷是否仍可安全繼續工作的能力。
員工必須接受訓練及指導，瞭解與其工作相關的安全程式，並依僱主為安全所訂的指示與規則工作。法規承認：當知識／經驗不足以確保安全工作時，人員往往需要某種程度的監督。
第 29 條：抗辯。就違反第 4(4)、5、8、9、10、11、12、13、14、15 或 16 條的犯罪訴訟，若能證明已採取所有合理步驟，並盡應有注意以避免犯罪，可作為抗辯。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p21。


## PDF p22｜其他法規與個人防護裝置

### English OCR

Regulation 29 applies only in criminal proceedings. It provides a defence for a dutyholder who can establish that they took all reasonable steps and exercised all due diligence to avoid committing an offence under regulations 4(4), 5, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15 or 16.

Portable Electrical Apparatus

These are clearly covered by the regulations. Simply, the term applies to anything fitted with a plug top or connector.

All such items are required to be regularly inspected, tested, maintained and appropriate records kept.

I.E.E. (Institution of Electrical Engineers) Wiring Regulations * These are non-statutory and are a code of practice. However, compliance with them is likely to achieve compliance with the Electricity of Work Regulations 1989.

* Apply to systems up to 1000 volts AC only.

Electricity Safety, Quality and Continuity Regulations

The Electricity Safety, Quality and Continuity Regulations 2002 (ESQCR) impose

requirements in respect of the generation, distribution, and supply of electricity, including supply networks and electrical equipment. The Department of Energy and Climate Change (DECC) leads on ESQCR matters, though HSEperforms some functions on DECC's behalf with regards to public safety and incident notifications. Q

Personal Protective Equipment at Work (PPE) Regulations

PPEis equipment that will protect the user against health or safety risks at work. It can include items such as safety helmets, gloves, eye protection, high-visibility clothing, safety footwear and safety harnesses. PPEshould be used as a last resort.

Wherever there are risks to health and safety that cannot be adequately controlled in other ways, PPE must be supplied. PPE must be supplied at no charge to the employee.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

第 29 條僅適用於刑事程式。責任人若能證明已採所有合理步驟並盡應有注意，避免違反第 4(4)、5、8、9、10、11、12、13、14、15 或 16 條，可援用此抗辯。
可攜式電氣器具：明確在規範範圍內。本教材簡單描述為任何裝有插頭或接頭的物品；須定期檢查、測試、維護並儲存適當紀錄。
IEE（電機工程師學會）配線規則：屬非強製法規的實務守則，但遵守通常有助符合《1989 年工作場所電氣法規》。教材寫其僅適用於最高 1000 V AC 的系統。
《2002 年電力安全、品質與連續性規則》（ESQCR）：對發電、配電、供電、供電網路及電氣裝置施加要求。教材指 DECC（能源與氣候變遷部）主責，HSE 代其執行部分公共安全及事故通報職能。
工作個人防護裝置（PPE）規則：PPE 用於保護使用者免受工作健康或安全風險，可包括安全帽、手套、眼部防護、高可視度衣物、安全鞋及安全帶。PPE 應作為最後一道措施。其他方式無法充分控制的健康安全風險存在時，必須提供 PPE，且不得向員工收費。

## PDF p23｜PPE、COSHH 與 RIDDOR

### English OCR

These regulations require that PPEis:

• Properly assessed before use to make sure that it is fit for purpose

• Maintained and stored properly

• Provided with instructions on how to use it safely

• Used correctly by employees.

Control of Substances Hazardous to Health (COSHH) Regulations

COSHH requires employers to control substances that are hazardous to health by following these steps:

Put suitable Enforce and | j Provide

Identify the H Assess the ■ control M maintain information, hazards. H associated risks. ■ measures in ■ control instruction and place. measures. J training.

Employers are also required to:

• Provide monitoring and health surveillance where appropriate

• Plan for emergencies.

..eporting of Injuries, Diseases and Dangerous Occurrences Regulations

(RIDDOR)

RIDDOR requires employers, and other people in control of work premises, to report and keep records of:

• Work-related accidents that cause death

• Work-related accidents that cause certain serious injuries

• Diagnosed cases of certain industrial diseases

• Certain 'dangerous occurrences'.

Reporting allows the enforcing authorities to target their work and provide advice about how to avoid work-related deaths, injuries, ill health, and accidental loss.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

PPE 規則要求：使用前適當評估以確保適用；妥善維護與儲存；提供安全使用說明；由員工正確使用。
《危害健康物質控制法規》（COSHH）要求僱主依下列步驟控制有害健康的物質：辨識危害 → 評估相關風險 → 設定適當控制措施 → 執行並維持控制措施 → 提供資訊、指導與訓練。僱主還必須在適當情況下提供監測與健康監護，並規劃緊急應變。
《傷害、疾病及危險事件通報法規》（RIDDOR）要求僱主與其他工作場所控制人通報並記錄：工作相關死亡事故；造成特定嚴重傷害的工作事故；已診斷的特定職業病；特定危險事件。
通報使執法機關能針對重點工作，並提供預防工作死亡、傷害、疾病及意外損失的建議。

## PDF p24｜電的危害：觸電與火災

### English OCR

Dangers of Electricity

Electric shock

The human body responds in several ways to electrical current flowing through it. The sensation of shock is only one such effect and this can be extremely painful. When a shock is received, the electric current may take multiple paths through the body and its intensity at any one point is difficult or impossible to predict. The passage of electric current may cause muscular contractions, respiratory failure, fibrillation of the heart, cardiac arrest, or injury from internal burns. Any of these can be fatal.

The nature and severity of injury depends upon the magnitude, duration, and path of the current through the body and, in the case of alternating current (ac), on its frequency.

A. Quite low currents, of the order of only a few milliamps (mA), can cause fatal / * \ electric shock.

The effects of electric shock are most acute at around the public electricity supply frequency of 50 Hz.Susceptibility to electric shock is increased if a person is in:

a) good electrical contact with earth, such as in damp or wet conditions or in conducting locations such as inside a metal tank

b) hot environments where people may become damp due to perspiration or humidity, thus reducing the insulation protection offered by clothing.

o

Fires of an Electrical Origin

Fires may be started by electricity in several ways, the main causes are:

a) overheating of cables and electrical equipment due to overloading of conductors

b) leakage currents due to poor or inadequate insulation

c) overheating of flammable materials placed too close to electrical equipment

d) the ignition of flammable materials by arcing or sparking of electrical equipment.

The injuries associated with fire are usually burns but may include other injuries such as smoke inhalation, each with the potential to kill.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

觸電：人體對透過的電流有多種反應，電擊感只是其中之一，而且可能極為疼痛。電流可能在人體內走多條路徑，任一點的強度難以甚至無法預測，可能導致肌肉收縮、呼吸衰竭、心室顫動、心搏停止或體內灼傷；任一項都可能致命。
傷害性質與嚴重程度取決於電流大小、持續時間及透過人體的路徑；交流電還與頻率有關。即使僅數毫安（mA）等級的小電流，也可能造成致命觸電。
約 50 Hz 的公共供電頻率對人體的電擊影響特別顯著。下列情況更易受害：a）與大地導通良好，例如潮濕、積水處或金屬槽內等導電環境；b）炎熱環境下因汗水／濕氣使人潮濕，降低衣物提供的絕緣保護。
電氣起火的主要原因：a）導體過載造成電纜與裝置過熱；b）不良或不足的絕緣造成漏電；c）可燃物靠裝置太近而過熱；d）裝置電弧或火花引燃可燃物。
火災傷害通常是燒傷，也可能包括吸入煙霧等其他傷害，每一種都有致命可能。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p24。


## PDF p25｜電灼傷、電弧與爆炸

### English OCR

Electric burns

Electric burns are most commonly associated with electric shock and often occur in and on the skin layers at the point of contact with the electrical conductors which gave rise to the electric shock. Electrical arcs jump across gaps and can cause burns.

At high frequencies, eg radio frequencies (RF), which include microwaves, it may not even be necessary for contact to be made with live conductors for an electric burn to be received.

Electric burns are usually painful and very slow to heal. Permanent scarring is common.

Arcing

Arcing causes a particular type of burn injury which is distinct from other types. Arcing generates ultraviolet radiation which causes damage like severe sunburn. Molten metal particles from the arc itself can penetrate burn and lodge in the flesh. These effects are additional to any radiated heat damage caused by the arc.

On its own, ultraviolet radiation can cause damage; sensitive skin and eyes are especially vulnerable to arc flash.

Arc flashovers caused during work on live circuit conductors are likely to be particularly hazardous because the worker is likely to be very near to or even enveloped by the arc. Such yes often lead to very serious, sometimes fatal, burn injuries.

Explosion

Electrical explosions include the violent and catastrophic rupture of any electrical equipment.

Switchgear, motors, and power cables are liable to explode if they are subjected to excessive currents, which release violent electromagnetic forces and dissipate heat energy, or if they suffer prolonged internal arcing faults. Explosions caused by ignition from an electrical source include ignition of flammable vapours, gases, liquids and dusts by electrostatic discharge, electric sparks, arcs, or the high surface temperature of electrical equipment.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

電灼傷：通常與觸電同時發生，常出現在與導體接觸處的皮膚表面與皮層。電弧可跨越間隙並造成灼傷。射頻（RF，包括微波）等高頻情況，甚至不必接觸帶電導體也可能灼傷。電灼傷通常疼痛、癒合緩慢，且常留下永久疤痕。
電弧：造成的灼傷有別於其他型別。其紫外線可造成類似嚴重曬傷的損害；熔融金屬微粒可穿入、灼傷並留在皮肉中，這些傷害還會疊加於電弧輻射熱損傷。紫外線本身就會造成傷害，敏感皮膚與眼睛尤其容易受電弧閃光損害。
在帶電電路導體工作時發生的電弧閃絡特別危險，因為人員往往非常靠近電弧，甚至被其包覆；常造成重度乃至致命燒傷。
爆炸：包括電氣裝置猛烈、災難性的破裂。開關裝置、馬達與電力電纜若承受過大電流，釋出強烈電磁力與熱能，或長時間發生內部電弧故障，都可能爆炸。
電源引燃爆炸還包括：靜電放電、火花、電弧或裝置高溫表面，引燃可燃蒸氣、氣體、液體與粉塵。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p25。


## PDF p26｜高壓開關裝置：概述

### English OCR

> 4 «>c» ip1 p )

> yfjjo ~ > - 5oo High-Voltage Switchgear

Introduction

Switchgear is a generic term used to cover all types of equipment specifically designed for the controlling and protection of electrical circuits. It is a link in the chain between supply and load, with the strength of the link being of a known characteristic and having the unquestionable ability to perform its functions when required to do so.

High-Voltage Switches

HVSwitches - are normally used where operational or maintenance isolation is required oij where local isolation is required at an item of equipment, such as a transformer sited at a location remote from its source of supply.

Although oil has been the insulating medium used in HVswitchgear for many years,and is still widely used, switches are now available which useSF6 (sulphur hexafluoride) as the insulating medium.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

開關裝置（Switchgear）是為控制與保護電路而設計之各類裝置的統稱。它是電源與負載之間的一環，此環節的能力特性必須已知，而且在需要時確實能執行其功能。
高壓開關（HV switches）：通常用在操作或維護需要隔離的場合，或需在裝置處就地隔離的情況，例如位於遠離供電來源處的變壓器。
油長年用作高壓開關裝置的絕緣介質，至今仍普遍使用；現在也有以六氟化硫（SF₆）為絕緣介質的開關。

## PDF p27｜高壓開關：功能與額定值

### English OCR

Duties

In serviceSwitches have been designed to perform the following duties:

• make 'normal' or 'load' current (load make).

• carry 'normal' or 'load' current.

• break 'normal' or 'load' current (load break).

• make 'abnormal' or 'fault' current

• carry "abnormal" or "fault" current (for a specific time).

Note: It is important to remember that Switches are not designed to break abnormal or

'fault' currents.

Ratings

S2631 (Section 2) requires oil switches to be rated in terms of the following:

• Number of Poles - Normally 3

• Voltage - BSStandard rating - 3.6kV, 7.2kV,12kV, etc

• Normal Current - 'The RMSvalue of the current that a switch is capable of carrying continually under prescribed conditions'

• Frequency - Normally 50 Hz

• BreakingCurrent - "The current that exists at the instant of contact separation expressed as an RMSvalue"

• Making Capacity - "the making-current that a switch is capable of making and carrying instantaneously at the rated voltage".

"Making-current" is defined as- "The peak value of the maximum current loop (including the de component) in any pole during the first cycle of current when the circuit is closed by a switch".

BSStandard Ratings are:

• 20.1kA (equivalent to 150 MVA @ llkV) and

• 33.4kA (equivalent to 250 MVA @ llkV)

Short Time Current - "The RMSvalue of current that a switch is capable of carrying for the stated time under prescribed conditions".

### 繁體中文學習譯文

使用中的開關設計可執行下列功能：接通正常／負載電流；承載正常／負載電流；切斷正常／負載電流；接通異常／故障電流；在指定時間內承載異常／故障電流。
注意：開關不是用來切斷異常或故障電流的裝置。
教材引用 BS 2631 第 2 節，油開關額定專案包括：
• 極數：通常為 3。
• 電壓：BS 標準額定值例如 3.6 kV、7.2 kV、12 kV。
• 正常電流：在規定條件下可連續承載的電流有效值（RMS）。
• 頻率：通常 50 Hz。
• 遮斷電流：接點剛分離瞬間存在的電流，以 RMS 表示。
• 接通能力：在額定電壓下，開關可瞬時接通並承載的接通電流。
接通電流定義為：開關閉合電路後第一個週期中，任一極最大電流波峰的峰值，包含直流分量。
教材列 BS 標準額定值：20.1 kA（相當於 11 kV 下 150 MVA）、33.4 kA（相當於 11 kV 下 250 MVA）。
短時間電流：在規定條件下，開關可於指定時間承載的電流 RMS。

> 標準編號與額定數字按原教材保留；不可據此替代現場裝置銘牌與製造商額定資料。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p27。

## PDF p29｜獨立手動機構與防反射操作手柄

### English OCR

OperatingMechanism

For Switches which have rated voltage above llkV or have a rated making current more than 33.4 peak kA, it is recommended that the operating mechanism should store energy during the first part of the movement of the operating handle which is released during the latter half of the same operation. This type of operating mechanism is referred to as 'Independent Manual'. Normally this is achieved by charging a set of springs during the first part of the handle movement (without moving the switch contacts) which are released during the latter part of the operation to 'free' the switch contacts to either the open or closed position, i.e., the speed of operation of the switch contacts is independent of the speed of movement of the handle.

Whilst the duties of an oil switch referred to above include making and carrying a fault current, they are not designed to break a fault current, therefore any attempt to break a ( juIt current may cause a catastrophic failure of the switch and serious injury to the operator. To overcome this possibility a time delay is built into the operating mechanism of modern switches to prevent an operator from switching to the 'off' position immediately after switching to the 'on' position and vice versa. Therefore, if an operator should inadvertently close onto a fault, the time delay in the operating mechanism will allow the fault current to operate the up-stream protection equipment before the operator is able to operate the switch again. To achieve this for older switchgear, such as the Reyrolle 'J' series the manufacturers have supplied "Break-Back" or "Anti Reflex" handles which allow the switch to be operated in one direction. To operate in the opposite direction the handle has to be removed from the operating mechanism, a lever has to be moved and the handle has to be refitted.

Note: For the safety of the operator, it is important that any non "Break-Back" or "Anti

Reflex" handles still in use are destroyed immediately and replaced with the "Break-Back" or "Anti Reflex" type.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

對額定電壓高於 11 kV 或額定接通電流大於 33.4 kA 峰值的開關，教材建議採用在手柄前半行程儲能、後半行程釋能的機構，稱獨立手動（Independent manual）。通常是先使彈簧儲能而不移動接點，再釋放彈簧，令接點迅速到全開或全閉位置；接點速度與手柄移動速度無關。
油開關雖可接通及承載故障電流，但不能切斷故障電流。嘗試切斷可能使裝置災難性損壞並重傷操作者。現代開關機構因此設延時，防止剛合上就立即切開，或反向立即操作。若誤合於故障，延時可讓上游保護先切除故障，再容許操作開關。
舊式 Reyrolle J 系列等，製造商提供 Break-Back／Anti Reflex 手柄，每次只允許單一方向操作。要反向，須先取下手柄、移動一個撥桿，再重新裝上。
教材注意事項：為操作者安全，仍在使用的非 Break-Back／Anti Reflex 手柄應立即銷毀，並換成相應安全型式。

> 最後一段是教材對特定舊式裝置的指示之翻譯；實際器具更換須依適用製造商與現場管理程式。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p29。


## PDF p30｜開關接地與測試探棒

### English OCR

Earthing

Switches are fitted with earthing facilities to enable the cable side to be earthed. The arrangement is integral to the switch and the earthing switch has the same rated making capacity and rated short-time current as the main contacts of the switch. It is not required to have a rated breaking-current or a rated normal-current.

TestProbes

IABLE

SWITCH

To allow access to the cable for testing almost all HV Switches are equipped either with facilities for the insertion of test probes or fixed test points behind a test access cover.

o

In all cases the switch operating mechanism is interlocked with the test facilities so that access can only be gained when the switch is in the earth position, earthing the cable.

OFF

JABl E

SWITCH

OFF

JABL.E

SWITCH

Once the test connections have been made the interlocks on the switch will allow the earth to be removed for testing but will not allow the switch to be switched to the ON position.

o

When test connections are made either by test probes or fixed test points it is possible to carry out the full range of cable tests, Continuity, Insulation, HV PressureTest, dead phasing etc, but as the cable has to be earthed before any test connections can be made it is not possible to use test probes to prove that the cable is dead

Under no circumstances should the cable be energised from the remote end whilst test probes are inserted or the cover over any fixed test points is open

2 x ck i-* * >72 -> 1 x M T L.r

### 繁體中文學習譯文

接地：開關設有內建接地裝置，可將電纜側接地。接地開關的額定接通能力及短時間電流與主接點相同，但不要求有額定遮斷電流或額定正常電流。
測試探棒：幾乎所有高壓開關都設有插入測試探棒的設施，或位於測試蓋後的固定測試點，以便測試電纜。
機構與測試設施具有聯鎖，只有開關在接地位置、電纜已接地時，才可接近測試設施。建立測試連線後，聯鎖允許移除接地以進行測試，但不允許把開關操作至 ON。
透過探棒或固定測試點，可做導通、絕緣、高壓耐壓及無電對相等電纜測試；但因接入前電纜必須先接地，不能用這些測試探棒作為初始驗明電纜無電的方法。
警告：插有測試探棒，或固定測試點的蓋板開啟時，任何情況下都不得由遠端向電纜送電。

## PDF p31｜開關熔絲組合與熔絲開關

### English OCR

Switch Fuses or Fuse Switches

These items of switchgear are a combination of a switch and an HRCfuse. The term switch fuse indicates that the fuse is fixed, and the term fused switch indicates that the fuse forms part of the moving contact arrangement. The switch fuse is the more common type of device. The switch provides the facilities described previously and the addition of the HRCfuse provides the device with a fault break capacity. All fault currents are broken initially by the HRCfuse and as the fault may be present on only one phase,the fuses are provided with striker pins which eject with the operation of the fuse. The pin ejecting causes a tripping bar to open the switch and disconnect all 3 phases from the source of the supply.

r

Making Capacity - 'The making-current that a switch is capable of X making and carrying instantaneously at the rated voltage'.

X, 'Making-current' is defined as - 'The peak value of the maximum current loop (including the de component) in any pole during the

” MVA).

Short Time Current - 'The RMSvalue of current that a switch is capable of carrying for the stated time under prescribed conditions.

The rated short time current of a switch shall be not less than its rated making current divided by 2.55 (i.e.., the RMSof the maximum current loop excluding the de component).

5.4fcyl Rated short time current = = 2.1/c/l
2.55

The duration of the rated short time current shall be three seconds.

A remote trip coil may be fitted to the Switch Fuse or fuse Switch for load shedding purposes.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

這類裝置由開關加高遮斷容量熔絲（HRC fuse）組成。Switch fuse 指熔絲固定；fuse switch／fused switch 指熔絲屬於可動接點機構的一部分。較常見的是 switch fuse。開關提供前述功能，HRC 熔絲則增加故障電流切斷能力。
故障電流先由 HRC 熔絲切斷。因故障可能只在單一相，熔絲設有動作時彈出的撞針（striker pin）；撞針推動跳脫桿，使開關開啟並將三相都與電源斷開。
接通能力：在額定電壓下可瞬間接通並承載的接通電流；接通電流是閉合後第一週期、任一極最大電流波峰的峰值，含直流分量。
教材列額定電壓 12 kV 的標準額定值為 5.4 kA（相當於 102 MVA）；此數字保留原文，請看掃描核對。
短時間電流：規定條件下在指定時間可承載的 RMS。不得低於額定接通電流／2.55；書中算式 5.4 kA／2.55 = 2.1 kA，持續 3 秒。
可在這類裝置上裝遠端跳脫線圈，用於解除安裝負載。

> 已核對原始掃描：5.4 kA、102 MVA 及 5.4／2.55 = 2.1 kA 均為原件數值。可能有原文排印疑點，未默改，須向講師核對。

## PDF p33｜環網單元（RMU）

### English OCR

Ring Main Units

A combination of two switches feeding a switch fuse or fuse switch, via a short busbar, is generally described as a "ring main unit" (RMU). The unit is usually non-extensible, although some manufacturers do provide extension facilities. RMU's are used extensively in HV networks where open-ring working is applied. This is a system of ring feeder cables feeding, from both ends to several outdoor RMU's, one of which has its ring feeder switch open and is nominated as the normally open point. As a consequence, a single fault will cause only one of the feeder circuit breakers to trip and half the ring will remain energised.

RMU's normally provides isolation and earthing facilities to the tee off circuit or transformer, and for the ring feeder cables. In addition, there are normally testing facilities for the ring feeder cables and, in some cases, these are also available for the tee off circuit or transformer.

PROBE)

SWITCH SWITCH

Most modern ring main units use SF6 as the insulating medium with a circuit breaker feeding the transformer and providing the required protection.

The system could be further enhanced by using earth fault passage indicators which aid rapid location of the faulty section of the ring. This indicator is basically a core balance CT fitted over the ring feeder cable or inside the cable end box and feeding a drop flag relay or indicator lamp unit.

• BOWDEN BROS LTD

EEF1 Model RR

■m.w ■ i!«■—<■•»■ —■■•<I I

,»ACN TTaje?XneMI

The RMU is normally an outdoor unit, much less costly than circuit breakers, and will generally only be suitable when the building complex is made up of several individual buildings which in themselves do not require a multi-panel intake switchboard. The HV switch fuse or fused switch does not normally, therefore find application within a large single building fed direct from a Distribution Network Operator.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

兩個開關透過短匯流排供電給 switch fuse 或 fuse switch 的組合，通常稱為環網單元（Ring main unit，RMU）。多數不可擴充，部分製造商提供擴充設施。
RMU 廣泛用於採開環運轉的高壓網路：環網電纜由兩端供應數個戶外 RMU，其中一處環網饋線開關保持開啟，稱正常開路點（normally open point）。因此單一故障只使其中一端饋線斷路器跳脫，另一半環網仍帶電。
RMU 通常提供支線迴路／變壓器及環網饋線電纜的隔離與接地功能，也通常提供環網電纜測試設施；部分裝置亦提供支線或變壓器的測試設施。
多數現代 RMU 以 SF₆ 為絕緣介質，由斷路器供電並保護變壓器。系統可加裝接地故障透過指示器，以快速定位故障區段。其基本構造為套在環網電纜上或電纜端箱內的零序／平衡式 CT，連線掉旗繼電器或指示燈。
RMU 通常為戶外裝置，成本遠低於斷路器，適合由數個個別建築構成、各建築不需多盤進線配電盤的建築群。因此，由配電網營運商直接供電的大型單一建築，通常不採此類高壓開關熔絲配置。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p33。


## PDF p34｜RMU 額定值與裝置例子

### English OCR

VT's and CT's, until recently, could not be accommodated within the switch fuse and fused switch but a number of manufacturer's have now developed measurement VT's and CT's capable of being fitted into the switch fuse unit and this will allow local metering.

Ratings

TheSwitch:

a) complies generally with BS 2631 (or BS5463)

b) normal current 500A or 630A

c) breaking current 500A

d) making capacity 33.4kA peak at llkV

e) short-time current 13.1kA for 3 secs (250MVA at llkV).

o

The FuseSwitch: maximum normal current is dictated by the maximum fuse rating which can be fitted with all the other ratings as for the oil switch.

Typical examples of oil insulated RingMain Units are as follows:

a) South WalesSwitchgear "Tiger" (non-extensible)

b) Long and Crawford T3GF3 (non-extensible, but obtainable with 2 switch fuses).

c) Reyrolle ROKSS(extensible and non-extensible versions).

d) Lucy FRMU (extensible and non-extensible versions).

Typical examples of SF6insulated RingMain Units are as follows:

a) Hawker Siddeley Falcon (Alpha or Beta)

b) LucySCRMU

c) Merlin Gerin (Schneider) RN2-Tor RN2-C(extensible and non-extensible versions).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

過去 switch fuse／fuse switch 不易容納 VT 與 CT；教材指出部分製造商已開發可裝入開關熔絲單元的量測 VT／CT，讓裝置可作就地計量。
開關額定值：a）一般符合 BS 2631（或 BS 5463）；b）正常電流 500 A 或 630 A；c）遮斷電流 500 A；d）11 kV 時接通能力 33.4 kA 峰值；e）短時間電流 13.1 kA、3 秒（11 kV 時 250 MVA）。
熔絲開關：最大正常電流由可安裝熔絲的最大額定值決定，其他額定值如油開關。
油絕緣 RMU 例：South Wales Switchgear Tiger（不可擴充）；Long and Crawford T3GF3（不可擴充，但有兩組 switch fuse 型式）；Reyrolle ROKSS（有可擴充及不可擴充型）；Lucy FRMU（兩種型式皆有）。
SF₆ 絕緣 RMU 例：Hawker Siddeley Falcon（Alpha／Beta）；Lucy SCRMU；Merlin Gerin（Schneider）RN2-T／RN2-C（有可擴充及不可擴充型）。

> 裝置型號、標準與可供應情況為教材原有例子，並非目前產品供應調查。

## PDF p35｜室內高壓開關裝置與斷路器

### English OCR

Indoor High-Voltage Switchgear

Indoor switchgear is widely used where complex protection, metering and power operation is required. The most common type of switchgear found indoors is the HVCircuit Breaker where it performs the dual functions of electrical isolation (a) for maintenance or operational reasons and (b) the protection of equipment by automatic disconnection.

When electrical isolation for maintenance or operational reasons only is required the cheaper indoor HVswitch may be used.

Instrument

ENCLOSURE

Fig6 HV circuitbreaker

The traditional HVCircuit Breaker (fig 6) comes in two parts the fixed portion (enclosure) is where the permanent HVconnections are made it also houses the auxiliary parts such as the T's,the VT,meters and relays.

The moving portion (truck) houses the main load breaking contacts and the associated operating mechanism, being removable this facilitates the maintenance of the active parts of the Circuit Breaker.

Definitions

A Circuit Breaker is defined as a switching device capable of making, carrying, and breaking currents under normal circuit conditions and making, carrying for a specified time, and breaking currents under specified abnormal conditions such as those of a short circuit.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

需要複雜保護、計量及動力操作時，常用室內開關裝置。最常見的是高壓斷路器，兼具 a）為維護或運轉而進行電氣隔離；b）以自動切斷保護裝置兩項功能。若只需維護／運轉隔離，可採較便宜的室內高壓開關。
圖 6，高壓斷路器：傳統型式分兩部分。固定部（外殼）具有永久高壓連線，也容納 CT、VT、儀表及繼電器等輔助裝置。可動部（臺車）容納主負載遮斷接點及操作機構，可移出，便於維護活動部件。
斷路器定義：可在正常電路條件下接通、承載與切斷電流，並可在短路等指定異常條件下接通、承載指定時間及切斷電流的開關裝置。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p35。


## PDF p36｜斷路器銘牌與工作電壓

### English OCR

The circuit breaker is the most common item of HV switchgear used within a building complex and although much more expensive than the switch fuse it has the major advantage of total versatility in that many types of protection device can be used in conjunction with it. It can also be easily controlled from remote positions. The circuit breaker can provide integral isolation, circuit and busbar earthing and a means of access for test purposes. Circuit access is also provided for metering and protection CT's and VT's

Ratings

BS.5311

RATED VOLTAGE 12Kv

RATED IMPULSE VOLTAGE 75Kv

RATED FREQ. 50-60 Hz

RATED NORMAL CURRENT 630A

RATED S.C. BREAKING CURRENT 12.5Ka

SHORT TIME CURRENT 12.5KS 3S

OPENING DEVICE 50 VDC

CLOSING DEVICE 50 VDC

MOTOR 110 VAC

MASS 205kg

RATED GAS PRESS. (20°C) bar

Fig7 Rating Plate complying with BS5311

Service Voltage

"This is the upper limit of the highest voltage of systems for which the Circuit Breaker is intended".

BSStandard ratings - 3.6kV, 7.2kV, 12kV

It is advisable to buy CBs with a voltage rating suitable for the highest system voltage normally used, i.e., llkV, this permits standardisation, and the switchgear can be used in systems where the voltage is below 11 kV.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

斷路器是建築群最常用的高壓開關裝置。雖比 switch fuse 昂貴許多，但可搭配多種保護裝置，用途彈性高，也容易遠端控制。可提供內建隔離、迴路／匯流排接地、測試存取，以及供量測與保護 CT／VT 連線的位置。
圖 7，符合 BS 5311 的銘牌例：N.E.I. REYROLLE–ENGLAND；序號 1989 YMV 453；料號 1321A1131；型式 YMV2/X21/QMRO；額定電壓 12 kV；額定衝擊電壓 75 kV；頻率 50–60 Hz；正常電流 630 A；額定短路遮斷電流 12.5 kA；短時間電流 12.5 kA、3 s；開路裝置 50 VDC；合閘裝置 50 VDC；馬達 110 VAC；重量 205 kg；20°C 額定氣壓欄以 bar 為單位，未填值。
工作電壓（Service voltage）：本斷路器預定使用之系統最高電壓的上限。教材列 BS 標準額定值 3.6、7.2、12 kV。
教材建議選購適合通常最高系統電壓（例 11 kV）的斷路器，以利標準化，也可用於低於 11 kV 的系統。

## PDF p37｜絕緣等級與短路遮斷電流

### English OCR

InsulationLevel

This is the maximum voltage that the switchgear can withstand for a short time under fault conditions e.g., lightning strikes.

For switchgear having a service voltage rating of 12kV the BSvalues are:

• Lightning impulse withstand voltage - 60 or 75kV(peak)

• Impulse voltage withstand levels of 95kVare not uncommon with the more modern items of vacuum and SF6circuit breakers.

• One-minute power frequency withstand voltage - 28kV(rms).

Frequency

Normally quoted as 50/60Hz. which allows the equipment to be sold worldwide?

c

Normal Current

"The rms value of the current which the Circuit Breaker shall be able to carry continuously without deterioration under prescribed conditions".

BSStandard ratings 400A, 500A, 630A, 800A, 1250A

Short Circuit BreakingCurrent

"The current that can be broken at a stated voltage".

Breaking capacity will determine the maximum fault current that can be broken by the CB. x"r he fault current that flows in a distribution system under fault conditions is made up of two components:

a) a symmetrical ac component - determined by the power available and network impedance.

b) a de component - short (4-5 cycle) transient due to the discharge of stored magnetic energy into the fault. 2z £• x f

Of these components, (a) can be calculated, but (b) is unpredictable (as it depends on several factors such as the instantaneous voltage at time of fault).

For this reason, when determining the BreakingCapacity of a CBthe current considered is the RatedShort Circuit Current.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

絕緣等級：開關裝置在雷擊等條件下短時間可承受的最高電壓。對工作電壓額定 12 kV 的裝置，教材列 BS 值：雷電衝擊耐壓 60 或 75 kV 峰值；較新真空與 SF₆ 斷路器採 95 kV 衝擊耐壓亦不罕見；1 分鐘工頻耐壓 28 kV RMS。
頻率：通常標示 50／60 Hz，以適用不同地區。
正常電流：規定條件下，斷路器可連續承載且不劣化的電流 RMS；標準額定例 400、500、630、800、1250 A。
短路遮斷電流：在指定電壓下可切斷的電流。遮斷能力決定斷路器可切除的最大故障電流。
配電系統故障電流包含：a）對稱交流分量，由可用電力與網路阻抗決定；b）直流分量，教材描述為儲存磁能流入故障所產生的短暫（4–5 週期）暫態。
交流分量可計算，直流分量受故障發生瞬間電壓等多種因素影響。教材因此以額定短路電流作為決定遮斷能力時考量的電流。

## PDF p38｜短路額定值：kA 與 MVA

### English OCR

Rated Short Circuit Current

"The rms value of the ac component of the rated short circuit breaking current".

Since 1952 British Standard 116 and 162 have detailed the basis for the design of circuit breakers and their associated enclosures. These publications were superseded in 1975 and

1976 by BS5227 and 5311 respectively and have now been superseded by BS EN 62771. These standards align very closely with the existing IEC specifications. The significant changes from the user's point of view are way the switchgear is rated.

In this earlier BS a slightly different terminology was used e.g., Rated Short Circuit Current was known as Symmetrical Breaking Current and the standard ratings were: 3.196kA,

5.25kA, 7.88kA, 13.1kA.

For Practical convenience, the rated symmetrical breaking current was multiplied by servic voltage and the phase factor (V3 in a 3ph system) and quoted as Symmetrical Breaking Capacity in MVA.

e.g., if symmetrical breaking current is 13.1kA and service voltage is llkV, then symmetrical

breaking capacity - 13.1 x 103 x 11 x 103 x V3 = 250 MVA at llKv

Standard ratings at (llkV) - 75, 150*, 250*, 350, 500* and 750 MVA (* preferred values)

Note: A CB with a symmetrical breaking capacity of 250 MVA at llkV has a symmetrical

breaking current rating of 13.1kA. If such a CB is used at 6.6kV, its symmetrical breaking current rating remains 13.1kA, but its symmetrical breaking capacity becomes, 6.6 x 103 x

13.1 x 103 x V3 = 150 MVA at 6.6kV

To these fault ratings are coupled normal current carrying ratings of 400, 630, 800, 1200 or

2000 Amps and an impulse voltage (1.2/50 Sec wave), withstand level of 75kV. Specification BS EN 62271 species short circuit currents for a range of voltages:

12.5kAatl2kV 20.0kA at 7.2kV 16.0kA at 12kV 12.5kA at 3.6kV

### 繁體中文學習譯文

額定短路電流：額定短路遮斷電流之交流分量的 RMS。
教材回顧自 1952 年起 BS 116／162 的設計基礎，後由 1975／1976 年的 BS 5227／5311 取代，再提到 BS EN 系列。第一段 OCR 為 62771，頁末為 62271，兩處不一致；不把前者自行當成已確認正確編號。標準與 IEC 規格接近，對使用者的重要變更是額定方式。
舊稱「對稱遮斷電流」，額定例為 3.196、5.25、7.88、13.1 kA。為實務方便，以該電流 × 工作電壓 × 三相係數 √3，表示為 MVA 對稱遮斷容量。
例：13.1 × 10³ × 11 × 10³ × √3 ≈ 250 MVA（11 kV）。教材列 11 kV 標準容量 75、150*、250*、350、500*、750 MVA，* 為優選值。
同一斷路器在 11 kV 時 250 MVA，對應 13.1 kA；改用於 6.6 kV，電流額定仍為 13.1 kA，但容量變為 6.6 × 10³ × 13.1 × 10³ × √3 ≈ 150 MVA。
這些故障額定搭配正常載流額定 400、630、800、1200 或 2000 A，以及 75 kV 衝擊耐壓。原文波形標示 1.2／50，單位 OCR 不可靠，請看原圖。
頁末 BS EN 62271 所列例：12 kV 下 12.5／16.0 kA；7.2 kV 下 20.0 kA；3.6 kV 下 12.5 kA。

> 標準編號 62771／62271 及衝擊波形時間單位有疑義，保留原文與掃描供核對。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p38。


## PDF p39｜輔助開關

### English OCR

Auxiliary Switches

—

■i? r Tr » Wi 1 4?/

■’ *wR

"Switches working in conjunction with a circuit-breaker to control a circuit for operating auxiliary devices..."

These are low voltage (e.g. 50V de) low current (e.g. 5A) switches operated by the same mechanism as the main circuit breaker. Most circuit breakers in use are fitted with 4 or 8 auxiliary switches, typically described as 2+2

or 4+4.

2 + 2 would indicate that there are 2 auxiliary switches which are open when the circuit breaker is open and 2 auxiliary switches which are closed when the circuit breaker is open.

Typical usages of these auxiliary switches are the control of trip circuits, remote indication of circuit breaker position i.e. ON or OFF and interlocking.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

輔助開關是與斷路器配合、控制輔助裝置操作迴路的開關。通常為低電壓（例如 50 V DC）、低電流（例如 5 A），由與主斷路器相同的機構驅動。
多數斷路器裝有 4 或 8 個輔助開關，常寫為 2+2 或 4+4。
2+2 表示：斷路器開路時，有兩個輔助開關處於開路，另兩個處於閉路。
典型用途包括跳脫迴路控制、遠端顯示斷路器 ON／OFF 位置，以及聯鎖。

Earthing

Most Circuit Breakers are provided with facilities to enable either the busbars or the feeder circuit to be connected to earth for the purpose of complying with the Electricity at Work Regulations. The method of earthing will be one of the following:

Non withdrawable/fixed pattern circuit breakers are becoming much more common due to the cost and space savings associated with them.

These circuit breakers are effectively integrally earthed by switching to an earth position, but this facility is normally only available for earthing the feeder or circuit.

Where busbar earthing is required, this is normally achieved using a separate busbar earthing switch in the switchboard.

## PDF p42｜四種合閘機構

### English OCR

Closing Mechanisms

Closing mechanisms are divided into four types, the classification being determined by the method of achieving the power required to complete the closing operation.

Dependent Manual or Hand Closing(H)

This is a closing operation by hand, without using any other supply or storage of energy, and the speed of which is therefore entirely dependent on the operator. This type of mechanism is no longer manufactured as, if incorrectly operated, they could lead to a disruptive failure and explosion within the circuit breaker.

Independent Manual Closing(IM)

This is closing by hand in a single operation in which energy supplied during the initial part of the operation is used to close the Circuit Breaker at a speed which is independent of the — operator. The energy is usually that of compressed springs.

Spring Assisted (S)

This is a form of closing by hand where springs are compressed by one operation and their energy is released by a separate later operation to close the contacts.

The state of the spring is described as 'charged' when it is in a condition to close the circuit and 'free' when not in that condition.

Power Closing(P)

Used where remote or automatic closing of a circuit breaker is required.

Remote closing (e.g., from a Control Room) is generally achieved using a solenoid closing mechanism. Solenoid power being obtained from substation batteries or voltage transformers.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

合閘機構依完成合閘所需能量的取得方式，分四類。
依賴手動／手動合閘（H）：完全靠手操作，不使用其他能源或儲能，所以速度完全取決於操作者。教材指此類已停止製造，因操作錯誤可能導致斷路器內破壞性故障與爆炸。
獨立手動合閘（IM）：單次手動操作前段提供能量，後段以不受操作者速度影響的速度合閘，通常以壓縮彈簧儲能。
彈簧輔助（S）：先以一個動作壓縮彈簧，再以另一個、較後的動作釋能使接點閉合。彈簧可供合閘時稱 charged（已儲能），否則稱 free（未儲能／釋放狀態）。
動力合閘（P）：用於遠端或自動合閘。從控制室等處遠端合閘，通常使用電磁線圈機構，由變電站電池或電壓互感器供電。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p42。
