### English OCR

Interlocks

Withdrawable Circuit Breakers are fitted with Interlocks which prevent them being withdrawn or "Racked Out" whilst the Circuit Breaker is in the ON position.

This Interlock is to prevent the slow-moving isolating connections which have no rated making or breaking capacity being used to make or break the load current.

ISOLATING

MECHANISM

FREE ►

LOCKED ►

Shutters on Busbar and Circuit Spouts

. ne enclosures or fixed portions of withdrawable Circuit Breakers are fitted with Shutters which automatically close over the High-voltage Busbar and Circuit connections or Spouts as the Circuit Breaker is Racked Out.

1

W- _______

These Shutters are fitted with facilities for locking them closed using a padlock and they should be locked closed at all times when the circuit breaker has been Racked Out to prevent accidental contact with live parts unless access is required for testing purposes.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

可抽出式斷路器設有聯鎖，防止在 ON 位置將其抽離或退出（rack out）。
這是為了避免移動緩慢、沒有額定接通或遮斷能力的隔離接點，被拿來接通／切斷負載電流。圖示標籤：隔離機構，FREE／LOCKED（可動／鎖住）。
匯流排與迴路插接觸口的防護活門：可抽出式斷路器的固定外殼內設活門；斷路器退出時，活門會自動蓋住高壓匯流排與迴路插接觸口（spouts）。
活門可用掛鎖鎖閉。除測試需要接近外，斷路器退出後應一直鎖閉，防止誤觸帶電部分。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p43。


## PDF p44｜輔助變壓器：VT 與 CT

### English OCR

Auxiliary Transformers

Circuit Breakers, depending on position and usage, are fitted with meters, instruments, protective relays etc. These are supplied via voltage and/or current transformers.

Voltage Transformers

Voltage transformers are normally supplied with a secondary voltage of 110V, with the primary voltage to suit the system voltage. Normally oil immersed, star wound HVand LV,both windings being fused, the LV winding being earthed at the yellow phase.Thus, the neutral is fused, and the yellow phase "linked".

Where solenoid or motor wound spring power closing of a CBis required (e.g., standby sets), the power is normally derived from an additional power closing secondary winding (although sub-station batteries are sometimes used). The voltage required for power operation varies from 110 to 220Vbut is commonly 165V on existing switchgear. Modern motor wound spring mechanism use110 or 220V ac/dc universal motors.

Current Transformer

Current transformers with a secondary rating of 5A (or 1A) and primary rating in the range 3 0A, 50A, 75A, 100A, 200A,400A are common.

Thesecondarywinding should never be open circuited when the primary is energised, as this will result in the generation of HV at the secondary terminals leading to insulation breakdown. If it is required to disconnect the secondary circuit whilst the primary is energised, the secondary winding should first be short circuited.

Measuring CTs are used to supply ammeters, wattmeter's etc. and are accurate to approx 120%of rating and saturate at approx 125%of rating, to protect the meter movements.

Protective CTs are used to supply protective relays etc. and are accurate to approx 10 x rating at their rated burden.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

依位置及用途，斷路器可配電表、儀器及保護繼電器，由電壓互感器（VT）及／或電流互感器（CT）供應。
VT：通常二次電壓 110 V，一次額定配合系統電壓。教材描述傳統油浸式、高低壓皆星形繞組，兩繞組均配熔絲，低壓繞組在黃相接地，因此中性線設熔絲、黃相用連片。這是原教材特定配置描述。
電磁或馬達儲能合閘（例如備用發電機組）通常由額外的合閘電源二次繞組供電，有時使用站用電池。動力操作電壓約 110–220 V，既有裝置常見 165 V；現代馬達儲能機構採 110 或 220 V AC／DC 通用馬達。
CT：常見二次額定 5 A 或 1 A，一次額定 30、50、75、100、200、400 A。一次帶電時，二次繞組絕不可開路，否則二次端子產生高電壓並可能使絕緣擊穿。若一次帶電而需斷開二次迴路，必須先短接二次。
量測 CT 供電流表、功率表等，教材例述約在額定 120% 內準確、125% 左右飽和，以保護儀表機構。保護 CT 供保護繼電器，在額定負載（burden）下約至額定 10 倍仍保持準確。

> VT 接地、熔絲配置及 CT 準確／飽和值是教材描述，實際需依銘牌、等級和製造商檔案核對。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p44。


## PDF p45｜電弧控制

### English OCR

ARC Control

On making or breaking via a Circuit Breaker an arc will inevitably be drawn between the fixed and moving contacts. The size and duration of the arc is determined largely (for a given current) by the medium within which the switching occurs, by the arc control device fitted and the operating method.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

斷路器接通或切斷時，固定與可動接點之間不可避免會產生電弧。
對一定電流而言，電弧大小與持續時間主要取決於切換所在的介質、所裝電弧控制裝置及操作方式。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p45。


## PDF p46｜油斷路器與橫向噴流滅弧室

### English OCR

Types of Circuit Breakers

Circuit breakers are categorised around their moving contact insulation, and arc interrupting medium; the accepted HV mediums being oil, usually bulk oil at 3.3, 6.6, 11 and 33kV (3.6, 7.2, 12 and 36kV), vacuum, SF6 and air.

Oil Circuit Breakers (OCB)

This was the most popular and least expensive HVcircuit breaker to buy; metal-clad using light mineral oil as in insulant and arc quenching media and utilising some form of arc control device, the oil circuit breaker has catered for all the circuit interrupting requirements of power engineering for many years at distribution voltages. However, today it must be conceded that the oil circuit breaker has virtually reached the limits of its potential development to meet the demands of circuit interruption and in comparison, with some of its more modern alternatives is much more expensive in maintenance cost.

On the later types of OCB's, with ratings of 250 MVA at llkV, the method of arc control is by use of a Cross Jet ExplosionPot. The cross-jet explosion pot is a device made up of insulating material to form a chamber surrounded by a strong shell so that the contacts are completely surrounded (SeeFig11). When the contacts part the arc drawn causes the oil to break down and form a gas.The gas causes a ) pressure to build up inside the chamber and as the moving contact reveals the side vents the gas and arc products are forced out through these vents, displacing and lengthening the arc. At each current zero the arc is extinguished, and fresh oil enters the chamber and at some point, in this process the insulation value between the fixed and moving contacts will be sufficiently high to prevent a re-strike. An OCBhas a defined operating sequence which is: O-T-CO-T-CO

O = Operates
T = Time
C = Close

(Trips to OFF due to fault) (three minutes)

(Switch to ON)

OCB's are only able to break fault current three times before the concentration of Carbon in the oil becomes too high for future safe operation.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

斷路器依可動接點的絕緣及滅弧介質分類。高壓常見介質為油、真空、SF₆ 及空氣；油型通常用於 3.3、6.6、11、33 kV 系統（裝置額定 3.6、7.2、12、36 kV）。
油斷路器（OCB）曾是最普遍、購置最便宜的高壓斷路器。金屬封閉式裝置以輕質礦物油絕緣及滅弧，搭配滅弧裝置，長期滿足配電電壓等級的需求。教材指出其技術發展已接近極限，相較較新型式，維護成本高。
較後期、11 kV／250 MVA OCB 採橫向噴流滅弧室（Cross jet explosion pot）：絕緣材料形成室體，外有堅固外殼，包圍接點。
接點分離時電弧分解油產氣，室內壓力上升；可動接點露出側排氣口時，氣體及電弧產物噴出，使電弧偏移、拉長。每逢電流過零，電弧熄滅且新油進入；當接點間絕緣足以阻止重燃時，完成開斷。
教材列操作序列 O–T–CO–T–CO：O 為故障跳至 OFF；T 為時間（3 分鐘）；C 為合至 ON。並敘述 OCB 切除故障電流 3 次後，油中碳濃度會過高而不宜再安全操作。

> 三次故障切除及三分鐘間隔按本教材保留，不能當作所有油斷路器的通用操作／維護限制。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p46。


## PDF p47｜油斷路器滅弧、油位與氣體

### English OCR

FIXED

CONTACT

OIL UNDER

PRESSURE

FROM ARC

IS FORCED

THROUGH

PASSAGE

ARC

SPLITTERS

MOVING

CONTACT

OIL CIRCUIT BREAKER CROSS JET EXPLOSION POT

REAR OIL

PASSAGE

Fig.ll Oil Circuit Breaker CrossJetExplosionPot

The earlier types of OCB's having a breaking capacity up to 150 MVA at llkV were of the Plain Break type. In this type the quenching of the arc is achieved by contact separation and the head of oil in the tank. The head of oil above the contacts affects the gas bubble formation and must be sufficient to ensure ample pressure at the arc and to prevent a chimney of gas from the arc to the oil surface. A small volume of air above the oil will result in a higher pressure for a given volume of gas and consequently a reduction in arcing time. It is therefore most important that the recommended oil level be maintained.

Kegardless of the arc control method used, gas will be generated when breaking current using an oil circuit breaker. The gas generated will be a mixture of hydrocarbon gases,the majority of which will be acetylene, with all the fire and explosion risks associated with this. As acetylene is heavier than air adequate ventilation at low level must be installed to clear the potentially explosive vapour.

When gas is generated, carbon is left in the oil which reduces its electrical strength, this means that it is essential that insulating oil is tested regularly in normal service and it should be replaced either after it is found unsatisfactory or after the circuit breaker has broken fault current.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

圖 11：油斷路器橫向噴流滅弧室。標籤：固定接點；電弧加壓的油被迫透過流道；分弧片；可動接點；後方油道。
早期最高約 11 kV／150 MVA 的 OCB 為簡單開斷（plain break）型，靠接點分離及油箱中的油柱滅弧。接點上方油深影響氣泡形成，必須足夠，以在電弧處維持壓力並避免氣體形成通向油麵的煙囪通道。油上方空氣體積較小，對一定產氣量會產生較高壓力，縮短燃弧時間，因此維持建議油位很重要。
不論採哪種滅弧方式，OCB 開斷電流都會產氣。教材稱產生混合碳氫氣體，大部分為乙炔，伴隨火災及爆炸風險；接著以「乙炔比空氣重」為理由，要求低處通風排除潛在爆炸蒸氣。此敘述按來源翻譯，另見疑義提示。
產氣時會有碳留在油內，降低電氣強度，因此正常使用中須定期測試絕緣油；若檢測不合格，或斷路器已切除故障電流，教材要求更換。

> 原文『acetylene is heavier than air』及產氣組成敘述有技術疑義，譯文保留來源說法；不可直接作為氣體或通風設計依據，請向講師核對。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p47。


## PDF p48｜真空斷路器（VCB）

### English OCR

Vacuum Circuit Breakers (VCB)

Whilst the potential advantages of alternating current interruption in a vacuum have been recognised since the end of the 19th Century the manufacture of vacuum circuit breakers was not economically viable until the mid 20th Century. The first vacuum circuit breakers, installed on the British electricity system in 1965, have given excellent service without any unforeseen maintenance being required, and all the claims for performance made by the manufacturers have been proven to be conservative.

The operation of the vacuum circuit breaker is based on the excellent dielectric strength of a vacuum when it reaches ' values of 10-5 to 10-6 Torr (1 Torr - 1mm mercury). At such low pressures the breakdown strength increases because the mean free path length of the residual gas molecules increases beyond the contact spacing, and consequently no ionising collisions of the molecules occur. At a pressure of 10-4 Torr the mean free path length between residual gas molecules is of the order of 1 metre.

The arc which originates in such a device inevitably generates metallic vapours from the surface of the contacts, and the only applicable means of de-ionisation consists of; electromagnetically making these vapours travel outwards towards regions far from the arc. The vapours condense on the surrounding shields, which help to prevent the deposition of the vapour products on the inside wall of the insulating body where they could reduce the internal insulation level.

The arrangement of a typical vacuum interrupter is shown in Fig 12. It consists of a pair of butt contacts enclosed within a sealed ceramic or glass body with stainless steel end plates. The moving contact is sealed into the enclosure via a stainless-steel bellows which permits axial movement of the contact.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

交流電流在真空中開斷的優點於 19 世紀末已被認識，但直到 20 世紀中期才具製造經濟性。教材敘述英國電力系統 1965 年首批 VCB 服務良好，無需非預期維護，製造商對效能的宣稱反而偏保守。
VCB 依賴 10⁻⁵ 至 10⁻⁶ Torr 真空下的良好介電強度（1 Torr = 1 mm 汞柱）。如此低壓時，殘餘氣體分子的平均自由程超過接點間距，不再發生使分子電離的碰撞，因此擊穿強度提升。10⁻⁴ Torr 時平均自由程約 1 m。
電弧仍會使接點表面產生金屬蒸氣。除遊離的方法是以電磁作用使蒸氣向外移至遠離電弧的區域，並凝結在周圍遮蔽上；遮蔽也避免蒸氣沉積於絕緣外殼內壁而降低內部絕緣。
圖 12 的典型真空滅弧室由一對對接接點構成，封在陶瓷或玻璃體內，兩端為不鏽鋼端板；可動接點透過不鏽鋼波紋管密封，允許軸向移動。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p48。


## PDF p49｜真空滅弧室：隔離與測試限制

### English OCR

Gla««-ceramlc body Moving contact

Shield for bellows

Fixed-contact stem

Moving-contact stem

End shield

Fig. 12 Vacuum Circuit Breaker

The breaking capacity of VCB's and their normal ratings are as for OCB's manufactured to BS

EN 62271

As the contacts of a Vacuum circuit breaker are typically only between 8 and 12mm apart when the circuit breaker is in the off position it should not be used for isolation as it is not electrically secure if the vacuum seal fails and air enters the vacuum interrupter.

Non withdrawable/fixed pattern Vacuum circuit breakers are normally fitted with a disconnector in series with the main contacts to provide a means of safe isolation.

As there is nothing on the vacuum interrupter to indicate the state of the vacuum it should be tested regularly to ensure that it is serviceable.

The testing is done by applying a high DC voltage across the open contacts of the vacuum interrupter and measuring the leakage current. This test will generate X rays and precautions need to be taken to minimise the risks associated with these emissions. Guidance on the test voltage and the precautions to be taken should be sought from the switchgear manufacturer. The test will also leave the vacuum interrupter with an electrical charge which will need to be safely discharged before the test connections are removed.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

圖 12，真空斷路器。標籤包括玻璃／陶瓷本體、可動接點、波紋管遮蔽、固定接點桿、可動接點桿及端部遮蔽。
VCB 遮斷能力與一般額定值，與按 BS EN 62271 製造的 OCB 相同。
VCB 在 OFF 時，接點通常僅相距 8–12 mm；若真空密封失效而進氣，此間距不能提供可靠電氣隔離，因此不能單靠它作隔離。固定式／不可抽出 VCB 通常在主接點串接隔離開關，以提供安全隔離。
真空滅弧室沒有直接顯示真空狀態的指示，應定期測試確認適用。教材描述以高直流電壓施加於分開的接點間，量測漏電流。此測試會產生 X 射線，須採措施降低風險；測試電壓及防護應向製造商確認。
測試亦會使滅弧室帶電荷，移除測試接線前須安全放電。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p49。


## PDF p50｜SF₆ 斷路器與滅弧方式

### English OCR

SF6 Circuit Breaker

This is another option at distribution voltage and offers similar benefits to the vacuum breaker.

Sulphur hexafluoride (SF6)is an inert, non-toxic, non-flammable, colourless, odourless gas, which does not exist in a natural state and, therefore has to be synthesised. It is a very heavy gas, approximately five times heavier than air.

Not only does sulphur hexafluoride possess a high dielectric strength to prevent arcs from occurring, but the dissociated molecule, caused by the arcing process,rapidly recombines after the source of arcing is removed. It should be noted however that after SF6 has been dissociated by the energy of an arc a small proportion of the breakdown products produce stable compounds some of which are toxic and therefore care should be taken if equipme?... leaks.

SF6 is, therefore, very suitable for quenching electric arcs. It is, in fact, of the order of 100 times more effective than air.

Sulphur hexafluoride was first produced in Paris in the year 1900 and initial research concerning industrial applications was performed by GECin 1937. In 1960 the first SF6 circuit breakers and switches were launched simultaneously in Europe and the USA. It was logical that initially SF6 would find its way into EHVcircuit breakers where the cost of expensive ancillary equipment could be supported.

o

In distribution switchgear terms, the development of interrupting devices appears to be following two paths:

(i) the use of SF6in gas blast devices, and (ii) the use of SF6in magnetic spinning arc devices.

In the first case,generally referred to as a "puffer type", the gas is driven past the arc in order to cool and de-ionise it; in the second case,referred to as a "spinning arc type", the arc is driven through the gas to achieve the same effect.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

SF₆ 斷路器是配電電壓等級的另一選擇，優點與 VCB 相近。
六氟化硫（SF₆）為惰性、無毒、不燃、無色、無味的氣體。教材稱其自然狀態不存在，須人工合成；重量約為空氣的 5 倍。
SF₆ 介電強度高，可抑制電弧；被電弧分解的分子，在電弧能量來源移除後會迅速重新結合。但少量分解產物形成穩定化合物，其中有些有毒，因此裝置漏氣時須小心。
教材稱其滅弧效力約為空氣的 100 倍。1900 年首次在巴黎製得，GEC 於 1937 年研究工業用途，1960 年歐洲及美國同時推出 SF₆ 斷路器／開關；早期用於可負擔昂貴輔助裝置的超高壓斷路器。
配電開斷裝置的兩條發展路線：1）氣吹／壓氣式（puffer）：使氣體流過電弧以冷卻及去遊離；2）磁力旋弧式（spinning arc）：使電弧在氣體中旋轉，達到相同效果。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p50。

## PDF p52｜SF₆ 環網單元

### English OCR

Sulphur Hexafluoride (SF6) Ring Main Units

Following the successful development of rotating arc SF6 circuit breakers as an economic and environmentally more acceptable alternative to oil, some manufacturers began to consider its use in other items of high-voltage switchgear. Consequently in 1980 Brush Switchgear introduced the world's first SF6 ring main unit, the Brush Falcon. It consisted of two faults make, load break switches and a rotating arc circuit breaker for the tee off circuit all enclosed in a tank of SF6 at a pressure of 4 bar gauge.

The advantages of SF6 over oil include virtual elimination of fire risk, considerable reduction in maintenance and no adverse effects to varying environmental conditions. The use of a circuit breaker for the tee off circuit instead of the traditional HRC fuse also gives a number of advantages, particularly when protecting distribution transformers.

The circuit breaker allows better protection of the transformer over the full range of fault current values, permits protection of transformers above 1000KVA and eliminates the onerous task of opening up the RMU to the elements to replace fuses before restoring supplies.

Not surprisingly, therefore, SF6 RMUs became popular with Regional Electricity Companies and other operating bodies. Most major manufacturers have now developed SF6 RMU's

Examples of SF6 ring main units now include:

a) Brush Falcon, the original of which was non-extensible.

b) Brush Falcon Beta, an upgraded version which is also extensible.

c) Merlin Gerin SF6 Ringmaster, available in both extensible and non-extensible forms.

d) Long and Crawford LCG SF6 series which are available in both extensible and non-extensible forms.

e) Lucy SCRMU which has a Vacuum circuit Breaker and associated disconnector inside the SF6 tank for the tee off to the transformer.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材敘述：旋弧式 SF₆ 斷路器作為油型裝置的經濟替代品成功後，製造商開始把 SF₆ 用於其他高壓裝置。原文稱其在環境上較能接受，此為當時教材說法。
1980 年 Brush Switchgear 推出 Brush Falcon SF₆ RMU，由兩個可接通故障電流、切斷負載電流的開關，以及一個支線旋弧斷路器組成，全部封在表壓 4 bar 的 SF₆ 槽中。
教材列相對於油的優點：幾乎消除火災風險、大幅減少維護，且對環境條件變化不敏感。支線改用斷路器取代傳統 HRC 熔絲，能在更完整的故障電流範圍保護變壓器、保護大於 1000 kVA 的變壓器，並免去為更換熔絲而在天候暴露下開啟 RMU 的繁重工作。
因此 SF₆ RMU 在區域電力公司及其他營運機構廣受採用，多數主要製造商均有產品。教材例：a）原 Brush Falcon，不可擴充；b）改良 Brush Falcon Beta，可擴充；c）Merlin Gerin SF₆ Ringmaster，兩種型式皆有；d）Long and Crawford LCG SF₆，兩種型式皆有；e）Lucy SCRMU，在 SF₆ 槽內使用 VCB 及其隔離開關供應變壓器支線。

> 『環境上較能接受』是原教材的歷史比較說法，並非本閱讀器對 SF₆ 現行環境影響或政策的評估。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p52。


## PDF p53｜SF₆ RMU 保護與電纜測試接點

### English OCR

Not all the manufacturers have gone as far as using circuit breakers for the tee off circuit. The Long and Crawford LCG range have two switches and a switch fuse inside a sealed SF6 chamber but use air insulated fuses for transformer protection.

Most units, however, now use circuit breakers with the protection being operated via CTs and time limit fuses or IDMT Relays for larger transformers. Recent development and changes in Pressure System Regulations has also seen a reduction in gas pressure to slightly above atmospheric pressure.

As it is clearly not possible to insert test probes in to the pressurised SF6 tank for cable testing purposes connections have had to be made using a removeable earth connection.

This is interlocked with the main switch mechanism in such a manner that access can only ? gained when the switch is in the earth position. TO TRANSFORMER

CTs

EARTH

ON

EARTH

RING OR RADIAL FEED

A similar arrangement is used with fixed pattern/non withdrawable SF6 and Vacuum circuit breakers to allow the outgoing cables to be tested.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

並非所有製造商都在支線使用斷路器。Long and Crawford LCG 系列的密封 SF₆ 室內有兩個開關與一組 switch fuse，但使用空氣絕緣熔絲保護變壓器。
多數單元使用斷路器，由 CT 搭配時限熔絲（TLF）啟動保護；大型變壓器則可用 IDMT 繼電器。教材稱後續發展與壓力系統法規變更，使氣壓降低至略高於大氣壓。
因不能把測試探棒插入加壓 SF₆ 槽，電纜測試須透過可拆除的接地連線接入。該部位與主開關機構聯鎖，僅在開關處於接地位置時才可接近。
示意圖示籤：至變壓器、CT、EARTH（接地）、ON（合上）、環網或放射式饋線。固定式 SF₆／真空斷路器也採相似安排，以測試出線電纜。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p53。


## PDF p54｜空氣斷路器與隔離開關

### English OCR

Air Circuit Breaker

This is not a common HV circuit breaker but was, for many years, the only alternative to oil.

The arc is carried in a mixture of nitrogen, oxygen and metallic vapours. Interruption is largely due to elongation of the arc which results in cooling and de-ionisation. Owing to the high temperature of the arc relative to the surrounding air, the arc is subjected to strong convection currents which, coupled with the electromagnetic effect of the current loop, cause the arc to rise vertically. It is then driven into the arc chute where splitter plates assist the cooling and lengthening process so that its length is much greater than the direct distance between the fully open contacts.

Many variations around the above theme will be found.

The air circuit breaker offers similar benefits, in terms of minimum fire risk, as the vacuum and SF6 breaker but is larger physically and has higher capital and maintenance costs for HV applications.

Disconnector

o

A disconnector is a switching device with no current making or breaking capability but with adequate contact separation to provide safe isolation.

Almost the only time disconnectors are used as single items of HV switchgear is on overhead line distribution systems.

The most common current use of disconnectors is as a means of isolation in non-withdrawable/fixed pattern vacuum switchgear.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

空氣斷路器在高壓用途不常見，但多年來曾是油型唯一替代方案。
電弧位於氮、氧及金屬蒸氣的混合物中，主要藉拉長電弧使其冷卻、去遊離。電弧相對周圍空氣極熱，強對流加上電流迴路的電磁作用使其向上移動，進入滅弧柵；分弧片促進冷卻與拉長，令電弧長度遠大於全開接點間的直線距離。此原理有多種變形。
空氣斷路器在低火災風險方面與真空／SF₆ 相近，但高壓用裝置體積更大、初期與維護成本更高。
隔離開關（Disconnector）：沒有電流接通或遮斷能力，但接點分離距離足以提供安全隔離。作為獨立高壓裝置，幾乎只見於架空配電系統；目前更常用作固定式／不可抽出真空開關裝置的隔離手段。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p54。

## PDF p56｜電力變壓器原理

### English OCR

Power Transformers

Introduction

ATransformer is an item of electrical apparatus which consists of two or more electrical circuits interlinked by a common magnetic circuit for the purpose of transferring electrical energy between them.

In an electricity supply network transformer are used to step up (increase) and step down (decrease) voltages, so as to make them more appropriate for Transmission,Distribution and Utilisation.

Transformers used for Power and Distribution applications are constructed in accordance with British Standard 171:1987 Power Transformers.

Principle of Operation

In a single-phase transformer two coils called the primary and secondary, are wound so that they are in close proximity to each other but are not in electrical contact. Current flowing in the primary produces an associated flux I, which will be largely confined to the high permeability material on which the coils are wound. Therefore, most of the flux through the primary will pass through the secondary coil as shown in fig 1.

If the primary winding is connected to an a.c. source, an alternating flux will set up in the ( magnetic circuit or core. This flux, by linking with both windings, produces in each an alternating e.m.f. El and E2 (see fig 1).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

變壓器由兩個以上電氣迴路構成，以共同磁路互相耦合，將電能在迴路間傳遞。
供電網路中，變壓器用於升壓與降壓，使電壓適合輸電、配電與使用。教材引用電力／配電變壓器依 BS 171:1987 製造。
單相變壓器的一次與二次線圈彼此靠近，但沒有直接電氣接觸。一次電流產生磁通，大多侷限於線圈所繞的高導磁率材料內，所以透過一次的多數磁通亦透過二次。
一次接交流源後，在鐵心磁路建立交變磁通，同時鏈結兩繞組，在各自產生交變電動勢 E1、E2。圖 1 標籤：電源、一次、二次；磁通與繞組配置見原圖。

## PDF p57｜匝數比與三相磁路

### English OCR

As the flux is approximately the same throughout the magnetic circuit, the relationship between input and output voltages of a transformer is given by:

El _ N1

E2~N2

Where:

• N1 = Number of Primary turns

• N2 = Number of Secondary turns

Thus,the output voltage is given by:

A N2

V E2 = — X El

If the secondary is left on open-circuit, no current will flow in the secondary and E2 will be the secondary open-circuit (or no-load) voltage. This is the voltage indicated on the transformer rating plate. The secondary voltage on load (V2) will be lower, due to the voltage drop across the impedance of the windings.

Magnetic Circuit

### 繁體中文學習譯文

磁路各處磁通近似相同，因此輸入／輸出電壓關係為 E1／E2 = N1／N2。N1 是一次匝數；N2 是二次匝數。故 E2 = (N2／N1) × E1。
二次開路時沒有二次電流，E2 為二次開路／無負載電壓，也就是銘牌標示的電壓。帶載二次電壓 V2 因繞組阻抗壓降而較低。
磁路：多數配電電力變壓器為三相，一次與二次繞在同一鐵心柱以降低漏磁。圖 2 為常見構造：磁軛（yoke）、紅相、黃相、藍相及三柱鐵心。

## PDF p58｜鐵心、繞組構造與接線

### English OCR

Construction

The core of a modern transformer is generally constructed of a number of laminated sheets of Cold Rolled Grain Orientated Steel, built into a rectangular frame. The windings are arranged concentrically with each other on cylinders around the side members known as the legs or limbs of the core (see fig 2).

Apart from electrical and thermal considerations the windings must have great mechanical strength.

In general, there is only one LV winding per limb, which in the past consisted of a single layer. Thishas now been superseded by a two-layer winding which has the following advantages: o

1. Improved cooling (There are four winding surfaces).

2. Greater mechanical strength as the windings have less tendency to unwind.

A single multi-layer cylindrical HV winding would present difficulties with insulation due to the potential differences which could occur between layers. This is usually overcome by connecting a number of cylindrical windings in series.

To improve flux linkage and reduce electrical stresses,the HV winding is usually wound over the top of the LV winding. This also has the advantage of reducing transformer size.

o

Winding Connections

The interconnection of the phase windings to produce a three or four wire supply is achieved by three alternative types of connection:

1. Star (see fig 3)

2. Delta (see fig 4)

3. Interconnected Star or zigzag(see fig 5)

### 繁體中文學習譯文

現代變壓器鐵心一般以冷軋方向性鋼片疊製成矩形框架，繞組同心排列於鐵心柱（legs／limbs）周圍的圓筒上。除電氣與熱條件外，繞組還須具高度機械強度。
每柱通常只有一個低壓繞組；過去為單層，後來由雙層取代，優點是有四個繞組表面可加強冷卻，且較不易鬆散，機械強度較高。
單一多層圓筒高壓繞組可能因層間電位差而難以絕緣，通常以數個圓筒繞組串聯解決。為改善磁通鏈結並降低電氣應力，高壓繞組通常繞在低壓繞組外，也可縮小體積。
三相繞組互連形成三線或四線供電，可採：1）星形；2）三角形；3）互連星形／曲折形（zigzag）。

## PDF p60｜繞組與端子標示

### English OCR

Terminal Markings

The markings for windings and terminals are laid down in BS171 and consist of the following:

Letters are assigned to phase windings. The same letter is used for all windings on one limb of the core. The higher voltage winding is described by a higher case letter, and the lower voltage winding of the same phase is described by the corresponding lower-case letter. A third winding is described by a higher case letter appropriate to its phase, preceded by the number 3. Additional windings are marked in like manner, using higher numbers in sequence.

1.

2.

3.

For a single-phase transformer, the letters used are as follows:

1. A - For the higher voltage winding

2. a - For the lower voltage winding

3. 3A - For a third winding.

For a three-phase transformer the letters used are as follows:

1. ABC - Higher voltage windings

2. a b c - Lower voltage windings

3. 3A 3B 3C - Third windings.

Subscript numbers are used to distinguish between winding ends and tapping's brought ou from the winding. An example of this is where voltage tapping is provided (see fig 6).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材引用 BS 171 的標示方式：
1. 每相繞組分配一個字母，同一鐵心柱上的所有繞組用同一字母。
2. 高電壓繞組用大寫；同相低電壓繞組用對應小寫。
3. 第三繞組以相別大寫字母前加 3；更多繞組依序加較大的數字。
單相：A 高壓、a 低壓、3A 第三繞組。
三相：A B C 高壓；a b c 低壓；3A 3B 3C 第三繞組。
下標數字用來區分繞組兩端及引出的分接頭。圖 6 為附分接頭的三相高壓繞組，各相端子 1–8；圖內 OCR 把部分 8 辨為 B，請看掃描。

## PDF p61｜接線組別與時鐘相位表示法

### English — OCR / scan transcription

Windings and Phase Displacement Designations

In a normal two winding transformer each winding could be STAR, DELTA or ZIG-ZAG connected. BS171:1978 designations for such winding connections depend upon whether they are High or Low Voltage windings. These designations are shown in Table 1. If a neutral connection is available in the terminating box, then this is represented by a 'N'.

Table 1 - Winding Connection Designations (BS171:1978)

Winding Connection Designation

Higher Voltage

Delta D

Star Y

Zig-Zag Z

Neutral Available N

Lower Voltage

Delta d

Star y

Zig-Zag z

Neutral Available n

The British Standard also gives a designation for the Phase displacement between different combinations of High and Low voltage windings (see Table 2).

BS171:1978 states that:- "The phase displacement is expressed as the hour figure shown on a clock whose large (minute) hand is pointing at 12 and coincides with the vector of the voltage between the neutral point (real or imaginary) and the high-voltage line terminal, and hose small (hour) hand coincides with the vector of the voltage between the neutral point (real or imaginary) and the corresponding low-voltage or intermediate-voltage terminal".

The complete designation thus gives details of the method of connection of both High and Low Voltage windings and the relative phase displacement between the two voltages. For example, consider the designation Dy11, which can be represented by the drawing in fig 7. Thus: HV winding is connected in Delta LV winding is connected in Star Phase shift is 330E lagging.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

一般雙繞組變壓器，每側可採星形、三角形或 Zigzag。教材引用 BS 171:1978，符號依高壓／低壓而不同；若端子箱引出中性點，另標 N／n。
表 1：高壓三角形 D、星形 Y、Zigzag Z、中性點 N；低壓分別為 d、y、z、n。原 OCR 低壓星形為大寫 Y，掃描確認是小寫 y。
相位移以鐘面小時數表示：分針固定指向 12，代表實際或假想中性點至高壓線端子的電壓相量；時針代表中性點至相應低壓或中壓端子的電壓相量。
完整接線代號因此表示高、低壓的接線方式及相對相位移。例 Dy11：高壓三角形、低壓星形；原文寫「Phase shift is 330E lagging」，意指落後 330 度，但度數符號在原掃描也印為 E。

Power transformers used in general have one of the following winding configurations:

Delta/Star (Dyll or Dynll) - Typically used for stepping down from high distribution voltages to the lower utilization voltages.

## PDF p64｜冷卻四字碼與分接調壓

### English OCR

The identification code used consists of four letters giving details of the cooling medium and circulation method for both primary and secondary cooling systems (see Table 4).

Table 4 -Transformer Cooling Code

1st Letter 2nd Letter 3rd Letter 4th Letter

Medium Circulation Medium Circulation

Cooling Medium in contact with windings Cooling Medium in contact with external cooling systems

For example, an oil-immersed transformer with natural oil and air circulation is designed ONAN, if however, the oil was pumped, and the air forced the designation would be OFAF.

Because of concern over the possible health hazards arising from the use of Askarels (These are fire resistant synthetic insulating liquids), Electrical Power Transformers containing Askarel fluids are no longer to be installed in electrical distribution systems. Existing Askare filled transformers may remain in service until it becomes necessary to replace them for other reasons.

Tap Changing

The secondary no-load voltage is given by:

N2

F2 = wi x£1
o

The secondary on-load voltage (V2) will be lower due to voltage drop in the transformer windings. The voltage at the intake terminals at the point of utilization will be lower still due to voltage drop in the distribution cables. Circumstances may arise where owing to excessive voltage drop and/or low supply voltage, the voltage at the intake terminals at the point of utilization is outside the statutory limits (i.e., + 6% of the declared voltage).

One solution to this problem is tap-changing, i.e., varying the number of turns on the HV windings (N1 in the expression on page2). It can be seen from the expression that a Reduction in N1 will lead to an Increase in E2.

Almost every transformer is fitted with tap-changing facilities, quite often with an external switch in order to obviate removing transformer top to carry out tap-changing.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

冷卻程式碼由四個字母組成：第 1、2 字母表示接觸繞組的冷卻介質與迴圈方式；第 3、4 字母表示外部冷卻系統的介質與迴圈方式。
例如油浸式、油與空氣皆自然迴圈為 ONAN；油由泵浦迴圈且強制送風則為 OFAF。
教材提及 Askarel 耐燃合成絕緣液的健康疑慮，表示不得再於配電系統安裝含此液的電力變壓器，而既有者可使用至因其他原因須更換。此為原教材敘述。
分接調壓（Tap changing）：二次無負載電壓 E2 = (N2／N1) × E1。帶載 V2 因繞組壓降較低；使用點進線電壓又因電纜壓降更低。壓降過大及／或供電過低，可能使使用點電壓超出法定界限，教材在此括註宣告電壓的 +6%〔原掃描如此印示，未改為 ±6%〕。
解法之一是改變高壓繞組匝數 N1；由公式可見，減少 N1 會提高 E2。幾乎所有變壓器都有分接調整設施，常用外部開關，以免為調整而開啟頂蓋。

> Askarel 使用與電壓容許偏差為教材原述，未核實為現行規定；不可用作現場合規判定。
> 已核對原掃描，本頁電壓偏差符號印為 +6%。

## PDF p65｜分接開關與阻抗電壓

### English — OCR / scan transcription

A common form of tap-changing switch consists of a moveable shoe which makes contact between two contacts at a time. Common tapping points are 105%, 102.5%, 100% 97.5% and 95% of the HV turns.

The general policy for oil-immersed power transformers is to specify OFF-CIRCUITtap-changing switches, thus tap-changing must be carried out with the transformer isolated. A typical plate showing switch positions and corresponding HV turns is shown in Table 5. Assuming the switch is in position 3 and the secondary voltage has to be increased by 2.5%. Then it will be necessary to reduce the HV turns, to say 97.5% by changing switch position 4.

Table 5 - Tapping Switch Label

HV Turns % | Switch Position

105 | 1

102.5 | 2

100 | 3

97.5 | 4

95 | 5

Impedance Voltage

Impedance voltage is defined in BS171:1978 as "The voltage required to be applied at rated frequency to the line terminals of a winding of a polyphase transformer,..., to cause the rated current to flow through these terminals when the terminals of the other winding are short circuited...". This is shown diagrammatically in fig 8.

a.c. Supply Short Circuit

Fig.8 Short Circuit Test

### 繁體中文學習譯文

常見分接開關使用可移動觸靴，一次跨接兩個接點；常用高壓匝數分接點為 105%、102.5%、100%、97.5%、95%。
油浸式電力變壓器一般指定無激磁分接開關（OFF-CIRCUIT），因此必須先隔離變壓器才能調整。表 5 對應：位置 1→105%；2→102.5%；3→100%；4→97.5%；5→95%。
教材例：原在位置 3，若要提高二次電壓約 2.5%，就將高壓匝數降至約 97.5%，切至位置 4。
阻抗電壓：教材引用 BS 171:1978，指在另一繞組端子短路時，於額定頻率向多相變壓器某繞組線端施加、足以使額定電流流過的電壓。
圖 8 短路測試：一側交流電源及電壓表／電流表，另一側短路。

## PDF p66｜百分比阻抗與故障電流

### English OCR

It is usually expressed in percentage terms and is often referred to as the percentage impedance (%Z). The voltage found as a result of a short-circuit test, such as that above, is expressed as a percentage of the rated voltage, e.g., for an 11/0.433 kV transformer, if V was measured as 529 volts, then Impedance Voltage - 529V.

Example: For an llkV/433V transformer, if V was measured as 529V during the short circuit test, then Impedance Voltage is 529V. Therefore, Percentage Impedance is given by:

529
%z = 11000 x 100 = 4
'81%

The percentage impedance of a transformer gives a guide to the probable current which would flow under fault conditions, if, in service a short-circuit occurred, the full transformer primary voltage would be applied instead of the (approx.) 5% applied in the short-circuit test. This would mean that instead of full-load current flowing in the primary (and secondary) winding a current of:

100

x Rated Full Load Current would flow

There are other, more rigorous, methods of calculating fault currents, but the above method is sufficiently accurate for the majority of applications.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

阻抗電壓通常用百分比表示，稱百分比阻抗（%Z）。短路試驗量得電壓除以額定電壓，再乘 100。
例：11 kV／433 V 變壓器，短路試驗施加 529 V 即流過額定電流：%Z = 529／11000 × 100 = 4.81%。
%Z 可估算故障電流。短路試驗只施加約 5% 額定電壓；實際短路時若施加完整一次電壓，電流估計為 (100／Z%) × 額定滿載電流。書中提出「20 倍法則？」作思考。
教材稱另有更嚴謹方法，但此近似對多數應用足夠。
表 6 典型值：3.3／0.433 kV、100–1000 kVA：4.63%–5%；6.6／0.433 或 11／0.433 kV、25–50 kVA：4.5%；同電壓、199–1000 kVA：4.6%–5%。

> 已對照 PDF p66；表中 199–1000 確為原件印字，未擅改成 100。故障電流式為教材簡化模型。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p66。


## PDF p67｜變壓器並聯：條件與原式疑點

### English — OCR / scan transcription

Parallel Operation

The following factors should be considered if two transformers are to be operated in parallel.
1. Their voltage ratios must be the same.
2. Their vector groups must be the same.
3. Their phase rotations must be the same.
4. Their percentage impedance voltages should be approximately equal for good load sharing.

When operating transformers of unequal size and/or percentage impedance in parallel, the current taken by each transformer can be calculated by:

[Transcription of the printed expressions, with apparent errors retained:]
IA = [QA / (IL × ZA)] / [(QA / ZA) + (QB / ZB)]
IB = [QB / (IL × ZB)] / [(QA / ZA) × (QB / ZB)]

Where: IL = Total load current
IA = Transformer A current
IB = Transformer B current
QA = Transformer A kVA rating
QB = Transformer B kVA rating
ZA = Transformer A % impedance
ZB = Transformer B % impedance

[Do not use these printed expressions for calculation; see the Chinese page note and the existing formula crosscheck.]

### 繁體中文學習譯文

兩臺變壓器並聯時，教材列須考量：1）電壓比相同；2）向量組別相同；3）相序相同；4）百分比阻抗電壓宜大致相等，以獲良好負載分配。
對容量及／或百分比阻抗不同的並聯變壓器，原書接著給出電流分配公式，但掃描可見排版／公式錯誤，不宜照式計算。
原件公式轉錄（保留疑點，非推薦演算法）：
IA = [QA／(IL × ZA)]／[(QA／ZA) + (QB／ZB)]
IB = [QB／(IL × ZB)]／[(QA／ZA) × (QB／ZB)]
符號：IL 總負載電流；IA、IB 分別為 A、B 電流；QA、QB 分別為額定 kVA；ZA、ZB 分別為百分比阻抗。
請搭配每日任務所列 18_Transformer_Formula_Crosscheck.md 的推導與假設閱讀，不把以上原式當成正確計算式。

> 已核對原始掃描 PDF p67；A、B 分子及 B 分母的問題不是單純 OCR 錯誤。正確演算法與適用前提另見既有公式核對筆記。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p67。


## PDF p68｜變壓器與開關裝置的絕緣油

### English OCR

Oil Maintenance for Transformers and Switchgear

Introduction

The insulating oils used in modern electrical equipment are mineral oils specially selected and refined from crude petroleum. The desired properties of insulating oil are as follows:

• High dielectric strength

• Low viscosity

• Non-sludging

• High specific heat

• High flash point

• Small evaporation

• Chemically neutral

Although the insulating oil used in transformers and oil filled switchgear is the same,its function is different and therefore the two uses will be discussed separately.

Transformers

When used in transformers the functions of the oil are three-fold:

1. To act as a coolant

2. To insulate electrically

3. To protect windings from atmospheric effects

o

Oil is one of the most efficient mediums for absorbing heat from the core, windings, and conductors of a power transformer. It transmits the heat, sometimes aided by forced circulation, to the naturally or artificially cooled outside surfaces of the equipment.

The need to withstand system voltage transients due to lightning and switching surges, and the fact that transformers are often operated for short periods at well above their rated capacities, requires the oil to have particularly good insulating qualities. The tendency towards more compact designs and narrow spacings in modern transformer design has increased the importance of this requirement.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

現代電氣裝置絕緣油為特選並精製的礦物油。理想性質：高介電強度、低黏度、不易產泥、高比熱、高閃火點、低揮發性及化學中性。
變壓器與油式開關裝置雖用相同絕緣油，但功能不同，以下分述。
變壓器油的三項功能：冷卻；電氣絕緣；保護繞組免受大氣影響。
油能有效吸收鐵心、繞組及導體的熱，經自然或強制迴圈，送至裝置自然或人工冷卻的外表面。
變壓器須承受雷擊與開關突波，有時短暫超額定運轉，因此油須有良好絕緣品質；現代設計更緊湊、間距更小，更凸顯此要求。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p68。


## PDF p69｜油劣化、外觀與乾燥呼吸器

### English OCR

Insulating oils in service are subject to deterioration due to the conditions of use. The transformer "breathes" causingmoisture and impurities in the atmosphere to be absorbed by the oil. This can cause oxidation, subsequent sludge formation and failure of the dielectric strength.

The temperature of the oil has considerable effect on its satisfactory life. This varies from the "ambient" to the "hot spot" (the point of maximum internal temperature). Overheating will cause an increased rate of oxidation and risk of fire and explosion.

Maintenance

Transformers should be thoroughly inspected every 12 months and attention given to all

_ems where necessary.

Paint Work

The transformer tank and fittings should be carefully examined to see if rust is forming, especially at the welded seams. If so, it should be completely removed with a wire brush or other means and the metal given a coat of red oxide paint followed by an appropriate undercoat and finally the finishing coat.

DehydratingBreather

Examine the colour of the SilicaGel Charge and if the pink showing has reached halfway up the container, it is recommended that the charge be changed for a fully active one.

"Reactivation: It is not recommended that breathers should be reactivated by heating them to dry out the silica gel as at temperatures above 170°Fthe silica gel crystals can be irreversibly damaged.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

使用中的絕緣油會隨運作條件劣化。變壓器呼吸時，油吸收大氣濕氣與雜質，可導致氧化、油泥及介電強度失效。
油溫由環境溫度至內部熱點溫度不等，會大幅影響壽命。過熱會加速氧化，並提高火災／爆炸風險。
維護：教材要求每 12 個月徹底檢查變壓器，必要時處理各項問題。
塗裝：仔細檢查油箱與附件，特別是焊縫生鏽。以鋼刷等方式徹底除鏽，再塗紅氧化物底漆、適當中塗及面漆。
乾燥呼吸器：檢查矽膠顏色；教材所述型式若粉紅色到達容器一半高度，建議換成完全有效的矽膠。
再生：不建議加熱呼吸器使矽膠乾燥再用，因教材指出超過 170°F 可不可逆地損傷矽膠晶體。

> 週期、矽膠顏色與 170°F 為教材特定描述；實際依所用呼吸器／乾燥劑規格。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p69。


## PDF p70｜Buchholz 與溫度指示器維護

### English OCR

Gas and Oil (Buchholz) Surge Relay

In order to test the alarm and trip mechanisms,the relay is provided with a petcock which enables air to be injected into the body of the relay. To test operation of the alarm element, dry air from an air bottle should be slowly admitted, so that the alarm element gradually falls, until the switch operates. The quantity of air required to operate the switch may be observed on the graduate scale on inspection windows on each side of the relay and should be noted for the purpose of comparison on future tests.

To test the trip element, the valve controlling the air bottle should be opened quickly so that air rushes in, impinges on the flap, depresses it, and operates the switch. The approximate minimum air pressure required to operate the switch should be recorded for the purpose of future comparison.

Temperature Indicators

Instrument Calibration

The indicating accuracy of the instrument should be checked. Remove the temperature sensitive bulb from its pocket on the tank and immerse it in oil together with thermometer and a controlled heating element. The oil should now be warmed through definite intervals to a maximum temperature of 120oC,and instrument readings checked against the thermometer. The instrument should be allowed to steady off at each interval to allow for its inherent indicating time lag, and readings should be accurate to within ± 2oC.

o

Switch Adjustment

The mercury switches should be adjusted to operate at the required values.The recommended maximum settings are:

Oil Temperature WindingTemperature

85°C 105°C

To adjust the switches, remove the Instrument bezel and slacken the switch clamp screw located on the extremity of the switch arm. Adjust the switch to the desired operating temperature on the setting scale and re-tighten the clamp screw.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

氣體／油流突波繼電器（Buchholz）：教材描述以繼電器小閥注入空氣測試警報與跳脫。緩慢注入乾燥空氣，讓警報元件逐步下降至開關動作；由兩側觀察窗刻度讀取並記錄所需氣量，供後續比較。
測試跳脫元件時，快速開啟氣瓶控制閥，使空氣衝入並推下擋板而動作；記錄約略最低動作氣壓，以利後續比較。
溫度指示器校正：將感溫球自油箱套管取出，與溫度計、可控加熱器一起浸入油中。分階段升溫至最高 120°C，每階段待讀值穩定，補償儀器時間延遲，再與溫度計比較；教材要求誤差在 ±2°C 內。
開關調整：水銀開關應於所需設定值動作。教材建議最高油溫設定 85°C、繞組溫度 105°C。取下儀表外框，鬆開開關臂端的固定螺絲，在設定刻度調到所需溫度後鎖緊。

> 本頁完整翻譯教材的歷史維護示例；氣壓、溫度、開關型式與程式不是可直接套用的現場設定。

## PDF p71｜油位、密封接合與油維護

### English OCR

When carrying out this adjustment, the mercury switch table should be supported so that excessive pressure is not applied to the Bourdon movement.

Oil level

Check oil level preferably when cold. Top up with clean oil.

Oil tight joints

After the equipment has been in service for some time, it may be found that the compression gaskets have shrunk a little and that small oil leaks have developed at some of the joints. It is advisable,therefore, to carry out a general tightening of the joints after a period of not more than 12 months after commissioning. The correct method is to tighten each bolt slightly, moving round the flange until the whole joint is perfectly tight. Joints should never be tightened at one point alone even if the oil leaks appear to have developed ( : this point. Failure to adopt this method of tightening may result in a serious oil leak which will be found difficult to check. If, after carrying out the tightening described above, the oil leak still persists, the oil should be lowered below the gasket level and an inspection of the gasket carried out. If damage or deterioration is evident, a replacement gasket should be fitted, and if necessary,the transformer should be vented.

Oil Maintenance

The oil supplied with the transformer is a pure hydrocarbon mineral oil conforming to the requirements of BS148 for Insulating Oil.

The British Standard Code of Practice BS5730 (1979) entitled "Maintenance of Insulating Oils" deals comprehensively with methods of sampling, testing and treatment. The Maintenance Engineer is, therefore, advised to refer to this publication.

The oil in a transformer operating under normal load conditions adequately ventilated and free from moisture, will show little oil deterioration after years of service. If, due to overload or inadequate ventilating conditions, the oil temperature is high for prolonged periods, deterioration of the oil will be accelerated. Routine oil sampling and testing should be carried out periodically, so that from the information obtained it may be possible to determine whether the oil is suitable for further service.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

調整水銀開關時，應支撐其平臺，避免對波登管機構施加過大壓力。
油位：最好於冷態檢查，以乾淨油補足。
油密接合：投運一段時間後，壓縮墊片可能略收縮並滲油。教材建議試運轉後不超過 12 個月，對接合處全面重新緊固。沿法蘭逐一稍微鎖緊螺栓，繞行至整個接合均勻緊密；即使某處漏油，也不可只鎖該點，否則可能產生更嚴重且難抑制的漏油。
若仍滲漏，將油位降至墊片以下並檢查，發現損壞／劣化則更換，必要時對變壓器排氣。
油維護：隨裝置供應的油為符合 BS 148 的純碳氫礦物絕緣油。教材推薦參閱 BS 5730（1979）《絕緣油維護》，其涵蓋取樣、測試與處理。
正常負載、通風適當且無水分時，油多年後可能僅輕微劣化；過載或通風不佳導致長期高油溫，會加速劣化。應定期取樣測試，以判斷是否適合繼續使用。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p71。


## PDF p72｜絕緣油取樣、外觀與介電強度

### English OCR

Oil Sampling(BS5263:1975 or I.E.C. 475:1974)

Samples of oil should be drawn from the transformer when the oil is warm. Samplingof dielectrics is covered by BS5263:1975 and I.E.C.475:1974.

Samples taken on site are frequently found to be contaminated owing to inadequate cleaning of the drain valve. It is essential that the valve be first thoroughly cleaned externally and then wiped with clean material reasonably free from fibre (such as thin smooth paper) followed by a similar material soaked in clean oil.

Finally, the valve should be flushed by draining off a sufficient quantity of oil to ensure that the sample obtained is representative of the oil at the bottom of the tank.

Stoppered glass sampling bottles of one-quart size are recommended. They must be absolutely clean and dry and should be rinsed with the first sample drawn.

Tests should be carried out as soon as possible after drawing a sample.

Inspectionof Samples

A limited but useful amount of information can be obtained from the colour and odour of the oil and this should be noted for record purposes.

Cloudiness in the oil may be due to suspended moisture or suspended solid matter such as iron oxide or sludge. The moisture can be detected by crackle test.

If the oil is dark brown, the presence of dissolved Asphaltenes may be suspected. If the colour is green the presence of copper soaps is indicated, and it may be expected that further deterioration of the oil will be rapid. An acrid smell is indicative of volatile acids which can cause corrosion, and which may render the oil unsuitable for treatment on site; a petrol-like or acetylene odour may indicate a low flash-point due to a fault or some other cause.

ElectricStrength

The electric strength of the oil should be tested after filling or refilling prior to energising then after 1 year and subsequently every 6 years (for transformer less than 80kV).

Apparatus in accordance with BS148 is satisfactory for this test, and the oil should withstand 22kVfor one minute without breakdown and the average breakdown of tests should be 30 kV or greater. Both of these tests should be carried out with an electrode gap

### 繁體中文學習譯文

油取樣（教材引用 BS 5263:1975／IEC 475:1974）：應在油溫暖時取樣。現場樣品常因放油閥未清潔而汙染；應先徹底清潔閥外部，以乾淨、少纖維材料（如薄滑紙）擦拭，再用浸乾淨油的相同材料擦拭。最後放出足夠油沖洗，使樣品能代表油箱底部油況。
建議使用 1 誇脫、附塞玻璃瓶，必須完全清潔乾燥，並以最初取出的樣油沖洗。取樣後應盡快測試。
外觀檢查：顏色與氣味可提供有限但有用的資訊，應記錄。混濁可能是懸浮水分，或氧化鐵、油泥等固體；水分可用爆裂聲試驗（crackle test）檢出。
深褐色可能有溶解瀝青質；綠色指向銅皂，可能很快進一步劣化；刺激味可能是會腐蝕的揮發性酸，並可能不適合現場處理；汽油／乙炔味可能指向故障或其他原因造成低閃火點。
介電強度：教材對低於 80 kV 變壓器，列填油／補充換油後送電前測試、1 年後再測、之後每 6 年。以符合 BS 148 的儀器，油應承受 22 kV、1 分鐘不擊穿，且擊穿試驗平均值至少 30 kV；電極間距接下一頁。

> 取樣與測試標準、週期、電壓及判準按舊教材翻譯，不能直接視為目前適用試驗規範。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p72。


## PDF p73｜水分爆裂聲試驗與酸值

### English OCR

or 2.5mm. If frequent transient sparking occurs, this suggests the presence of foreign matter, e.g. moisture, fibrous material, carbon particles etc and the oil should be filtered. The test should be repeated after filtering.

Crackle Test for Moisture

The crackle test is a simple and useful test for detecting the presence of suspended moisture

in oil.

Test Option No. 1 Test Option No. 2

A metal rod 12.5mm in diameter heated to a dull redness is lowered to the bottom of the receptacle and used to stir the oil thoroughly.

It consists of pouring enough of the oil into a clean, dry test tube about 125mm long and 12.5mm in diameter to fill it quarter full. The temperature of the oil should be 15-25oC. The tube is then heated in a silent During this stirring process no crackle should be detected. Oil that does not pass this test should be suitably treated.

flame until the oil begins to boil. If any audible crackling occurs, then water is present.

Acidity

The pungent odour of the oil will give an indication of acidity. If such odour is present, immediate inspection for corrosion of the tank and cover above the oil level should be carried out steps taken to ascertain the acidity value. The recommended test period for transformers less than 80kV is every 6 years.

ne equipment and solutions required to perform this test may be obtained from suppliers of laboratory apparatus and chemicals; in case of doubt the advice of oil suppliers should be sought. The suggested acidity limits are as follows:

a. When the acidity is below 0.3mg KOH/g no action need be taken if the oil is satisfactory in other respects.

b. When the acidity is greater than 0.3mg KOH/g performance test more frequently.

c. When the acidity exceeds 0.5mg KOH/g the oil should be replaced, and existing oil needs to be reclaimed.

d. If the acidity is more than 2.5 KOH/g request a replacement transformer.

KOH = Potassium Hydroxide

### 繁體中文學習譯文

承前：兩種介電試驗電極間距為 2.5 mm。頻繁瞬時火花可能表示有水分、纖維、碳等異物，應過濾後重測。
爆裂聲試驗用於檢測油中懸浮水分。教材列兩種方式：
1）將直徑 12.5 mm 金屬棒加熱至暗紅，降至容器底部並充分攪拌；不應聽到爆裂聲，不合格油須適當處理。
2）乾淨乾燥、長約 125 mm、直徑 12.5 mm 的試管裝油約四分之一，初始油溫 15–25°C；以無聲火焰加熱至油開始沸騰，若有可聽見的爆裂聲，表示有水。
酸值：刺鼻氣味可提示酸性；有此氣味時，應立即檢查油麵以上油箱／頂蓋腐蝕，並量測酸值。教材對低於 80 kV 變壓器建議每 6 年測試。儀器、溶液可向實驗器材／化學品供應商取得，有疑問詢問油商。
建議界限：a）低於 0.3 mg KOH/g，其他性質良好則不需處置；b）高於 0.3 mg KOH/g，提高試驗頻率；c）超過 0.5 mg KOH/g，換油並再生處理舊油；d）超過 2.5 KOH/g，要求更換變壓器。KOH = 氫氧化鉀。

> 這是原教材試驗方法之翻譯，不作實作指令；涉及高溫油與火源。第 d 項原文缺 mg，不能自行補為已確認單位。

## PDF p74｜酸值、油泥與閉杯閃火點

### English OCR

If the acidity is allowed to exceed l.Omg KOH/g,there is a considerable risk of sludge precipitation and corrosion of metal surfaces above oil level by condensed acidic vapours. It may be possible to retain oil in service with acidity above this figure, provided that frequent internal examination of the transformer is made, but this practice is not recommended since the oil may reach a state at which it is not economical for it to be reconditioned.

Regular filtration checks the development of acidity but is not effective in removing acid once it has formed. To remove acid the oil may be returned to the oil supplier for reconditioning, or new oil may be provided, but in either case the lower the acidity at the time of changing the oil, the less the new filling will be affected by acid absorption from the core and windings.

Sludge

Although severe sludge is not frequently experienced in transformer oil in service,it is, ( nevertheless, a most serious form of deterioration, not only because of the danger of insulation breakdown owing to the restriction of cooling, but also because a transformer with sludge deposits is difficult to clean thoroughly without dismantling.

Although there may be no solid deposit visible in oil samples,it is still possible that sludge may have formed, and that the concentration in the oil has not yet reached a point where deposition is occurring. The presence of sludge may then be detected by dilution of the oil with normal Heptane and it is recommended that a test should be carried out when the acidity of the oil approaches l.Omg KOH/g.

When comparison of records as suggested under "Recording the Results"indicates the presence of precipitated sludge it may be necessary,even though the acidity may be within the prescribed limit, to consider applying treatment or changing the oil. When sludge is allowed to accumulate, the oil circulating ducts become choked which results in higher core and winding temperatures with consequent formation of still more sludge,the action being cumulative.

Flashpoint(Closed)

Flash-point tests should be made if the oil has been subjected to a high temperature due to an internal fault, or shows any sign of unusual odour, but they are not otherwise necessary.

A slow fall of the flashpoint of oil in a transformer may occur with increasing age and is not harmful. A fall exceeding16.5oCor a flash-point below 130oCmay indicate unsatisfactory conditions such as electrical discharge, excessively high internal temperature, core faults or

### 繁體中文學習譯文

酸值若超過 1.0 mg KOH/g，油泥沉澱及酸蒸氣凝結腐蝕油麵以上金屬的風險顯著增加。頻繁內部檢查下可能仍可保留使用，但教材不建議，因油可能劣化至不具再生經濟性。
定期過濾可抑製酸性發展，但不能去除已形成的酸；可送油商再生或更換新油。換油時酸值越低，鐵心與繞組殘留酸被新油吸收的影響越小。
油泥：雖不常嚴重發生，卻是重大劣化；它限制冷卻，增加絕緣失效危險，而且不拆卸難以徹底清除。樣品無固體沉積，不表示未形成油泥，可能僅濃度尚未達沉澱點。以正庚烷稀釋油可檢測；教材建議酸值接近 1.0 mg KOH/g 時測試。
若紀錄比較顯示油泥沉澱，即使酸值仍在界限內，也可能需處理／換油。累積油泥堵塞迴圈油道，使鐵心與繞組升溫，進而形成更多油泥，成為惡性迴圈。
閉杯閃火點：內部故障造成高溫，或有異常氣味時應測試；否則一般不需。隨年齡緩慢下降未必有害；下降超過 16.5°C 或低於 130°C，可能表示放電、內部過熱、鐵心故障，或異物造成帶電部件與機架間導電路徑（續下頁）。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p74。


## PDF p75｜油試驗紀錄與開關裝置油

### English OCR

foreign matter providing a conducting path between live parts and the frame of the transformer, in which case the units should be taken out of service for examination.

Recording the Results

It is essential to keep records of all tests. The acidity should be plotted on a graph with time as the base,and the records should include relevant operating data such as maximum loads and maximum oil temperatures.

Switchgear Oil

. ne major component parts of oil filled switchgear which have to be considered are circuit breakers (CBs) voltage transformers VT's and oil switches (RMUs).

In circuit breakers the chief function of the oil is the quenching of the electric arc that forms between the contacts as they separate, although insulation and cooling are also important.

The deterioration of oil in circuit breakers due to the effects of oxidation is normally very slow and while there may be some acidity development it does not usually extend to sludge formation. The extremely high temperature created by the arc, however, causes the formation of carbon particles resulting in a reduction of the electric strength of the oil. Another major hazard is caused by the deposition of carbon upon the horizontal surfaces of {J isulating material which can lead to surface tracking.

Voltage Transformers (VTs)

Require the oil to be replaced at the same time as the relevant OCBoil.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

承前：若有上述異物導電路徑等異常，應將裝置停用檢查。
結果紀錄：必須保留所有試驗結果，以時間為橫軸繪製酸值趨勢，並記錄最大負載、最高油溫等相關運轉資料。
開關裝置油：主要考量油斷路器、VT 與油開關（RMU）。在斷路器中，油最主要用途是熄滅接點分離形成的電弧，絕緣與冷卻同樣重要。
斷路器油的氧化通常很慢，可能酸化但通常不至形成油泥；然而電弧極高溫會形成碳微粒，降低介電強度。碳沉積於絕緣材料水平表面，也可能導致沿面漏電痕跡／爬電（surface tracking）。
VT：教材要求與相關 OCB 同時更換其油。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p75。


## PDF p76｜開關裝置換油注意事項

### English OCR

Maintenance of Switchgear Oil

Maintenance of switchgear oil should be carried out in accordance with manufacturer's handbook.

ChangingInsulatingOil

A small extra quantity of new oil is needed to rinse the interior of the tank and immersed parts. It is essential that the tank and surfaces of conductors and insulators be kept free from fibres. Such fibres can be introduced by the use of unsatisfactory cleaning materials during plant maintenance. In practice the only efficient and permissible materials are plastics, and chamois leathers.

It is also essential that the tank and other surfaces be kept free from moisture from the hands of working personnel, such moisture can lower the electric strength of the new oil tv below the required value.

There should be as little aeration as possible during the filling of tanks and, as far as possible, the end of the delivery pipe should be held below the surface of the oil to avoid splashing.

A standing period of not less than one hour should be allowed to facilitate de-aeration before re-energising the equipment.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

開關裝置油的維護應依製造商手冊。
更換絕緣油：需額外少量新油沖洗油箱內部與浸油部件。油箱、導體及絕緣表面不可有纖維；維護時使用不合適擦拭材料可能引入纖維。教材稱塑膠材料與麂皮才是有效且允許的清潔材料。
也必須避免工作人員手部水分沾到油箱或其他表面，否則可能使新油介電強度降至要求以下。
注油時應盡量減少混入空氣，出油管末端盡可能保持在油麵以下，避免濺油。
教材要求重新送電前靜置至少 1 小時，以利排氣。

> 材料與靜置時間是本教材描述；實際程式仍以裝置製造商要求為準。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p76。


## PDF p77｜絕緣油搬運、儲存與衛生

### English OCR

Handling

WATER WATER

AIR ESCAPES

WARM COOL POWERFUL SUCTION

CREATED

AIR SPACE REDUCED

Water drawn in when

oil and air contract when coding. Oil and air in barrel expand when warm some of the air above oil escapes.

WATER-Figure 1

To ensure satisfactory service the utmost care in handling oil is essential. In practice, owing to contamination of the containers, difficulty may be experienced in maintaining the purity of the oil if it is transferred from one vessel to another. Once a vessel or drum has been filled with moist oil it is extremely difficult to clean. Drums should be clearly marked to indicate whether they are for clean or for dirty oil and should be reserved for the type indicated.

Where portable oil handling equipment is used, such as flexible pipework and pumps, it should be inspected to ensure that it is free from dirt and water and then flushed with clean oil before use. The clean oil should have been recently tested and if contained in drums their Hing orifices should be clean.

Drums used for transport and storage should be kept under cover. In addition, when oil is stored in drums, they should be placed in such a position that there is a head of oil on the stopper or plug to prevent the entry of water during storage. See Figure 1.

Hygiene Precautions

When handling and using mineral insulating oils attention is drawn to the need for personal hygiene.

For greater detail on the effects of insulating oil and the precautions to be taken, see Health and Safety Executive Cautionary Notice SHW 397 entitled "Effects of Mineral Oil on the

Skin".

### 繁體中文學習譯文

圖 1：油桶變暖時油與空氣膨脹，部分空氣逸出；冷卻時油與空氣收縮，可能產生強吸力把積水吸入。圖示建議讓桶塞處保持有油覆蓋，防止水進入。
必須極謹慎處理油。容器汙染使油在轉桶時難維持純淨；曾裝濕油的容器或油桶非常難清乾淨。應清楚標記乾淨油／髒油用途，並專桶專用。
可攜軟管、泵浦等使用前，檢查無汙垢／水分，再以乾淨油沖洗；該油應近期測試合格，油桶注入口亦應清潔。
運輸與儲存油桶應置於遮蔽處，並擺放成桶塞／堵頭有油壓頭覆蓋的位置，防止儲存期間進水。
衛生：處理礦物絕緣油須注意個人衛生。更詳盡的影響與措施，教材指向 HSE SHW 397《礦物油對皮膚的影響》。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p77。


## PDF p78｜高壓配電系統概述

### English OCR

High-voltage Distribution Systems

Introduction

An electrical distribution system is used to connect the source of supply to the consumer's equipment at their point of utilisation. Electrical power is often distributed at voltages higher than those supplied to the domestic consumers.

The use of high voltage for electrical distribution has the following advantages.

Higher voltages result in lower currents for a given amount of electrical power.

Since the currents needed to supply given amounts of power are reduced, it is possible to use smaller diameter conductors.

The voltage drop is reduced and is a smaller proportion of the initial voltage.

Power losses are reduced.

There are a number of disadvantages which include the increased danger introduced by high-voltage and the need for expensive insulation.

The definition of electrical system is given in the Electricity at Work Regulations 1989, these lead to the following definitions, from Regulation 2, with respect to electrical distribution.

o

‘Circuit Conductor' means any conductor in a system which is intended to carry electric current in normal conditions, or to be energised in normal conditions, and include a combined neutral and earth conductor, but does not include a conductor provided solely to perform a protective function by connection to earth or other reference point;

'ElectricalEquipment' includes anything used, intended to be used or installed for use,to generate, provide, transmit, transform, rectify, convert, conduct, distribute, control, store, measure or use electrical energy.

'System' means an electrical system in which all the electrical equipment is, or may be, electrically connected to a common source of electrical energy, and include such source and such equipment.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

配電系統把電源連到使用者使用點的裝置，配電電壓常高於住宅使用者電壓。
高壓配電優點：相同功率下電流較低；因此可用較小直徑導體；壓降較低且佔起始電壓比例更小；功率損失減少。缺點包括高壓造成更高危險，以及昂貴的絕緣需求。
教材引《1989 年工作場所電氣法規》第 2 條：
電路導體：正常時預定承載電流或帶電的導體，包括中性與接地合用導體，但不含僅藉接地／參考點連線執行保護功能的導體。
電氣裝置：任何用於、預定用於或安裝供發電、供電、輸電、變壓、整流、轉換、傳導、配電、控制、儲存、量測或使用電能的物品。
系統：所有電氣裝置已經或可能連線至共同電源的系統，包括電源及裝置本身。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p78。


## PDF p79｜配電規劃：放射式與環網

### English OCR

When technical and economic considerations dictate the adoption of a high-voltage distribution system, account is taken of:

• Immediate requirements and probable site development

• The customer's requirements for reliability of supply

• The division of loads into essential and non-essential and the required speed of restoration of supply after failure

• The need to otherwise to provide a standby supply, its capacity type and location

• The need to lay cables for control, indication and protection, in conjunction with power cables

• Operation and maintenance requirements.

The two basic distribution methods are via Radial Feeders or Ring Mains (see Fig 1).

ISS ISS

DSS C DSS A

DSS A DSS B

DSS C DSS B

RADIAL FEEDER RINGMAIN

ISS = INTAKE SUBSTATION
DSS = DISTRIBUTION SUBSTATION

Figure 1

The use of Radial Feeders where possible is advised provided they satisfy the requirements as stated above. In practice, where the number of Distribution Sub-stations exceeds (say) six and/or a central Standby is provided, Ring Mains are used, often with the Standby Sub-station sited remote from the Intake Sub-station.

Distribution Sub-stations should be sited in accessible positions with direct road access, as near as possible to the centre of the load to be served or nearest to the heaviest load. The area to be served is then supplied via low voltage feeders serving individual buildings.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

技術及經濟因素決定採高壓配電時，須考量：當前需求與未來場址發展；使用者供電可靠度要求；重要／非重要負載區分及停電後復電速度；備用電源的需要、容量、型式及位置；配合電力電纜敷設控制、指示、保護電纜的需要；操作與維護需求。
兩種基本方式：放射式饋線（Radial feeders）與環網（Ring mains）。圖 1：ISS = 進線變電站；DSS = 配電變電站；圖列 DSS A、B、C。
若能符合要求，教材建議盡可能採放射式。實務上，配電站超過約 6 座及／或有集中備用電源時，常用環網，備用電源站常設於離進線站較遠處。
DSS 應設於可直接由道路進入的可及位置，盡可能接近負載中心或最大負載，再以低壓饋線供應各個建築。

## PDF p81｜閉環配電系統

### English OCR

Closed RingMains

The design of ring mains will depend upon the way in which they are to be operated. If there is a requirement for high security of supply, then a ring main that can be operated as a Closed ring may be selected. The typical arrangement of a closed ring system is shown in Fig

4.

ISS

SBSS

DSS A

REC

DSS D

CLOSED RING SYSTEM

DSSC DSS B

Figure 4

This type of ring main has circuit breakers installed throughout, and under normal conditions all would be switched ON (unless subject to interlocking i.e., Standby circuit breaker). As circuit breakers are generally items of indoor equipment, they will require a building in which to locate them.

he protection equipment is selected to isolate only the faulty equipment in the event of a fault. This is usually called unit protection.

A fault on a local transformer would result in its corresponding circuit breaker tripping to

off.

A fault on a ring feeder cable would result in the circuit breakers at either end of the faulty length of cable tripping to off.

Due to the arrangement of the protection outlined in the paragraph above, it will be necessary to install a supervisory system to monitor the state of each circuit breaker. Without such a monitoring system the tripping to off of a single circuit breaker could go undetected.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

環網設計取決於運轉方式。若需要高度供電可靠性，可選閉環運轉。圖 4 列 ISS、SBSS、REC 與 DSS A–D。
閉環各處都裝斷路器；正常狀態全部 ON，受聯鎖限制者除外，例如備援斷路器。斷路器一般屬室內裝置，因此需建築容納。
保護裝置選擇為只隔離故障裝置，通常稱單元保護（unit protection）。本地變壓器故障，對應斷路器跳脫；環網電纜故障，故障段兩端斷路器跳脫。
由於這種保護配置，必須有監督系統監測各斷路器狀態，否則單一斷路器跳脫可能未被發現。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p81。


## PDF p82｜開環配電系統

### English OCR

This type of distribution system is very expensive due to the cost of:

a) Circuit Breakers

b) Indoor Sub-stations

c) Closed Ring Protection Systems

d) Supervisory Systems.

Thus, the use of a closed ring distribution system is usually restricted to sites of prime importance.

Open Ring Mains

If the requirements for the electricity supply do not warrant the expense of a closed ring then a ring main normally operated with an OPEN point will be the next best option. This type of system when compared with a radial, gives improved speed in restoration of supplies in the event of a fault, and more flexibility in operation and for maintenance. A typical arrangement is shown in Fig 5.

ISS

SBSS

IL

IL

REC

INTER-CONNECTOR

OPEN RING SYSTEM

DSSD DSS A

DSSC

Figure 5

DSSB

This type of ring main requires circuit breakers installed at each end of the ring. The switchgear located at Distribution Sub-stations around the ring will consist of switches and fuse switches. If installed today, a combination of two switches and fuse switches could be obtained in the form of a Ring Main Unit.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

閉環系統昂貴，成本來自斷路器、室內變電站、閉環保護及監督系統，通常限用於極重要的場址。
若供電需求不值得負擔閉環費用，正常維持一處開路點的環網是次一選擇。相較放射式，故障後復電較快，運轉與維護更靈活。
圖 5 開環例：ISS、SBSS、REC、聯鎖 IL、互連線及 DSS A–D。環網兩端須有斷路器，沿線配電站使用開關及熔絲開關；兩個開關加一組熔絲開關的組合可採 RMU。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p82。


## PDF p83｜開路點選擇與環網擴充

### English OCR

Switches, Fuse switches and RingMain Units are all available as outdoor switchgear, thus there is no need for a building to house them.

Under normal conditions all switchgear would be switched on (unless subject to interlocking

- e.g., Standby circuit breaker) with the exception of the switch on the ring which will form the OPEN point. The ring should normally be open at one point only to ensure all cables are monitored by their individual ring feeder circuit breaker.

In determining which switch should form the open point the following factors should be considered:

a) The importance of loads

b) Balancingthe number of switches to be operated either side of the open point ( ) Cable route lengths.

A fault on a ring feeder cable would lead to the circuit breaker located in the ISSor SBSS disconnecting the faulty circuit. This would result in a loss of supply to all sub-stations between that circuit breaker and OPEN point.

In order to locate the fault (in the absence of earth fault passage indicators) it will be necessary to carry out sequential switching. This will consist of switching off all switches on the affected leg of the ring and then switching on the circuit breaker and then the switches sequentially.

z Where the numbers of Distribution Sub-stations is excessive (say15 to 20+) or the total load \jn Ringexceeds (say) 4000 kVA,then it may be necessary to consider the following alternatives:

Formation of a second open ring main from ISSto SBSS

Installation of an interconnector from the ISSor SBSSto a sub-station (equipped with circuit breakers) located at approximately mid point of the existing ring (see Fig6).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

開關、熔絲開關與 RMU 均有戶外型，不必建屋。正常時除聯鎖限制裝置及形成開路點的環網開關外，其餘皆合上。環網通常只留一處開路點，確保所有電纜均受其對應環網饋線斷路器監測。
選擇開路點須考量：負載重要性；平衡開路點兩側需操作的開關數；電纜路徑長度。
環網饋線電纜故障會使 ISS 或 SBSS 的斷路器切除該迴路，造成該斷路器至開路點間所有配電站停電。
沒有接地故障透過指示器時，教材描述以順序切換定位：先斷開受影響環段所有開關，再合上斷路器，之後依序合上開關。此為教材的故障定位敘述。
若配電站過多（例 15–20 座以上）或環網總負載超過約 4000 kVA，可考慮：從 ISS 至 SBSS 建第二個開環；或由 ISS／SBSS 加互連線至原環網約中點、配備斷路器的配電站（見圖 6）。

> 順序切換定位是教材案例敘述，並非授權對未知故障進行試送電。學習用途，不可直接作為現場操作指令。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p83。

## PDF p86｜配電電纜：路徑與選型

### English OCR

Distribution Cables

Routing Cables

In selecting a cable route, the following factors should be considered:

a) Follow physical features (roads, fences etc.)

b) Avoid open ground (especially if liable to development)

c) Avoid physical obstructions (drains, ducts, trees, water etc.)

d) Avoid sensitive or restricted access areas

e) Where possible utilise a common route for several cables (but use separate routes for alternative feeds and ring cables to and from substations).

Where crossings of paved areas are unavoidable, and at road crossings it is recommended that ducts are to be provided with separate ways for each cable together with at least 25% spare duct ways.

Selection of Cables

The selection of the type and size of cable for a particular application requires consideration of several factors including:

a) Operating voltage

b) Normal full load current and whether it is sustained or intermittent.

c) Derating factors due to:

i. Thermal resistivity of soil ii. Ground temperature iii. Ambient air temperature iv. Depth of laying

v. Grouping and proximity of other cables and services.

d) Prospective fault current and the type of system protection

e) Voltage drop

f) Degree of mechanical protection required and the method of installation.

g) Overall diameter, flexibility, and minimum bending radius

h) Type of termination and space in termination compartment

i) Cost and delivery time.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

選擇電纜路徑應考量：a）沿道路、圍籬等實體特徵；b）避開空地，尤其可能開發處；c）避開排水、管道、樹木及水域等障礙；d）避開敏感或限制進出區；e）可行時多條電纜共用路徑，但替代電源及往返變電站的環網電纜應分開路徑。
不可避免穿越鋪面或道路時，建議每條電纜各有獨立管道，另留至少 25% 備用管孔。
選型與尺寸因素：操作電壓；正常滿載電流及連續／間歇性；降額因素（土壤熱阻、地溫、環境氣溫、埋深、分組及鄰近電纜／公用管線）；預期故障電流與保護型式；壓降；機械保護與安裝方式；外徑、柔性、最小彎曲半徑；終端型式與終端室空間；成本及交期。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p86。


## PDF p87｜電纜材料與導體尺寸

### English OCR

Where several types of cable are equally suitable on technical and delivery grounds then the selection should be made on the basis of lowest total cost (including installation and operation).

Consideration should always be given to the use of cables having aluminium (AL) conductors as an alternative to copper (CU) conductors. After allowing for the larger size of an aluminium conductor necessary,the choice of aluminium may show worthwhile savings in many situations.

Where armouring of single core ac cables is necessary, non-magnetic armour should be specified.

Care should be taken to ensure that PVCcables will not be installed or operated outside the ( ,'mperature range recommended by the manufacturer, as:

a) they are liable to crack if handled at temperatures in the region of 00Cor lower, and

b) at high working temperatures the insulant will soften and may flow, causingvariation in the insulation thickness.

Ethylene propylene rubber (EPR)insulated cable, which have greater flexibility and a higher operating temperature limit is suitable for use up to llkV in appropriate circumstances i.e. cables are run across water-logged ground.

It is important that the drawings show clearly the type of cable required. Cables complying with British Standard 6480 shall be identified by a full description and not \jy code letters.

Cross linked Polyethylene (XLPE)insulated cables are now the most commonly available type of cable from manufacturers for distribution purposes.

Conductor Sizing

For LVcables reference should be made to the IEEWiring Regulations and/or Manufacturers data to assist in considering the factors listed on page one.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

若多種電纜在技術及交期上同樣適合，應按含安裝與運轉的最低總成本選擇。
可考慮鋁導體替代銅導體；即使需較大截面，仍可能有實質節省。交流單芯電纜若需鎧裝，應指定非磁性鎧裝。
PVC 電纜不得超出製造商安裝／運轉溫度範圍：在約 0°C 或更低溫搬動可能龜裂；高工作溫度使絕緣軟化甚至流動，造成厚度變化。
EPR（乙丙橡膠）絕緣更柔軟、工作溫度上限較高；教材表示適當情況可用至 11 kV，例如跨越積水土地。圖面應清楚標示所需電纜型式。符合 BS 6480 的電纜應用完整描述識別，不只用程式碼字母。
XLPE（交連聚乙烯）是教材描述的常見配電絕緣型式。
低壓導體尺寸：參閱 IEE 配線規則及／或製造商資料，以評估前述因素。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p87。


## PDF p88｜電纜搬運與直埋準備

### English OCR

For HV cables, manufacturer's data should be used with particular attention being paid to:

a) the need to provide for any probable load growth, and

b) prospective fault currents and the short circuit capacity.

Cable Handling and Laying

The need for care when handling and laying cables cannot be over emphasised. Apart form the obvious cases of workmen inadvertently piercing cables, faults on underground cables are nearly always attributable to poor workmanship either in laying or jointing.

UnloadingCableDrums

Where a crane is available it must be used to unload drums of cable using a suitable spindfl through the centre of the drum as a means of lifting. Should a crane not be available then a suitable ramp of heavy timber should be constructed for the purpose. The drum should be rolled down the ramp, its progress carefully controlled by ropes secured to a spindle through the centre of the drum. On no occasion should a drum of cable be dropped, even onto soft ground as flattening of the inner layers will occur. Damage of this sort will not be easily apparent.

RollingCables

Drums of cable must be rolled in the direction of the arrow painted on the side - this again is to avoid damaging the cable.

c

LayingCablesDirectin the Ground

a) It is recommended that trenches shall be excavated to provide the minimum cover specified in Table1 and minimum distance between cablesTable 2.

b) Before cables are laid the bottom of the trench shall be evenly graded, cleared of loose stones and then covered with a 75mm layer of earth which has passed through a sieve, with a maximum mesh of 12mm or with sand where local ground is unsuitable. To avoid risk of damage during handling, cable laying shall be carried out only when the ambient temperature is above OoCand the cable is above this temperature and has been so for the previous 24 hours.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

高壓電纜應依製造商資料，特別注意可能的負載成長、預期故障電流及短路能力。
搬運與敷設必須謹慎。除施工人員誤刺等明顯事件外，教材認為地下電纜故障大多歸因於敷設或接續施工不良。
卸電纜盤：有吊車時，應用合適心軸穿過盤中心起吊；沒有時，以厚木料建適當坡道，中心心軸繫繩控制滾下。任何情況都不可讓電纜盤墜落，即使地面柔軟也會壓扁內層，且不易發現。
滾動時依盤側箭頭方向，避免損傷。
直埋：a）溝槽埋深與電纜間距依後頁表 1、2。b）槽底整平並清除鬆石，鋪 75 mm 厚、透過最大 12 mm 網孔篩的土；現地土不適用則用砂。教材要求環境與電纜溫度均高於 0°C，且電纜過去 24 小時也保持高於此溫度，才可敷設。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p88。


## PDF p89｜直埋施工、覆土深度與間距表

### English OCR

c) Power cables shall be pulled in over adequately spaced cable roller and the resulting surplus cable shall be snaked across the width of the trench. In straight run trenches cable crossing is not permitted except where cables branch from the main run. At each draw-in point, joint or junction box the cable shall be left slack. Cable shall not be pulled taut to straighten them after laying. Cable stockings shall be used for cable hauling. In order to ensure that the strain is taken on the cores as well as the sheath when cables are laid with a cable stocking, a solid plumbed hauling end shall be made. The only permissible exceptions to this requirement are lengths of up to 10m not pulled into a thrust boring.

d) After laying, the cables shall be covered with enough fine sieved earth (or sand where the local ground is unsuitable) to ensure a 50mm cover after tamping. Warning tapes and/or covers may be laid over the cables and the trench filled in and compacted (see para 4.5).

e) Reinstatement shall be affected by backfilling in 100mm layers and hand ramming the first two layers. Power rammers may be used for the remaining layers. The turf shall be replaced, and the level of finished reinstatement shall not protrude more than 25mm above the normal ground level.

c）以間距適當的滾輪拉纜，多餘長度在槽寬內蛇形擺放。直線溝槽除分支外不可交叉；拉入點、接頭及接線箱須留鬆弛量，不可敷後拉緊拉直。以拉纜網套牽引時，應製作實心鉛封拉纜端，讓線芯與護套共同受力；例外為不超過 10 m 且非拉入頂管的長度。
d）敷後以細篩土／適用砂覆蓋，夯實後至少 50 mm；可加警示帶／護板，再回填壓實。
e）每 100 mm 一層回填，前兩層手夯，其餘可用動力夯；恢復草皮，完成面高出原地面不得超過 25 mm。

## PDF p90｜警示帶、建築進線封堵與電纜試驗

### English OCR

WarningTapesand Covers

a) A warning tape shall be placed above each cable that is laid direct in the ground and above each cable duct. The tape shall be laid at a depth of 300mm below the finished surface level except where the depth of roadway or paved area base exceeds 300mm in which case the tape shall be laid immediately below the base.

b) Warning tapes shall be of polyethylene not less than 150mm wide and 0.1mm thick. They shall be red for HVand yellow for LVand bear the continuously repeated legend 0- CautionElectric Cable Below-0, or similar, in black letters not less than 30mm high.

TheSealingof CableDuctsinto Buildings

If the fabric of a building is breached below ground level (to permit the passage of power and telephone cables, gas, water, drainage, and other services)it is essential that such ( entries are effectively sealed to prevent gas from leaking or damaged main entering the building and creating an explosive atmosphere.

Seals should be sufficiently resilient and flexible to withstand the vibration set up by traffic and any differential movement of the building.

Where ground conditions require, the seals should be capable of withstanding ground water pressure.

For a single service it is generally sufficient for the gap between cable or pipe and the surrounding sleeve to be plugged with suitable filler.

Several types of proprietary mastic compounds are available. The compounds chosen should, however, be non-hardening, non-cracking and resistant to both moisture and gas. They should also be resistant to mineral acids,alkalis and salts and should be of a nature unlikely to cause corrosion of any materials with which they are liable to come into contact. The service cable or pipe should be wrapped with a suitable tape prior to mastic sealingin all cases where proprietary materials are being used, the manufacturer's instructions and recommendations should be carefully followed.

Testingof Laid Cables(HV)

All HVcables are to be pressure tested prior to commissioning and following repairs or alterations.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

每條直埋電纜與電纜管道上方應設警示帶，深度在完成面下 300 mm；若道路／鋪面基層更深，則緊貼基層下方。
警示帶採聚乙烯，寬不少於 150 mm、厚不少於 0.1 mm；高壓紅色、低壓黃色，以高至少 30 mm 黑字重複標示「注意，下方有電纜」或同等文字。
建築地下進線孔供電力、電話、瓦斯、水、排水等穿越時，必須有效封堵，避免洩漏／損壞的瓦斯主管氣體進入建築形成爆炸環境。封材須有足夠彈性與柔性，承受交通振動及建築差異位移；地況需要時亦須耐地下水壓。
單一管線通常可用適當填料封住電纜／管線與套管間隙。密封膠應不硬化、不龜裂、耐水及氣體，也耐礦物酸、鹼與鹽，不腐蝕接觸材料。封膠前以適當帶材包覆電纜／管線；使用專有產品應遵循製造商指示。
高壓電纜完成敷設後：教材要求試運轉前，及修理／改動後進行耐壓試驗。
