## PDF p91｜電纜耐壓試驗：教材歷史例

### English OCR

Pressure testing is to be carried out in accordance with the Electricity Safety Regulations in force on that site. Particular attention must be paid to the Regulations relating to HV enclosures and 'Sanction to Test' procedures.

The HVpressure test should be preceded by an insulation test (minimum 1000V) between conductors and earth for a period of not less than one minute to establish any serious faults.

The HVpressure test will use a de voltage of the value specified by the cable maker. The voltage shall be applied and increased gradually to the full value and maintained continuously at that value for 15 minutes.

Where the cable to be tested is a mix of old and new cables the test voltage must be that specified for old cables.

c

c

### 繁體中文學習譯文

耐壓試驗應依該場址有效的電氣安全規則，特別注意高壓試驗圍設區域及測試許可程式。
教材描述：高壓耐壓前，導體對地先以至少 1000 V 做不少於 1 分鐘的絕緣測試，以找出嚴重故障。
之後以電纜製造商指定的直流電壓逐漸升至全值，連續維持 15 分鐘。若被測迴路混用新、舊電纜，採舊電纜所指定的試驗電壓。

> 1000 V、直流、15 分鐘等均為原教材內容；方法是否適用取決於電纜材料、年齡及當前標準，不能直接套用於現場 XLPE 或其他電纜。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p91。


## PDF p92｜高壓保護與 HRC 熔絲構造

### English OCR

High-voltage Protection Systems and Devices

Introduction

The Electricity at Work Regulations1989 state in Regulation 11 that - "Efficient means, suitably located, shall be provided for protecting from excess of current every part of a system as may be necessary to prevent danger".

This requirement is met by the installation of protection systems arranged to trip items of switchgear. The protection is set to prevent damage to electrical apparatus and to avoid danger in the event of a fault.

An additional objective for any protection scheme is to ensure the maximum continuity of supply. This is done by determining the location of a fault and disconnecting the minimurn amount of apparatus necessary to clear it. When a fault occurs, a number of protective devicesmay detect it, but only those directly associated with the faulty apparatus are required to operate. This is achieved by discrimination.

A number of different high-voltage protective devices and systems are available. High rupturing capacity fuses are installed in the primary circuit whereas ACtrip coils and protective relays require a secondary circuit fed from auxiliary transformers (CT'sand VTs).

High-voltage HRC Fuses

o

The most common HVhigh rupturing capacity (HRC) fuse in use is of the powder filled type manufactured to BS2692: 1986. These can be suitable for installation in air or immersion in oil and are used in Ring Main Units and distribution fusegear.

The fuse comprises a stout ceramic or alumina barrel with sealed end capping's of metal (with or without lugs as required). The main fuse element consists of a number of parallel wires or strips, often of silver, having reduced cross sections at their fusing points. The fuse element is surrounded by an inert powder serving to extinguish the arc which forms on operation. In some cases,this powder is stated to be a "chemically treated quartz dust".

Most fuses are provided with a striker pin which is ejected through the endcap via an explosive charge detonated by a second-high resistance element after the main fuse

### 繁體中文學習譯文

《1989 年工作場所電氣法規》第 11 條要求適當位置設有效過電流保護，以避免危險。藉保護系統使開關裝置跳脫，預防裝置損壞及故障危險。
另一目標是盡量維持供電連續性：定位故障並只切除必要的最小裝置範圍。多個保護可能同時感知故障，但只有直接相關者應動作，這稱選擇性／鑑別配合（discrimination）。
高遮斷容量（HRC）熔絲設於一次迴路；交流跳脫線圈與保護繼電器則由 CT／VT 供應二次迴路。
高壓 HRC 熔絲：常見為符合 BS 2692:1986 的粉末填充型，可供空氣中或油浸使用，見於 RMU 與配電熔絲裝置。
堅固陶瓷／氧化鋁管體兩端密封金屬帽；主熔體由多條平行線／片（常用銀）組成，熔斷點截面較小，周圍惰性粉末熄滅動作電弧，部分稱化學處理石英粉。
多數配有撞針：主熔體熔斷後，第二條高阻抗熔體引燃微小爆破藥，使撞針從端帽彈出（續下頁）。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p92。

## PDF p95｜交流跳脫線圈與時限熔絲（TLF）

### English OCR

AC Trip Coils/Time Limit Fuses (Tlf's)

A direct acting overcurrent trip coil is arranged to operate the circuit breaker trip mechanism. It consists of a solenoid coil with a central moving plunger. The trip coil is arranged to operate via a current transformer which permits the use of standard trip coils, the actual operating currents being dependent on current transformer ratios.

The standard direct acting overcurrent trip coil is instantaneous in operation for all currents in excess of coil rating, but it is possible to impart an inverse time current characteristic to the device by shunting the coil with a time limit fuse. This fuse is connected across the trip coil, and because of its lower impedance passes most of the current from the CT and prevents the coil from operating until the fuse melts.

TRIP

COIL

CT TLF

Time Limit Fuse and Trip Coil arrangement

Fig 2

The TLF has a time-current characteristic very similar to that of an HRC fuse (operating times Quoted down to IO-2 sec) and are non-deteriorating in service.

The construction and operating characteristics of both trip coils and TLF's are specified in the Electricity Supply Industry (ESI) Standard 12-6. Trip coils are specified with nominal ratings of 2A or 5A, 2A being standard. The impedance of the 2A coil is between 20 and 90 times TLF impedance (varies with rating). TLF ratings available are 3A, 5A, 7.5A, 10A, 12.5A and 15A, all of which may be used with the 2A coil.

TLF manufacturers publish time current characteristics, enabling the time taken to melt the fuse element to be found for a range of currents flowing through the trip coil/TLF fuse combination. To enable this characteristic to be used to compare the operating time of a TLF protected Circuit Breaker with, say, an HRC fuse, the appropriate current value(s) must be multiplied by the current transformer ratio.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

直接動作過電流跳脫線圈由電磁線圈及中央可動柱塞構成，驅動斷路器跳離線構。由 CT 供電，可採標準線圈，實際一次動作電流由 CT 比決定。
標準線圈遇超過額定的電流即瞬時動作；若並聯時限熔絲（TLF）跨接線圈，可獲反時限特性。TLF 阻抗較低，使大多 CT 電流先流過熔絲，直到熔絲熔斷才使線圈動作。圖 2 示 CT、TLF 與跳脫線圈配置。
TLF 的時間—電流特性類似 HRC，動作時間可低至 10⁻² s，教材稱使用中不劣化。構造與特性由 ESI 12-6 規範。跳脫線圈額定 2 A 或 5 A，2 A 為標準；其阻抗約為 TLF 的 20–90 倍，依額定而異。
TLF 額定有 3、5、7.5、10、12.5、15 A，皆可配 2 A 線圈。
製造商曲線可查不同二次電流下的熔斷時間。若與一次側 HRC 等裝置比較動作時間，須將相應電流乘 CT 比，換至相同側。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p95。


## PDF p96｜TLF 接線與新舊型式限制

### English OCR

The application of overcurrent coils and time limit fuses is for the protection of individual transformers, up to a rating of lOOOkVA(llkV), where such transformers are directly controlled by a circuit breaker. They may also be found providing protection for radial and "open ring" feeders.

The most common form of connection for three phase systems uses only 2 TLF's and gives both three phase inverse overcurrent and instantaneous earth fault protection (see fig 3). An alternative method of connection using 3 TLF's and trip coils to give three phase inverse overcurrent protection only, may be used when earth fault protection is provided by other means.

Three phase over-current and earth fault protection

Fig3

Prior to 1966 trip coils and TLF's were provided by switchgear manufacturers which subsequently had operating characteristics that differed widely. A few of these are still in service and can be quickly recognised by the construction of the TLFwhich consists of a short glass barrel (50mm long 15mm diameter) with metal end caps suitable for clip mounting. The trip coils are normally of 5A, 2.5A or 1.25A rating.

With the older type of trip coils and TLF's it is important to match them correctly as the operating characteristics are only valid when used with the coil with which it was tested (i.e. same maker).

Unlike the most modern TLF(to ESIS12-6) the older type are prone to deterioration in service (they require replacement every 2-3 years) and have limitations in the range of fuses

that can be used with a given coil rating, i.e. Coil rating <TLF rating <2 x Coil rating.

After operations under fault conditions it is important to remember to replace the TLF's before re-closing the circuit breaker.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

過電流線圈加 TLF 可保護由斷路器直接控制、最高約 11 kV／1000 kVA 的單臺變壓器，也可能用於放射式與開環饋線。
常見三相接線只用兩個 TLF，提供三相反時限過電流及瞬時接地故障保護（圖 3）。若另有接地故障保護，可用三個 TLF 與跳脫線圈，只提供三相反時限過電流保護。
1966 年前，各製造商線圈與 TLF 特性差異很大；少數仍在用，常見為長 50 mm、直徑 15 mm 的短玻璃管、兩端金屬帽，可夾裝；線圈通常 5、2.5 或 1.25 A。
舊型必須正確配對，只有與測試時相同製造商／線圈一起使用，特性才有效。相較符合 ESI 12-6 的較新 TLF，舊型會劣化，教材要求每 2–3 年更換，且適用範圍為：線圈額定 < TLF 額定 < 2 × 線圈額定。
故障動作後，重新合上斷路器前必須先更換 TLF。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p96。


## PDF p97｜IDMT 繼電器原理

### English OCR

Inverse Definite Minimum Time (IDMT) Relays

The IDMT relay is widely used for fault detection on distribution systems and equipment. Its basic operating characteristics are.

1. Inverse time-current, (operating time decreases with increase in current),

2. Definite Minimum Time of operation (for all currents in excess of that required to cause magnetic saturation).

The traditional electromagnetic version of the relay operates on the induction principle and consists of a light non-magnetic disc free to rotate between the poles of two electromagnets against the action of a spiral resetting spring. Attached to the spindle supporting the disc is i arm carrying a contact which, when the disc is rotated completes the trip circuit between two fixed contacts.

The fluxes between the upper and lower electro-magnets are sufficiently out of phase with each other to induce eddy currents in the disc and if the fluxes are sufficient, they will cause the disc to rotate. The flux density and speed of the disc are proportional to the primary current through the protection current transformer giving the relay an Inverse Time/Current Characteristic.

2.5 3.75 5 6.25 7.5 8.75 10 A

50 75 100 125 150 175 200%

plug bridge shorted to highest setting when plug is withdrawn

TRIP

COIL

+ - BATTERY

Fig4 SingleElementof an Electromagnetic IDMT relay

### 繁體中文學習譯文

反時限、定最小時間（Inverse Definite Minimum Time，IDMT）繼電器廣用於配電系統與裝置故障偵測。基本特性：1）電流越大，動作時間越短；2）超過磁飽和所需電流後，具有固定最短動作時間。
傳統電磁型依感應原理工作：輕質非磁性圓盤在兩個電磁鐵極間轉動，克服螺旋復歸彈簧。圓盤軸上的接點臂轉到位時，橋接兩固定接點、完成跳脫迴路。
上下電磁鐵磁通有足夠相位差，在圓盤產生渦流；磁通足夠即使圓盤轉動。磁通密度與盤速隨保護 CT 的一次電流增加，形成反時限特性。
圖 4 單元：插頭設定 2.5、3.75、5、6.25、7.5、8.75、10 A，對應 50、75、100、125、150、175、200%；拔出插頭時，插頭橋會短接至最高設定。圖中亦列跳脫線圈及正負極電池。

## PDF p98｜插頭設定與時間倍率 TMS

### English OCR

To enable the relay sensitivity to be adjusted to give the desired primary current setting, the upper winding is provided with tapping's connected to a plug bridge to facilitate adjustment (see Fig 4). The effect of increasing the plug setting is illustrated in Fig 5.

The angle through which the disc has to turn can be varied to suit the operating time required. This adjustment is made by advancing the position of the disc by a time setting dial calibrated in 'time multipliers. The effect of reducing the Time Multiplier Setting is to move the time/current characteristic down the time scale, (see Fig 6).

Time

Fig 7 - Plug setting and time multiplier setting (PBO Relay)

They should be constructed to BS142: Part 2 1990 "Electrical Protective Relays" which specify for the Standard IDMT relay the following:

1. Relay must not operate at its current rating.

2. Accuracy limits from 1.3 to 20 x current rating.

3. Definite minimum time of 2.2 seconds.

The relay is normally used in the three-element form, with the elements connected together to give a number of alternative protective arrangements (see Fig 8).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

為調整靈敏度、取得所需一次動作電流，上線圈設分接頭並連至插頭橋（圖 4）。圖 5 表示提高插頭設定對時間—電流曲線的影響。
圓盤動作前需轉過的角度可調，以配合所需時間；時間旋鈕以時間倍率標定，調整圓盤起始位置。降低時間倍率設定（TMS）會使時間—電流曲線沿時間軸往下移（圖 6）。圖 7 為 PBO 繼電器插頭與時間倍率設定。
教材引用 BS 142:Part 2:1990《電氣保護繼電器》，標準 IDMT 要求：1）在自身額定電流時不得動作；2）準確度範圍為額定電流 1.3–20 倍；3）定最小時間為 2.2 秒。
繼電器通常為三元件，可互連成不同保護配置（圖 8）。

> 2.2 秒等為本教材所述傳統標準特性，不是所有現代 IDMT 曲線或 TMS 下的固定動作時間。

## PDF p100｜高壓保護時間分級與選擇性

### English OCR

Time Grading of HV Protection

Introduction

One requirement for any protection scheme is to ensure the maximum continuity of supply. This is done by determining the location of a fault and disconnecting the minimum amount of apparatus necessary to clear it. When a fault occurs, a number of protective devices may detect it, but only those directly associated with the faulty apparatus are required to operate. This is achieved by discrimination.

Discrimination is defined as:"The quality of a protective device to detect and correctly respond to a faulty condition, so that only the faulty circuit or apparatus isolated."

The two methods of obtaining discrimination in protection systems are as follows:

1. Time grading - where the protective devices are selected/set to operate faster the further they are from the source of supply.

2. Unit protection - where protection is only afforded to apparatus with which the protective device is associated.

Time Grading

The following types of protective device are used in time graded protection systems:

o

1. HRCfuses.

2. ACtrip coils in conjunction with Time Limit Fuses (TLFs).

3. Inverse Definite Minimum Time (IDMT) relays.

In determining suitable time separation between the operating characteristics of devices in series,the following factors are considered (see Fig1).

1. Fuse manufacturing tolerances.

2. Current Transformer (CT) manufacturing tolerances

3. Relay manufacturing tolerances.

4. Circuit Breaker opening time.

5. Relay overshoot (e.g. due to relay disc inertia).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

保護系統應盡量維持供電連續性，定位故障並只斷開清除故障所需的最少裝置。多個保護偵測到同一故障時，只有直接相關者應動作，藉選擇性（discrimination）達成。
選擇性定義：保護裝置偵測並正確回應故障，使只有故障迴路或裝置被隔離的能力。
兩種方法：1）時間分級，離電源越遠的保護越快動作；2）單元保護，只保護與該裝置相關的裝置範圍。
時間分級可使用 HRC 熔絲、交流跳脫線圈搭配 TLF、IDMT 繼電器。
串聯裝置所需時間差應考慮：熔絲製造公差、CT 製造公差、繼電器製造公差、斷路器開路時間，以及繼電器過衝（例如圓盤慣性）。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p100。

## PDF p102｜建議分級間隔與 I²t 比較

### English — OCR / scan transcription

In order to ensure discrimination, the following minimum time intervals have been suggested.

1. Relay in series with a relay

2. Relay in series with an AC trip coil shunted by a time, limit fuse (old glass type)

3. Relay in series with an AC trip coil shunted by a time, limit fuse (new cartridge type to ESI 12-6)

4. Relay in series with an HRC fuses

5. AC trip coil with TLF in series with an HRC fuse

0.4 secs

0.6 secs

0.4 secs

0.2 secs

0.2 secs

These time separations are required between the operating curves of each device over the complete range of fault currents they are likely to experience. Discrimination between HRC fuses in series is achieved by comparing I²t values of "let through" energy.

i.e., Total operating I²t of smaller fuse < Pre arcing I²t of larger fuse

This information is obtained from manufacturers published data.

AC trip coils shunted by TLF's are not generally installed in series due to difficulties in obtaining discrimination at high values of fault current.

Grading Charts

It is necessary to compare the operating characteristics of each device located on a fault path. This is done by plotting the operating characteristics of each device on a Grading Chart. Discrimination can then be checked by reference to the time separation between characteristics for all values of fault current likely to be experienced by that installation, and the minimum time interval criteria.

The selection of protective equipment and settings is initially carried out by a design engineer. A copy of the Grading Chart should be provided for the Local Works Office. The subsequent maintenance of the Grading Chart is the responsibility of the Grading Engineer who will periodically check the time grading. Any queries regarding the Grading Chart for a particular installation should be referred to the Grading Engineer.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材建議最低時間間隔：
1. 繼電器與繼電器串聯：0.4 s。
2. 繼電器與交流線圈＋舊玻璃 TLF 串聯：0.6 s。
3. 繼電器與交流線圈＋符合 ESI 12-6 的新筒式 TLF 串聯：0.4 s。
4. 繼電器與 HRC 串聯：0.2 s。
5. 交流線圈＋TLF 與 HRC 串聯：0.2 s。
這些時間差應在可能遇到的整個故障電流範圍內、兩裝置曲線之間維持。
串聯 HRC 的選擇性，以透過能量 I²t 比較：較小熔絲總動作 I²t < 較大熔絲預弧 I²t，資料取自製造商。
TLF 並聯跳脫線圈的裝置通常不串聯，因高故障電流時難達選擇性。
分級圖：將故障路徑每個裝置的特性畫在同一圖，對所有可能故障電流，檢查曲線間隔是否滿足最低要求。設計工程師初選裝置與設定，地方工程辦公室應保留分級圖；分級工程師負責後續維護與定期檢查，有疑問應詢問其本人。

> 已核對原始掃描，原符號為 I²t，標準代號為 ESI 12-6；修正英文 OCR 的 Ft 與 ESIS12-6。分級秒數屬教材例值，不能取代實際保護協調研究。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p102。


## PDF p103｜Buchholz 氣體動作繼電器

### English OCR

Gas Actuated (BUCHHOLZ) Relay

Introduction

The gas actuated Buchholz relay is used to protect oil filled conservator-type power transformers against low oil levels and internal faults which cause arcing. Visual warning of transformer gassingis given by indicator lights on associated relay panels. The transformer can be disconnected via a tripping relay before a dangerous condition is reached.

Fig 1 A Buchholz Relay

Buchholz relays are generally installed on the larger distribution power transformers (2000 kVA and above) and Generator transformers. Different sizes of relay are available and manufacturers details should be consulted when selecting one.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

Buchholz 用於具有儲油櫃（conservator）的油浸電力變壓器，保護低油位及引起電弧的內部故障。相關繼電器盤指示燈可提供產氣警示，在達危險狀態前由跳脫繼電器斷開變壓器。
圖 1：Buchholz 繼電器。
通常裝於較大型配電變壓器（2000 kVA 以上）與發電機變壓器，有不同尺寸，選型須查製造商資料。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p103。


## PDF p104｜Buchholz 構造與原理

### English OCR

Construction and Principle of Operation

Every type of fault which occurs under the oil in a transformer gives rise to generation of gas which may be slow in the case of minor or incipient faults and violent in the case of heavy faults. This fact is made use of in the Buchholz relay which is inserted in the pipe connection between the transformer tank and conservator (seeFig 2).

CONSERVATOR

nnn TRANSFORMER

1LLO1

Fig2 Buchholz Relay fitted to Transformer

The Buchholz relay comprises of a cast iron housing which contains two elements. A mercury float switch which detects a fall in oil level and a combined hinged flap and mercury float switch mounted so that it will detect any rapid movement of oil from the transformer to the conservator (see Fig3). The relay is normally maintained full of oil by the conservator and the floats, owing to their buoyancy, swing on hinges until they engage a stop.

PET-COCK

MERCURY

SWITCH

DEFLECTOR

PLATE FLOAT

DRAIN PLUG

Fig3 - Sectionof a typical BuchholzRelay

### 繁體中文學習譯文

變壓器油下故障會產氣；輕微或初期故障產氣緩慢，嚴重故障則猛烈。Buchholz 裝在油箱與儲油櫃間的管路（圖 2），利用此現象動作。
鑄鐵外殼內有兩個元件：偵測油位下降的水銀浮子開關，以及結合鉸鏈擋板與水銀浮子開關的元件，可偵測油由主箱快速流向儲油櫃。
正常時由儲油櫃維持繼電器充滿油，浮子受浮力上升至止擋。
圖 2 標籤：儲油櫃、變壓器。圖 3 典型剖面：小閥（petcock）、水銀開關、導流／擋板、浮子、排放塞。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p104。


## PDF p105｜Buchholz 警報、突波與低油位

### English OCR

A slow generation of gas due to a minor fault causesa stream of bubbles which pass upwards towards the conservator, but are trapped in the Buchholz chamber, resulting in a slow fall of the oil level and consequent lowering of the upper float. The build up of gas can be observed through glass windows and the quantity assessed via a calibrated scale. The upper float is provided with a contact which will normally be connected to an auxiliary relay located in an alarm panel and designated "Buchholz Gas" or "Buchholz Alarm".

When an alarm is raised the gas sample should be collected by connection of a bottle with a flexible tube to the petcock provided at the top of the relay. Information regarding the state of the transformer can be obtained from analysis of the gas and the rate at which it is generated.

A heavy fault would give rise to an explosive generation of gas which displaces the oil and ( auses a surge to pass along the pipe towards the conservator and in doing so displaces the flap switch which operates an auxiliary relay designated "Buchholz Surge" or "Buchholz Trip". Under these circumstances the circuit breaker will be tripped.

A leakage of oil from the transformer tank causes a gradual fall in oil level which will in the first place operate the upper float to give an alarm and if not corrected will cause the lower float to fall and trip the circuit breaker.

The relay is also fitted with a lower petcock (seeFig4), which is used for taking oil samples. Both the lower and upper petcocks are also used for testing purposes.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

輕微故障緩慢產氣，氣泡往儲油櫃移動時被繼電器室截留，使油位下降，上浮子隨之降低。可透過玻璃窗觀察並按刻度估氣量；上浮子接點通常連至警報盤的輔助繼電器，標示 Buchholz Gas／Alarm。
警報時，教材描述以軟管與容器連線頂部小閥採氣；氣體分析與產氣速率可提供變壓器狀態資訊。
嚴重故障猛烈產氣、推移油液，造成沿管路向儲油櫃的油流突波；擋板動作，帶動 Buchholz Surge／Trip 輔助繼電器，使斷路器跳脫。
油箱漏油時，油位逐步下降，先使上浮子發警報；未改善則下浮子下降，斷路器跳脫。
圖 4 為正面與側面圖；下方小閥供採油樣，上下小閥也供測試。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p105。


## PDF p106｜Buchholz 安裝與試運轉

### English OCR

Installation

As already discussed,the relay is sited in the oil pipe between the top of the transformer tank and the conservator (seeFig 2). It should always be mounted in a straight run of piping at least five pipe diameters on the transformer side of the relay and a minimum of three diameters on the conservator side of the relay. These straight runs of piping should slope up from the tank to the oil conservator at an angle between 1 and 9 degrees to the horizontal. Care should be taken when mounting that the arrow on the relay is in the same direction as the oil flow to the conservator.

The top petcock on the relay should be below the bottom of the conservator, whilst the distance between the bottom of the device and the transformer tank should be at least 75

mm.
c

Electrical connections are made to a terminal box on the relay. The float switches are already connected internally and brought out to this terminal box.

Commissioning

After installation of the relay, and when the transformer has been filled with oil up into the oil conservator, the air trapped in the relay gas chamber must be allowed to escape via the upper

petcock. Air which has been trapped in the transformer during filling may continue to collect in the relay for some time. This should be released via the upper petcock.

The alarm circuit can be checked by forcing air into the relay via the lower petcock until the oil level falls sufficiently for the top element to drop. The trip circuit can be checked by the sudden application of air or oil to the bottom element via the same petcock. The air collected in the relay must be drawn off via the upper petcock.

The tests discussed are carried out with the use of an air pump which is connected to an air bottle (see Fig5).

### 繁體中文學習譯文

繼電器安裝於變壓器油箱頂部與儲油櫃間的油管。教材要求直管段：變壓器側至少 5 倍管徑，儲油櫃側至少 3 倍；由油箱往儲油櫃向上傾斜，與水平夾角 1–9°。繼電器箭頭應與往儲油櫃的油流同向。
頂部小閥應低於儲油櫃底部；裝置底部距變壓器油箱至少 75 mm。電氣接線接至端子箱，內部浮子開關已引線至此。
試運轉：安裝、注油至儲油櫃後，由上閥排出繼電器氣室困住的空氣。注油時變壓器內殘氣仍可能持續收集，亦應由上閥排出。
教材以從下閥注氣降低油位，使上元件下落檢查警報；突然對下元件施加空氣或油以檢查跳脫。完成後由上閥排氣。測試使用氣泵連線氣瓶（圖 5）。

> 尺寸、傾角及試驗動作按教材翻譯；實際安裝／試驗須使用相應繼電器製造商程式。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p106。


## PDF p107｜Buchholz 測試氣瓶與動作後分析

### English OCR

CONSERVATOR

BUCHHHOLZ RELAY

AR BOTTLE

AR PUMP

Fig5 - Connectionof Air Bottle for Testinga BuchholzRelay

An air pump is connected to the inlet valve of the air bottle, and the flexible hose from the outlet valve on the bottle to the lower petcock on the relay. The bottle can now be charged to a pressure of 5 to 10 psi by means of the air pump. Opening the lower petcock will allow air to enter the relays gas chamber and result in operation of the upper float.

To test for operation of the lower float due to an oil surge, the bottle must be charged to a much higher pressure than before and the relay lower petcock suddenly opened wide.

Test air bottles are available from the manufacturers. The manufacturers operating instructions should be followed when carrying out the tests.

Post Operation Tests

In the main the relay will operate for one of the following three reasons.

2. Air inadvertently introduced during filling or because of mechanical failure of the oil system.

3. Gas produced by the breakdown of oil.

4. Gas produced by the breakdown of solid insulation.

The gases produced are hydrogen, acetylene and carbon monoxide and it is the proportions of these which point to the type of fault producing the gas.The information that can be derived from analysis is as follows.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

圖 5：氣泵接氣瓶入口，氣瓶出口以軟管接繼電器下方小閥。教材描述氣瓶先加壓至 5–10 psi，開下閥使氣體進入並操作上浮子。要模擬油流突波使下元件動作，則需更高氣壓並突然全開下閥。
測試氣瓶可向製造商取得，試驗應依製造商操作說明。
動作後檢查：教材列三項主要原因，但編號印為 2、3、4：
2. 注油時意外引入空氣，或油系統機械故障。
3. 油分解產氣。
4. 固體絕緣分解產氣。
氣體包括氫、乙炔、一氧化碳；各比例指向可能的故障種類，下頁續述。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p107。


## PDF p108｜Buchholz 氣體判讀與維護（教材原述）

### English OCR

1. If the gas is mainly hydrogen with less than 2% carbon monoxide then the fault is likely to involve only the insulating oil.

2. If the gas is hydrogen with about 20%carbon monoxide then the fault is concerned with both solid insulation and insulating oil.

In the first case the transformer can be left in service providing the accumulation of gas is slow. If successive alarms occur within a week then it should be taken out of service and examined. In the second case where the burning of solid insulation is involved the transformer should be taken out of service irrespective of the time taken for the accumulation of gas.

The following field tests may also give a useful indication:

o

1. Check for flammability - A small quantity of the gas evolved should be tried against a naked flame to see if the gas will ignite.

2. Check for Acetylene - By means of a rubber tube connected to the pet-cock pass some of the gas evolved through a solution of silver nitrate. If acetylene is present, this will cause clouding of the liquid, subsequently turning the liquid brown when exposed to sunlight.

If both of these tests are positive, then without doubt it can be concluded that the gas evolved is due to a fault, if both tests are negative, then it is probably due to small bubbles of air being released from the oil or windings.

Maintenance

The relay should be inspected annually, and any accumulated gas released from the Buchholz float chamber (testing if suspect). The manufacturers also recommend that the contacts and alarms are checked. In order to avoid tripping out the transformer, it is desirable to disconnect the tripping connections from the relays before performing these tests, and to substitute an auxiliary operation indicator. Tripping tests can be carried out during switchgear maintenance to minimise disruption.

Specialprecautions are required when transformer oil has been replaced, or is being circulated for cleaning, because of trapped air bubbles which can displace the floats.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材判讀：1）主要為氫、一氧化碳低於 2%，故障可能只涉及油；2）氫加約 20% 一氧化碳，可能同時涉及固體絕緣與油。
原文稱第一類若積氣緩慢可暫留運轉；一週內連續警報則應停用檢查。第二類涉及固體絕緣燃燒，不論積氣所需時間都應停用。
教材列現場試驗：1）少量氣體靠近裸火觀察可燃性；2）以軟管導氣透過硝酸銀溶液，若有乙炔會變混濁、日照後轉褐。原文稱兩試驗皆陽性可判為故障產氣，皆陰性可能是油／繞組釋出小氣泡。
維護：每年檢查並排出累積氣體，疑似異常則檢測；製造商也建議檢查接點與警報。教材描述為避免誤跳脫，試驗前斷開跳脫接線、代以輔助動作指示器；真正跳脫測試可配合開關裝置維護以減少中斷。
換油或迴圈清洗時須特別防範困住的氣泡使浮子動作。

> 保留原教材的歷史氣體測試與保護旁路敘述，不視為已驗證診斷法或現場操作指令。裸火、硝酸銀與跳脫迴路處置均不可依此譯文直接實作。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p108。


## PDF p109｜注油後積氣與警報敘述

### English OCR

The transformer should be filled with oil as long as possible before putting into service. This will allow most of the air, trapped in the windings, to escape, and the remainder will be released when the transformer is put on load and warms up. To stop continuous alarms the upper float switch is often disconnected for the first three to four weeks of service and during this period regular inspections of the relay sight glass should be made, and any excess air released.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材原述：變壓器應在投入運轉前盡早完成注油，讓繞組中大部分空氣先逸出，剩餘空氣會在帶載升溫後釋放。
為避免持續警報，原文稱開始運轉前 3–4 週常將上浮子開關斷開，期間定期檢查觀察窗並排出過量空氣。

> 此頁是教材對暫停警報的歷史敘述之翻譯，並未授權停用任何保護／警報。是否允許及所需補償措施必須由適用程式與負責人確認。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p109。


## PDF p110｜高壓保護系統與裝置對照表

### English — OCR / scan transcription

Table of High-voltage Protection Systems and Device

Protective Device | Known As | Common Uses

High Rupturing Capacity Fuse | HRC | Installed in Fuse Switches or Switch fuses for Transformers up to 800 KVA.

Time Limit Fuse & AC Trip Coils | TLF | Used with Circuit Breakers for: (i) Transformers up to 1000 KVA; (ii) Open ring feeders and radials.

Inverse Definite Minimum Time Relay | IDMT | Used with Circuit Breakers for: (i) Transformers; (ii) Open ring feeders and radials; (iii) Incoming supply.

Unit Protection: Balanced Voltage / Circulating Current | TRANSLAY; SOLKOR A & B; SOLKOR R & Rf | Used with Circuit Breakers for: (i) Closed ring feeders; (ii) Interconnectors.

Thermal Overcurrent Relay | THOCR | Use with Circuit Breakers for: (i) Alternator over current protection; (ii) Alternator short circuit protection.

Earth Fault Relays | Used with Circuit Breakers for earth fault protection when providing supplies from generators.
Neutral Current Relay | NCR | Unrestricted earth fault protection.
Balanced Current Relay | BCR | Restricted earth fault protection.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

表格：保護裝置｜通稱｜常見用途。
• 高遮斷容量熔絲｜HRC｜裝在 fuse switch／switch fuse，保護最高 800 kVA 變壓器。
• 時限熔絲＋交流跳脫線圈｜TLF｜搭配斷路器，保護最高 1000 kVA 變壓器，以及開環／放射式饋線。
• 反時限、定最小時間繼電器｜IDMT｜搭配斷路器，保護變壓器、開環／放射式饋線及進線。
• 單元保護：平衡電壓／迴圈電流｜TRANSLAY、SOLKOR A & B、SOLKOR R & Rf｜搭配斷路器，用於閉環饋線及互連線。
• 熱過電流繼電器｜THOCR｜搭配斷路器，提供交流發電機過電流與短路保護。
• 接地故障繼電器：中性線電流繼電器 NCR、平衡電流繼電器 BCR｜發電機供電時配合斷路器作接地故障保護；NCR 為非限制接地故障保護，BCR 為限制接地故障保護。
本頁是橫向表，分類與合併欄位請配合原掃描。

## PDF p111｜Develop 安全規則與 AE 資格

### English OCR

Develop High-voltage Safety Rules and Procedures

Introduction

Develop High-voltage Safety Rules and Procedures are written to "establish a general system of safe working practice" they also ensure the company complies with the current legislation listed below:

1. Health and Safety at Work Act 1974

2. Electricity at Work Regulations 1989

3. Electricity Safety, Quality and Continuity Regulations 2002.

,The following paragraphs are extracts taken from the Develop High-voltage Safety Rules and procedures for further information please refer to the original document.

Definition of Persons

Authorising Engineer (AE)

An Engineer is appointed in writing by senior management to take responsibility for the effective management of these Rules.

Qualifications:

( .) Chartered Engineer with a minimum of five years relevant experience or an Incorporated Engineer with a sound technical engineering background who is qualified to HNC level and has a minimum of eight years relevant experience as a practicing Senior Authorised Person (Electrical).

b) have satisfactorily completed an approved Senior Authorised/ Authorised Persons initial training course in accordance with Appendix A6 within the last three years

c) have satisfactorily completed an approved Authorising Engineer training course in accordance with Appendix A6 within the last three years

d) be familiar with the different types of equipment, installations, and systems in use within the area for which appointment is sought.

e) have a basic knowledge of the systems and installations in use in the area for which they will become responsible and become familiar with the more complex systems.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

Develop 高壓安全規則與程式旨在建立一般安全工作制度，並使公司符合教材列出的 HASAWA 1974、Electricity at Work Regulations 1989、ESQCR 2002。以下為摘錄，詳情須查原規則檔案。
授權工程師（Authorising Engineer，AE）：由高階管理階層書面任命，負責有效管理這套規則的工程師。
資格：a）特許工程師且至少 5 年相關經驗；或具紮實技術背景、HNC 程度、至少 8 年電氣 SAP 實務經驗的 Incorporated Engineer。b）過去 3 年內按附錄 A6 完成核準 SAP／AP 初訓。c）過去 3 年內按附錄 A6 完成核準 AE 訓練。d）熟悉欲受任區域的各類裝置、裝置與系統。e）對將負責區域的系統／裝置有基本知識，並逐步熟悉更複雜系統。

> 此為 Develop 教材所屬規則的職務資格，不直接等同其他公司、專案或 RelyOn 當班報名資格。

1 that are applicable to the systems and installations for which the appointment is sought.

b) Issue, cancel and withdraw of a Limitation of Access Safety Document for the systems, installations, and equipment for which the Authorised Person (HV) has been appointed

Competent Person (CPHV)

A suitably trained person who has sufficient technical knowledge and experience to avoid any danger that electricity may create, has sufficient knowledge of these rules and is suitable to carry out specific work activities on the types of installations, equipment and locations indicated on their Safety Document or Certificate of Appointment.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

AP：依規則由 AE 書面任命，負責指定高壓系統控制與操作之規則執行。
資格：a）超過 23 歲；b）充分了解規則與附錄 1 適用法規；c）具安全操作受任高壓開關裝置及控制系統／裝置的技術能力與資格；d）熟悉受任裝置／系統；e）完成 AE 核准的 AP 高壓初訓；f）任命前經 AE 正式面談展現能力與適任性；g）具足夠急救知識，並在過去 3 年內完成符合 7.3 的緊急急救訓練。
AP 責任：控制高壓配電系統並操作高壓開關裝置；對受任範圍發出、取消及撤回 LOA（進入限制）安全檔案。
高壓具能力人員（CPHV）：經適當訓練，具足夠技術知識與經驗避免電氣危險，瞭解本規則，適合在安全檔案或任命證書列明的裝置、裝置及地點執行特定工作。

> AP 只發出 LOA 是這份 Develop 規則的職權描述，不能套用為所有公司的 AP／SAP 通用許可權。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p114。


## PDF p115｜安全監護人與三種安全檔案

### English OCR

SafetyPerson

A person not involved in the work or test, who has received Emergency First Aid training and who has adequate knowledge, experience, and ability to avoid danger, keep watch, prevent interruption, apply First Aid, and summon help. The person is to be familiar with the system or installation being worked on or tested and is to have been instructed on the action to be taken to disconnect the equipment in the event of an accident.

The Safety Person is required to be in attendance when the Duty Senior Authorised Person considers it necessary and in the following circumstances:

a) Whilst equipment is being proved or confirmed dead.

b) Whilst equipment is being earthed, other than by means of a switch.

c) Whilst the Senior Authorised Person is spiking a cable. ( ') Whilst testing is being undertaken at High-voltage.

e) Whilst a High-voltage potential indicator is in use.

f) Whilst voltage and phasingtests are being undertaken at High-voltage.

g) Whilst any person is opening or working in a High-voltage Enclosure.

Safety Documents

Permit to Work

A written authority, issued by the Duty Senior Authorised Person to a Competent Person for work to be undertaken on defined HVelectrical equipment, that has been made safe.

Sanctionfor Test

A written authority issued by the Duty Senior Authorised Person for testing to be undertaken on defined HVelectrical equipment.

Limitationof Access

A written authority, issued by the Duty Senior Authorised or Authorised Person allowing a named person to enter and carry out specified tasks (which do not require the issue of a Permit to Work or Sanction for Test) in an HVSubstation or any other location which is under the control of the an Authorised Person.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

安全監護人（Safety Person）：不參與工作或測試，已受緊急急救訓練，並有足夠知識、經驗及能力避免危險、監看、防止幹擾、施急救及求援；須熟悉被作業系統，並已被告知事故時如何斷開裝置。
當值 SAP 判斷需要時，以及以下情況，監護人須在場：a）驗明／確認裝置無電；b）用開關以外方法接地；c）SAP 刺穿電纜；d）進行高壓測試；e）使用高壓驗電器；f）高壓電壓及對相測試；g）任何人開啟或進入高壓圍護空間工作。
安全檔案：
工作許可 PTW：由當值 SAP 發給 CP 的書面授權，允許對已使安全的指定高壓裝置工作。
測試許可 SFT：由當值 SAP 發出的書面授權，允許測試指定高壓裝置。
進入限制 LOA：由當值 SAP 或 AP 發出，允許具名人員進入高壓變電站或 AP 控制的其他地點，執行不需 PTW／SFT 的指定任務。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p115。


## PDF p116｜I&E、操作程式表與 Logbook

### English OCR

IsolationandEarthingDiagram

A diagram attached to the Permit to Work or Sanction for Test illustrating the safety measures taken.

SwitchingSchedule

A document issued by the Duty Senior Authorised Person,after countersignature, setting out the sequence of operations to be followed before a Permit to Work or a Sanction for Test is issued.

Logbook

For each geographical area for which Senior Authorised/Authorised Persons have been appointed a bound hard covered book (not loose leaf) with sequentially numbered pages and titled "Logbook" is to be prepared. The book is to be clearly and indelibly marked with the name of the Site, the location and the system or installation to which it refers and is to() be kept in the Lockable Document Cabinet when not in use.

Logbook entries are to be made in chronological order; each entry being ruled off with a horizontal line across the page. Entries are to show:

a) The acceptance and relinquishing of responsibility between Senior Authorised / Authorised Persons.

b) The removal returns and the transfer of the Authorised Person'sKey from the Authorised Person'sKeyBox.

c) Each individual operation of High-voltage switchgear and of low voltage distribution switchgear up to and including the Main LVIntake Switches.

d) Adjustment of the Mimic Diagram to indicate the present state of the system or installation.

e) The issue and return of any key for HVequipment, ie, switchgear, sub-stations, transformers etc.

f) The issue, cancellation, loss,or withdrawal of a Permit to Work, Sanction for Test, or Limitation of Access.

g) Spiking of a cable

h) The receipt, termination and remedial action associated with an Operational Restriction.

i) The withdrawal or replacement of the Authorised Person'sDuplicate Key, and of any other duplicate keys.

j) The 3-monthly inspection of HVswitch rooms and Substations.

k) The annual inspection of protective equipment, test equipment and the six-monthly inspections off portable earthing equipment.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

隔離與接地圖（I&E）：附於 PTW／SFT，說明已採安全措施的圖。
切換操作程式表（Switching schedule）：經副簽後由當值 SAP 發出，列明在 PTW／SFT 發出前須依循的操作順序。
操作紀錄簿（Logbook）：每一受任地理區域須有裝訂硬皮、非活頁、連續編頁的紀錄簿，永久清楚標示場址、位置、系統／裝置；不使用時放可上鎖檔案櫃。
按時間先後記錄，每筆以橫線劃開，內容包括：
a）SAP／AP 間責任接受與交還；b）AP 鑰匙自鑰匙箱取出、歸還及轉交；c）每次高壓及至主要低壓進線開關為止的低壓配電操作；d）模擬圖更新至目前狀態；e）高壓裝置、站房、變壓器等鑰匙發出與歸還；f）PTW／SFT／LOA 發出、取消、遺失或撤回；g）電纜刺穿；h）操作限制的收到、終止及補救；i）AP 備用鑰匙及其他備匙取用或補回；j）每 3 個月高壓開關室／變電站檢查；k）每年防護與測試裝置檢查，及每 6 個月可攜接地裝置檢查。

## PDF p117｜操作程式手冊與檔案儲存

### English OCR

Completed logbooks are to be retained in the lockable document cabinet for a period of three years after the date of the last entry.

Operational Procedure Manual

For each geographical area for which Senior Authorised Persons have been appointed, a ring binder file entitled "Operational Procedure Manual" is to be prepared. The binder is to be clearly and indelibly marked with the name of the site, location, system, or installation to which it refers and is to be kept in the 'Lockable Document Cabinet' when not in use.

The Manual is to contain, in separate sections, a copy of each of the following:

a) Certificate of Appointment issued to a Competent Person or for Contractor's Competent Person, a letter of competency from the contractor's employer.

j) A register of Competent Persons including details and dates of training, issue dates and review dates of certificates, etc.

c) Operational Restriction received.

d) Inspection report and details of any remedial work undertaken in connection with an Operational Restriction.

e) Cancelled Operational Restriction.

f) Demarcation agreement with other organisations.

g) Demarcation agreement with contractors.

h) Any operational agreements with a Distribution Network Operator.

i) The original copy of every approved and completed Switching Schedule, and Isolation and Earthing Diagram including any completed and subsequently not used.

t <) Details of protective equipment, test equipment and portable earthing equipment kept

within the establishment, including specifications, operators or users' instructions, maintenance instructions and, where appropriate, calibration records.

Each document added to a section of the manual is to be sequentially numbered

Operating and Maintenance Manuals

For each geographical area for which Senior Authorised Persons have been appointed, one or more ring binder files entitled "Operating and Maintenance Manuals" is to be prepared. The binder is to be clearly and indelibly marked with the name of the site, location, system or installation to which it refers and kept in the lockable document cabinet when not in use.

用完的 Logbook 自最後記錄日起在上鎖檔案櫃儲存 3 年。

h）與 DNO 的操作協議。
i）每份經核准、已完成的 Switching schedule 及 I&E 正本，包括完成後未實際使用者。

操作及維護手冊：每個受任區域建立一個或多個同樣永久標示的活頁夾，不用時放上鎖檔案櫃；內容接下頁。

## PDF p119｜Switching 規劃與操作紀律

### English OCR

Switching Schedules and Safety Documentation

Discipline

HVswitching for maintenance/installation work and for fault sectionalising requires planning and discipline. When you undertake any HVswitching make yourself fully aware of what needs to be done. Prepare your schedule and have it checked by another Authorised Person.Study your system schematic carefully and ensure that you understand how the system is normally operated. Be fully aware of any Operational restrictions that may be associated with the switchgear that you will be operating. Remember that operational restrictions are there because of incidents that may have injured (possibly fatally) other Authorised Persons.

discipline yourself and check, double check and check again. Have you isolated and fitted safety locks and caution signs where appropriate? Have you operated the correct switch or circuit breaker? Are you even in the right substation? These are some of the questions that you need to be constantly askingyourself, it is very easy to make an error which in the worst instance may prove fatal.

After you have completed your HVswitching operations it is important to update your diagrams or mimic when practicably possible to ensure that subsequent switching errors do not occur.

afety Switching Schedules

SafetySwitching Schedules form the basis from which the relevant safety documentation will be issued and, as an Authorised Person issuing documentation, you are legally responsible for ensuring that safety documentation is correct and that safety procedures are adhered to. The procedures are there for your safety as well as the safety of others.

The main documents applicable to the model Safety Rules are:

1. Limitation of Access

2. Sanction for Test

3. Permit to Work.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

為維護／安裝或故障分段進行高壓切換，都需要規劃與紀律。開始前充分了解要做什麼，準備操作程式表並請另一位授權人員核查；仔細研究系統圖，瞭解正常運轉方式，並確認相關操作限制。這些限制可能源自曾使其他 AP 受傷甚至死亡的事故。
保持紀律，檢查、複查、再檢查：是否已隔離，並在適當處加安全鎖與警示牌？操作的是正確開關／斷路器嗎？是否在正確變電站？必須持續自問，因錯誤可能致命。
完成切換後，應在實際可行時儘速更新圖面／模擬圖，避免後續操作錯誤。
安全操作程式表是發出相關安全檔案的基礎。教材表示發出檔案的授權人員有責任確保檔案正確、遵守程式，這些程式保障自己與他人。
本套示範規則主要檔案：LOA、SFT、PTW。

> 本頁泛稱由另一位 AP 核查；PDF p124 的 HV1 表明列另一位 SAP 副簽。保留教材用字差異，正式責任依適用規則釐清。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p119。


## PDF p120｜LOA 與 SFT 的用途

### English OCR

1. Limitationof Access A Limitation of Access is a safety document that is judgemental. It is issued when the Authorised Person believes verbal instructions are insufficient. The most common instances of issue would be when nonelectrical personnel require access to a substation to undertake work not associated with the electrical system, for example, painting the walls in a substation or taking meter readings. Of course, electrical personnel may also be issued with a Limitation of Access, possibly to install a 13A socket, telephone socket, or some task similar.

Maintenance being undertaken on a withdrawable truck type circuit breaker would also come under the Limitation of Access document. This is because the truck has been removed from the system, discharged to earth and the bus bar and circuit shutters have been locked shut, therefore, no access to the HVsystem is available.

o

If the Authorised Person is going to be present for the duration of the work then they may not feel that a Limitation of Access is necessary,it is the responsibility of the Authorised Person to determine this.

2. Sanctionfor Test A Sanction for Test is a safety document that would be issued if certain tests need to be undertaken on the HVsystem. These tests include continuity testing, cable identification and pressure testing. This document can only be issued after isolation and earthing of part of the HVsystem, ensuring that safety locks and signs have been positioned at the appropriate points. This document allows the restricted removal and application of earths on the isolated section.

It may be found in certain circumstances that a Sanction for Test is used 1) before a Permit to work is issued and 2) after a Permit to Work has been cancelled. For example, a cable may need identifying before work is undertaken and testing after the work has been completed.

ASanction for Test is normally issued by a Senior Authorised Person to a Competent Person. This may allow the use of contractors' Competent Persons to undertake the testing. However, it will be the responsibility of the Authorised Person to ascertain the competency of the Competent Person.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

LOA：屬需運用判斷的安全檔案，AP 認為口頭指示不足時發出。常見於非電氣人員進站執行與電力系統無關的任務，例如刷牆或讀表；也可給電氣人員安裝 13 A 插座、電話插座等。
可抽出臺車式斷路器維護也可屬 LOA，前提是臺車已自系統移出、對地放電，匯流排與迴路活門已鎖閉，無法接近高壓系統。若 AP 全程在場，可能判斷不需 LOA，由 AP 負責決定。
SFT：用於高壓系統特定測試，如導通、電纜識別及耐壓。須先隔離與接地，在適當位置加安全鎖及標牌，才能發出；允許在隔離區段受限制地移除與重新施加接地。
可能在 PTW 前及 PTW 取消後使用，例如工作前識別電纜、完工後測試。通常由 SAP 發給 CP，可由承攬商 CP 執行；授權人員須查明 CP 的能力。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p120。


## PDF p121｜PTW 的範圍、責任與接地

### English OCR

3. Permitto Work A Permit to Work is a safety document that would be issued if work is to be undertaken on the HVsystem. The definition of 'work' as against operations is open to interpretation but if the task to be undertaken involves touching the HVsystem without the use of insulated tools or PPEthen a Permit to Work would be required. To issue a Permit to Work the HV system in question must be isolated and earthed with safety locks and signs fitted at the appropriate points. The earths cannot be removed whilst a Permit to Work is in force. The most obvious instances of issue would be when maintenance work was being undertaken on a RingMain Unit or HVSwitchboard, or when jointing on an HVcable.

As the Senior Authorised Person, you are responsible for the section of isolated and earthed network and all the personnel working on it during that time.

( he Permit to Work must state clearly the name and location of the Senior Authorised Person issuingthe work, the name of the Competent Person doing the work, the extent of the work permitted, location of all isolation and earthing points, location of all safety locks and signs,methods of rendering automatic fire extinguisher systems inoperative, etc. and any relationship between the HVpermit and other safety permits in use at the same location.

The Permit to Work is issued to a Competent Person and it is the responsibility of the Authorised Person to ensure that the Competent Person fully understands the work that is to be undertaken and that the working area is clearly defined both electrically and geographically.

i he Competent Person then accepts responsibility for the work to be undertaken and must ensure that they are present for the duration of the work. The Competent Person may apply additional flexible earths at the point of work, but these are applied after the Competent Person has accepted the Permit to Work and must therefore be removed before the clearing of the Permit to Work by the Competent Person.

General

When a Sanction for Test is cancelled the position for the isolation and earthing switches is the same as at the issue of the Sanction for Test.

When a Permit to Work is cancelled the position for the isolation and earthing switches is the same as at the issue of the Permit to Work.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

PTW 用於高壓系統工作。教材指出 work 與 operation 的界線可有解釋空間，但若任務涉及不藉絕緣工具或 PPE 觸及高壓系統，就需要 PTW。發出前，該系統必須隔離、接地，並在適當處設安全鎖與標牌。PTW 有效期間不得移除其中的接地。常見例是 RMU／配電盤維護與高壓電纜接續。
SAP 對該隔離接地網段及其工作人員負責。PTW 應清楚列發出 SAP 的姓名與位置、工作 CP、允許範圍、所有隔離／接地點、鎖與牌位置、使自動滅火失效的方法，以及與同地點其他許可的關係。
AP 須確保接收 CP 完全理解工作，且電氣與地理工作界線清楚。CP 接受工作責任後，工作全程須在場。
CP 可在工作點加可攜式接地，但須在接收 PTW 後施加，並在簽署 clearance（工作清離）前移除。
一般原則：SFT 取消時，隔離與接地開關位置應與發出時相同；PTW 取消亦同。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p121。


## PDF p122｜驗電、對相與接地補充原則

### English OCR

For live HVphasing tests a document is not required.

For dead HV phasingtests a document is required (Sanction for Test). An isolated area is live until proved dead.

Safety Locks and Caution Signs are fitted at points of isolation.

Safety Locks are fitted at points of earthing (these positions are known as Circuit Main Earths).

Additional flexible earths may be fitted by the Competent Person after the receipt of a Permit to Work but must be removed prior to the Competent Person clearing the Permit to

Work.
o

Guidance on the rules

On the next page there is guidance in the form of the golden rules of HV working. Following on from that there is table HV1 from the safety rules which are all based upon the guidance

in HSG85.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

教材列：
• 高壓帶電對相不要求安全檔案。
• 高壓無電對相需要 SFT。
• 已隔離區段在驗明無電前仍按帶電處理。
• 隔離點裝安全鎖與警示牌。
• 接地點裝安全鎖，這些位置稱主迴路接地（CME）。
• CP 可在接收 PTW 後加附加可攜接地，但在清離 PTW 前必須移除。
下頁提供高壓作業黃金法則，再接規則的 HV1 表；教材表示均以 HSG85 指引為基礎。

> 對相檔案要求屬本教材規則，不能直接套用其他公司的規程；『不要求檔案』也不等於無需授權或安全措施。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p122。


## PDF p123｜高壓作業黃金法則

### English OCR

The Golden Rules of HV Working

1. Plan Work

• Prepare Switching Schedule and Isolation &

Earthing Diagram

• Obtain counter signature(s).

2. Isolate

• From all sources of supply

• Fix Safety lock and Caution sign

• Fit Danger signs if required to any adjacent live equipment.

3. Prove Dead

• Using HV potential indicator at all accessible places

• When proving dead a safety person will be required.

4. Earthing

• Switchgear to be moved to its earthing position

• 2nd Safety Lock to be fitted

• Spike cable if unable to identify with certainty.

5. Issue of Permit

• Issue the Permit to Work (PTW)

• Issue key to safety key box/lockbox to competent person / Person in charge (PIC).

AS A

### 繁體中文學習譯文

1. 規劃工作：準備 Switching schedule 及 I&E；取得副簽。
2. 隔離：與所有供電來源隔離；加安全鎖與警示牌；必要時在鄰近帶電裝置加危險牌。
3. 驗明無電：在所有可接近位置使用高壓驗電器；須有安全監護人。
4. 接地：開關裝置切至接地位置；加第二把安全鎖；若不能確切識別電纜，進行電纜刺穿。
5. 發許可：發出 PTW；將安全鑰匙箱／鎖箱鑰匙交給 CP／PIC（負責人）。
此為教材五步驟圖，非具體裝置操作程式。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p123。


## PDF p124｜HV1：SAP 程式表

### English OCR

Table HV1- Senior Authorised Person Procedures

The following table details the procedures to be carried out by a Senior Authorised Person to enable work on High-voltage Equipment. Steps in column 1 are to be undertaken in numerical order.

The Senior Authorised Person is responsible for all tasks.

Column 1 Column 2

Step 1 - Prepare a Switching

Schedule

Prepare a Switching Schedule and Isolation and Earthing Diagram in duplicate and obtain a countersignature form another Senior Authorised Person.

Sign on as Duty Senior Authorised Person, place notices in mimic, check mimic diagram before proceeding to step 2.

Step 2 - Isolate and Fix Signs

Isolate from all sources of supply.

Prevent unauthorised connection by fixing Safety Locks and Cautions Signs at points of isolation.

Fix Danger Signs on live equipment adjacent to the Point of

Work.

Step 3 - Prove Dead

Prove dead, with a high-voltage potential indicator, at all accessible points of isolation (where appropriate, prove dead on the Low Voltage side of A transformer, i.e., LVFeeder Pillars, LV Distribution Boards etc).

Step 4- Earth

Earth conductors at all points of Isolation and prevent unauthorised disconnection by fixing Safety Locks.

Identify with certainty or spike underground cables at the point/s of work if the conductors are to be cut or exposed.

Earth overhead lines near the working places.

Step 5 - Issue the Permit to Work

The Competent Person is to be shown the Isolation and Earthing Diagram and the safety arrangements at all the point/s of isolation and at the Point/s of the Work.

Issue the Permit to Work, Isolation and Earthing Diagram and the Competent Person's key to the Safety KeyBox to the Competent Person.

Step 6 - Undertake the Work

The Competent Person is to undertake or directly supervise the work and, on completion, or when the work is stopped and made safe, is to return the original of the Permit to Work, Isolation and Earthing Diagram and Competent Person's Key to the Safety Key Box to the Duty Senior Authorised Person and complete Part 3 of the Permit retained in the pad.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

本表說明 SAP 為使高壓裝置可供工作而執行的程式。第 1 欄步驟須按數字順序，SAP 對全部任務負責。
步驟 1：準備兩份 Switching schedule 與 I&E，由另一位 SAP 副簽；簽入擔任當值 SAP，在模擬圖放通知牌，確認圖況後再進入步驟 2。
步驟 2：隔離全部供電來源，在隔離點加安全鎖及警示牌，防止未授權接通；在工作點鄰近帶電裝置設危險牌。
步驟 3：以高壓驗電器在所有可接近隔離點驗明無電；適當時在變壓器低壓側驗電，例如低壓饋線箱或配電盤。
步驟 4：在各隔離點將導體接地並加安全鎖，防止未授權拆除；若要切斷／露出地下電纜導體，在工作點確切識別或刺穿；架空線在工作位置附近接地。
步驟 5：向 CP 展示 I&E、各隔離點及工作點安全措施，再交付 PTW、I&E 及 CP 的安全鑰匙箱鑰匙。
步驟 6：CP 執行或直接監督工作；完成，或停止並使安全後，把 PTW 正本、I&E 及鑰匙交回當值 SAP，並在許可簿保留的檔案完成第 3 部分。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p124。


## PDF p125｜電纜定位：資料與接收器

### English OCR

Location, Identification, and Spiking of Cables

Cable Location

During the initial stages of cable location, it is important to collect as much information as possible prior to physically undertaking the work. Studying cable records, which should include joint positions, is essential.

Remember - cable records, are only a guide and may be inaccurate.

Procedure for Cable Location

1. Familiarise yourself with the cable location equipment.

2. Confirm cable location equipment is calibrated.

3. Confirm batteries are still operational and that equipment is in working order.

4. To determine if there are cables in a specific area, prior to excavation work, it is only necessary to use the receiver and to make sweeps over the area in question. Switch the receiver to 'power' mode and the receiver will pick up mains hum from power cables.

Operating receiver Sweep Pattern

Your middle or little finger should be used to squeeze, and hold on, the on/off trigger. Your index finger will then be free to adjust the Frequency / Sensitivity Control.

Some cable location receivers will only pick up mains hum from cables that are carrying load, they will not pick up mains hum from energised cables on no-load (streetlights, etc) or cables that are balanced across all 3 phases.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

定位初期，在實際工作前應盡量收集資料，研究含接頭位置的電纜紀錄。記住：紀錄只是指引，可能不準確。
教材定位程式：
1. 熟悉定位裝置。
2. 確認已校正。
3. 確認電池與裝置正常。
4. 挖掘前要查某區是否有電纜，可先用接收器在區域掃描；切到 power 模式接收電力電纜的工頻訊號。圖示為接收器姿勢與掃描路徑。
以中指或小指持續壓住開關扳機，讓食指可調頻率／靈敏度。
注意：部分接收器只能拾取承載負載電流之電纜的工頻訊號，可能偵測不到已通電但無負載（如路燈）或三相平衡的電纜。

> 未偵測到訊號不能證明沒有電纜或沒有電；定位與驗明無電是不同證據。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p125。


## PDF p126｜發射器連線與定位掃描

### English OCR

5. To locate and trace a specific cable it is necessary to utilise both receiver and transmitter.

6. Determine which connection mode is most suitable. There are normally 3 options:

a) Placing the signal generator directly on the cable (the transmitter may have an induction mode setting for this situation).

b) Connecting the CT clamp included with the equipment to the transmitter and clamping the CT around the whole cable below the earth bar.

c) Connecting transmitter to the live circuit (some transmitters have fused leads to accommodate this mode whereas others have a 13A plug top which you plug into a socket outlet that is on a circuit emanating from the supply cable that you need to trace.

o

7. Switch the transmitter on and select the correct frequency or mode if this is available to

you.

Note: Lower frequencies should be used for shorter lengths - e.g., 8kHz up to 200m, and

higher frequencies for longer lengths.

8. Confirm the transmitted signal is propagating along the cable by sweeping the receiver over the cable where it is practicable.

Never swing the Locator such that it moves away from the vertical.

9. Some receivers have sensitivity controls. It is preferable to adjust the sensitivity control as you sweep to gain the best results. In non-congested areas sweeping across the cable using a lower sensitivity option will provide 2 minimal with a maximum in between, this maximum being the centre of the cable. When using the device in the highest possible sensitivity, the position of the cable may not be as clearly defined.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

5. 追蹤特定電纜須同時用接收器與發射器。
6. 選適當連線模式，通常三種：a）把訊號產生器直接放在電纜上方，採感應模式；b）附屬 CT 夾接發射器，夾住接地排下方的整條電纜；c）接至帶電迴路，部分有附熔絲測試線，另些可插 13 A 插座，該插座須由待追蹤供電電纜供應。
7. 開啟發射器並選正確頻率／模式。教材說短距離用較低頻率，例如 8 kHz 至 200 m；長距離較高頻率。
8. 可行位置以接收器掃過電纜，確認訊號沿纜傳播。正常掃描不要擺動至偏離垂直。
9. 有靈敏度調整者，掃描時調至最佳。在管線不密集區，以較低靈敏度橫越電纜可得兩個最小值、中間一個最大值；最大處為中心。最高靈敏度反而可能使位置不清楚。

> 頻率與距離及帶電連線方式按本教材保留；選用方式必須符合實際定位器製造商說明，非通用設定。

Visual display when pinpointing services using locator

12.Keep the receiver at 45° and at the same height (ground level is easiest) and sweep transversely across the cable. A maximum or minimum position will be obtained either side of the cable.

為精準定位所需的靈敏度調整量，依模式、訊號強度及管線深度而異。圖示為定位器搜尋時的視覺顯示。
10. 先追蹤路線並依步驟 9 找到電纜中心。

## PDF p128｜區別高壓與低壓電纜

### English OCR

Cable Identification

DistinguishingBetweenLVand HV Cables

1. If cable is plastic over sheathed inspect for voltage rating.

2. If cable is adjacent to an obvious piece of electrical equipment, excavate and undertake visual trace (use of rope noose).

3. A comparison may be made of cable diameters.

Note: Extreme care needs to be taken and thorough knowledge of cables in the vicinity is

required.

4. A comparison may be made of the armours.

Note: Again, a thorough knowledge of the cables is required.

5. Expose all cables adjacent to the cable required to confirm that they are in the correct relative position according to the records.

6. Use signal injection to positively identify any LVcable which is required for work.

7. Use signal injection to positively identify any HVcable that could be mistaken for the required LVcable.

DistinguishingBetweenLVCables

Where more than one LVcable is present it will be necessary to positively identify the required LVcable, this being achieved by signal injection. Alternatively, positive identification may be obtained by temporarily interrupting supply.

/|\ Accuracy of records must never be assumed.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

區分 LV／HV：
1. 有塑膠外護套者檢查電壓標記。
2. 靠近明確裝置時，開挖並目視連續追蹤，教材提到使用繩圈。
3. 可比較外徑，但須極謹慎並充分了解附近電纜。
4. 可比較鎧裝，同樣須充分了解電纜。
5. 露出附近所有電纜，核對相對位置是否符合紀錄。
6. 以訊號注入確切識別待工作的低壓電纜。
7. 同樣識別任何可能被誤認為該 LV 的高壓電纜。
區分多條低壓電纜：須以訊號注入積極識別目標；另一方式是暫時中斷供電確認。
警告：絕不可假設紀錄必然準確。

## PDF p129｜高壓電纜確切識別

### English OCR

HV Cables

1. Cables must be clearly identified. In situations where work is to be initiated without spiking it is essential that a positive identification is made.

2. All normal safety precautions must be followed when identifying cables.

3. Cable spiking, by the approved equipment, may be done (Under the model safety rules), without the issue of a safety document or alternatively whilst a sanction-for-test is outstanding.

4. A sanction-for-test must be issued prior to identifying equipment being connected to the cores of a cable, and also before testing into a spiked cable.

The most appropriate time to issue the sanction-for-test is therefore before any testing or spiking is undertaken.

5. The cable may be positively identified by direct visual tracing from a known circuit termination, such as a cable box of switchgear or a transformer.

6. The cable must be exposed throughout its length from the termination to the point of work.

7. Identification by visual tracing is not acceptable if the cable is hidden from view for any distance, no matter how short.

dote: The use of a rope noose around the cable as a positive marker is recommended where there are several cables.

8. If the circuit termination has been proved dead by an approved voltage indicating device, and connected to earth, then no further means of proving dead are required at the point of work.

9. If a cable terminates within the HVcable box attached directly to an HV/LVtransformer then, for the purpose of proving the HVcable dead, it will be enough to prove the transformer dead by testing for the absence of voltage on its LVside. The transformer should be confirmed dead on the LVafter the earth is applied to the HVSide.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

1. 電纜必須清楚識別；若不刺穿就開始工作，尤其必須積極確證身份。
2. 識別時遵守所有正常安全預防措施。
3. 依本示範規則，使用核准裝置刺穿，可在未發安全檔案時進行，或於 SFT 有效期間進行。
4. 識別器接至線芯前，以及對已刺穿電纜測試前，必須先發 SFT；因此最適當時機是在任何測試或刺穿開始前發出。
5. 可由已知終端（例如開關或變壓器電纜箱）連續目視追蹤以確切識別。
6. 由終端至工作點全長都必須露出。
7. 任一小段被遮蔽，都不能接受單靠目視追蹤。多條電纜時建議以繩圈作確實標記。
8. 若迴路終端已用核准驗電器驗明無電並接地，教材認為工作點不需再用其他方式驗電。
9. 若電纜直接接到 HV／LV 變壓器的高壓端箱，可藉低壓側無電壓測試確認變壓器無電；高壓側接地後，還應在低壓側確認無電。

> 以上為教材特定規則條件，尤其第 3、8、9 點不得抽離連續目視識別、接地及適用程式的前提套用。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p129。


## PDF p130｜中途暴露電纜的識別與刺穿確認

### English OCR

General Identification Procedure

Three basic methods of identification are available for a cable which has been exposed at some intermediate point along its length.

a) Reference to cable records.

b) Signal Injection.

c) Spiking and confirmatory test on the spiked cable.

To be acceptable the identification procedure must ensure that at least 2 of these methods are satisfied.

Unless the cable to be worked on can be visually traced from a circuit termination point, then a careful study of the appropriate cable records must always be made.

o

In most cases, and in particular where the cable is to be cut, method (b) followed by method

(c) is the preferred method of working. When a cable has been spiked the spike should be left in position and a further check made to ensure that the correct cable has in fact been spiked.

Two methods of checking are available:

1. If signal injection equipment has been used, the sender should be left switched on and a further test made for the presence of signal on each side of the spike position. Normal signal level should be found on the sender side of the spike and no signal, or a greatly attenuated signal on the other side (spike is acting as the earth connection).

2. If signal injection equipment has not been used, correct spiking may be confirmed by a further insulation test after all other intentional earths have been disconnected.

A Never assume that because no supplies have gone off that you are on the / correct cable. You may have spiked an old redundant cable that is not shown on the records.

In certain circumstances it may be undesirable to spike the cable because of the resulting damage to it, for example, work on oil-pressure cable, the reinstallation of a joint box, the repair of superficial damage.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

在電纜中途露出的某點，可採三種基本方法：a）參考紀錄；b）訊號注入；c）刺穿及刺穿後確認試驗。可接受的識別至少須符合其中兩種。除可從迴路終端連續目視追蹤者外，均須仔細研究相關紀錄。
多數情況，尤其要切斷電纜時，偏好 b 後接 c。刺穿後應保留刺釘在位，再確認確實刺中正確電纜。
兩種確認方法：
1. 已使用注入訊號者，發射器保持開啟，測刺點兩側；發射器側應有正常訊號，另一側沒有或大幅衰減，因刺釘構成接地。
2. 未用訊號注入者，在斷開所有其他有意施加的接地後再測絕緣，以確認刺穿。
絕不可因沒有任何供電中斷，就假設刺中正確電纜；可能刺到圖上未列的廢棄電纜。
部分情況不宜刺穿以免損傷，例如油壓電纜、重灌接線盒、表面損傷修理；例外條件接下頁。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p130。


## PDF p131｜不刺穿識別的條件與專用識別器

### English OCR

In these instances, the use of signal injection equipment as a means of positive identification is permissible with the following provisions:

a) Only approved cable identifying equipment shall be used.

b) The positive identification must be demonstrated to the person who is to work upon it.

c) Where a joint box or a portion of the cable sheath is to be removed, or the cable cores exposed,this shall be done under a Sanction-for-Test and the PersonalSupervision of the Senior Authorised Person in charge of the work.

ElectricalEquipment Usedfor Identification

Most of the equipment used for identifying dead(isolated) cables operate by detecting the external field produced when current is passed round a loop formed by 2 of the cores of the cable or trefoil under test. The injected current is usually at audio frequency and pulsed to ( d recognition in the presence of power frequencies or interference.

The detection of the applied signal is by means of a search coil applied directly to the serving of the exposed cable. The search coil must have extremely good directional properties so that with proper use it will permit correct discrimination between 2 cables that run side by side and may be very close together.

The equipment must be designed solely as a cable identifier and inappropriate equipment must not be used.

Much of the available equipment is intended primarily for cable route tracing or for fault location and does not meet the specific requirements of a cable identifier.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

承前特殊情況，允許用訊號注入作確切識別，但須：a）只用核准的電纜識別裝置；b）向實際工作者展示確證結果；c）拆接頭箱／護套或露出線芯，必須在 SFT 下由負責 SAP 親自監督。
多數無電／隔離電纜識別器，藉兩根線芯形成迴路，偵測其電流造成的外部場；注入通常為音訊且帶脈衝，以便在工頻／幹擾中辨別。
搜尋線圈直接貼在露出電纜的外被，須具極佳方向性，正確使用時能區分緊貼並行的兩條電纜。
裝置必須是專門的電纜識別器，不可用不適合裝置取代。許多電纜路徑追蹤或故障定位裝置，並不符合確切識別的特定要求。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p131。


## PDF p132｜SFT 後的訊號注入接線（教材案例）

### English OCR

Connecting the SignalInjection Transmitter after the Issue of a Sanction-for-Test

a) To a PlainFeeder

1. Insert test probes at one end.

2. Connect transmitter to 2 cores via test probes.

3. Remove earth at test probes.

4. Confirm signal in the cable adjacent to cable box.

5. Identify cable at point of work.

Do not connect the transmitter between one core and earth as the injected signalwill not remain confined solely to the intended cable circuit.

b) To ATeed Feeder

1. Insert test probes atone end.

2. Connect transmitter to 2 cores via test probes.

3. Remove earth at test probes.

4. Confirm signal in the cable adjacent to cable box.

5. Remove earth on remote end where signal is not required.

6. Identify cable at point of work.

c) To a Transformer Feeder(Switch-fuseOr FuseSwitch)

1. Switch HVswitch-fuse/fuse switch to on.

2. Prove dead at LVincomer contacts.

3. Connect transmitter to 2 cores via LVincomer contacts.

4. Confirm signal in the cable adjacent to HVswitch-fuse/fuse switch.

5. Identify cable at point of work.

The signal will be greatly attenuated because it passes through the transformer, but a definitive identification can still be made.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

取得 SFT 後，教材列下列方法。
a）單純饋線：1 插入一端測試探棒；2 經探棒將發射器接兩根線芯；3 移除探棒處接地；4 在電纜箱附近確認訊號；5 在工作點識別。不可接在單一線芯與地之間，否則訊號不會只侷限於目標電纜迴路。
b）T 接饋線：1–4 同上；5 移除不需要訊號之遠端的接地；6 在工作點識別。
c）變壓器饋線（switch fuse／fuse switch）：1 高壓開關熔絲合至 ON；2 在低壓進線接點驗明無電；3 經低壓進線接點將發射器接兩根導體；4 在高壓開關熔絲附近電纜確認訊號；5 在工作點識別。
訊號經變壓器會大幅衰減，但教材認為仍可明確識別。

> 本頁是完整隔離／接地情境下的教材接線練習，不是可直接執行的現場切換或測試程式。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p132。


## PDF p133｜使用電纜識別接收器

### English OCR

Using the Signal Injection Receiver

1. Apply the face of the search coil to all cables at the point of identification.

2. Move the search coil completely round the circumference of each cable to check whether it is emitting a signal.

3. The amplitude of the signal from the wanted cable will vary as the search coil is moved circumferentially (or longitudinally) due to the position of the cores in the cable (twist) and at a maximum point will greatly exceed the signal obtained from any other cable in the vicinity.

4. Position the search coil so that the face is pointing away from the cable.

c

5. Again,rotate the receiver circumferentially round the cable.

6. This will confirm whether the signal is coming from an unexposed cable at the side or underneath the cable under test.

7. With a trefoil group of single core cables,the search coil should be placed along the hollow formed between each pair of cores.

8. The hollow between the 2 cores carrying signal current well give the greatest amplitude of signal.

9. Change the connection at the transmitter end and repeat the test.

10.The hollow carrying the greatest signal will change if the cable is the correct one.

Note: The phase identities of the single cores can be determined prior to exposingthe cores.

No change in signal amplitude with longitudinal movement will be apparent with single core cables (no twist).

Always use the minimum sender output which will give a distinctive signal at the point of identification. The objective is to use just sufficient output to give an adequate signal when the search coil is applied directly to the serving of the wanted cable.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

1. 把搜尋線圈面貼近識別點所有電纜。
2. 繞每條全圓周移動，檢查是否發訊號。
3. 目標電纜因線芯絞合，訊號隨圓周／縱向位置變動；最大值應顯著大於附近其他電纜。
4. 再把線圈面朝離電纜方向。
5. 重新繞圓周移動。
6. 藉此確認訊號是否其實來自旁側／下方尚未露出的電纜。
7. 三葉排列單芯電纜，把線圈放在各對線芯之間凹槽。
8. 承載注入電流的兩芯之間，訊號應最大。
9. 改變發射端連線並重測。
10. 若電纜正確，最大訊號凹槽會隨之改變。
單芯相別可在露芯前判別；單芯沒有絞合，沿縱向移動不會有相同的幅度變化。
始終使用能在工作點明確辨認的最低發射輸出，只需讓線圈直接貼到目標外被時取得足夠訊號。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p133。


## PDF p134｜停用電纜識別與回收切割

### English OCR

Identification of Disused Cables

When an HVcable has been disused for a period or is disconnected from the system, and it is required to identify it at some point along its length, the following steps must be taken to enable tests to be made:

1. All capped ends must be removed, and the cores insulated from each other and the sheath.

2. If a pot-end is to be opened or removed, then the cable must first be spiked within a visually traced distance from the end.

3. The insulation resistance of the cable must be checked to confirm that there is no possibility of an unsuspected fault on the cable which would cause ambiguity in the identification tests or confirmatory tests after the cable has been spiked.

Recovery of Cables Disconnected from The System

It may not always be possible to positively identify a cable when the cut ends are not available for test. In this instance, after a careful study of the appropriate records the cable shall be spiked.

After the cable has been cut it is usually necessary to further cut the cable during the recovery process. This additional cutting of the cable shall only be permitted where it is possible to trace visually or use a rope noose from the point of cable cut to the next position where a cut is required. Where this is not possible the cable shall not be cut until it has again been spiked.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

高壓電纜停用一段時間或已與系統斷開，若要在中途識別，教材要求：
1. 移除所有封帽端，線芯彼此及與護套絕緣。
2. 若要開啟／移除封端（pot-end），先在可由該端連續目視追蹤的距離內刺穿。
3. 測絕緣電阻，排除未察覺故障造成識別或刺穿後確認結果混淆。
已脫離系統電纜的回收：切斷端無法取得供試驗時，未必能確切識別；此時研究紀錄後須刺穿。
回收常需再切多處，只有可從既有切點目視追蹤或以繩圈追蹤至下一切點時，才可再切；否則須重新刺穿才可切斷。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p134。


## PDF p135｜電纜刺穿裝置

### English OCR

Spiking of Cables

When deciding to spike cables you must first take into consideration of the following point.

Where underground cables cannot be positively identified and proved dead at the point of work, it may be necessary to spike the cable using a properly designed, cartridge-operated spiking gun.

There are various types of cable spiking device here are some of them:

Cartridge Operated Type Captive Chisel

Hydraulic Type

Which of the 3 examples do you think meets the requirements of HSG 85?

### 繁體中文學習譯文

決定刺穿前須考量：地下電纜若不能在工作點確切識別並驗明無電，可能需用適當設計、火藥筒驅動的刺穿槍。
本頁展示三例：火藥筒操作型（Cartridge operated）；受拘束鑿刀型（Captive chisel）；液壓型（Hydraulic）。
教材提問：這三個例子中，你認為哪個符合 HSG85 要求？
本頁未提供答案，圖片與構造請看掃描。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p135。


## PDF p136｜刺穿前檢核與程式示例

### English OCR

The following should also be considered:

• The implementation of safety paperwork to allow spiking of the cable

• Have you been correctly trained in its use?

• Have you the correct Personal protective equipment

• Have you tried all ways of identifying the cable?

• Have you checked with the DNO to see if they have any cabling in the area?

Procedure for spikinga cable

1. Consult the site drawings and any other document that may show any cable routes.

2. Carry out cable avoidance tool survey to establish any existing routes of cables.

3. Excavate the cable to expose the cable to be worked on.

4. Usingcable tracing to positively identify the cable.

o

At this point you will have to ascertain that this is the correct cable. If you cannotbe 100% certainsoyou will have to spike the cable

5. All safety paperwork is to be prepared

6. Inspect the spiking gun in preparation of its use

7. Assemble the spiking gun on the cable where you wish to spike

8. Ensure there is a safety person present

9. Ensure that all correct personal protective equipment (PPE)is in place

10.Operate the spiking gun

11.Approach the spiking gun with caution wearing the correct PPE 12.Check for any voltage that may be present as a precaution / 13.If no voltage found then dismantle the spike gun 14.Issue any safety paperwork that is required 15.Spiking of a cable effects the system therefor should be entered into the site log book 16.Strip and clean spikeing gun before storing

### 繁體中文學習譯文

應考慮：允許刺穿的安全檔案；是否受過正確使用訓練；PPE 是否適當；是否已嘗試所有識別方式；是否向 DNO 查詢區內電纜。
教材程式：
1 查場址圖及路徑檔案；2 用避纜工具調查既有路徑；3 開挖露出目標；4 追蹤並確切識別。若此時仍不能百分之百確定，原文指需刺穿。
5 準備所有安全檔案；6 使用前檢查刺穿槍；7 裝到選定刺點；8 確認監護人在場；9 確認適當 PPE；10 操作刺穿槍；11 穿正確 PPE 謹慎接近；12 檢查可能存在電壓；13 若無電壓，拆卸刺穿槍；14 發所需安全檔案；15 刺穿影響系統，記入 Logbook；16 拆洗刺穿槍後存放。

> 本頁第 13 步拆槍與 PDF p130 刺釘保留並確認的細節須合併向講師釐清，不能自行拼成現場 SOP。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p136。


## PDF p137｜高壓裝置試運轉與試驗電壓

### English OCR

Testing High-voltage Installations

1. The Commissioningof New or Modified Circuits

1.1.Pressure testing should be carried out after extensions, modifications, alterations, or repairs have been carried out to the high-voltage conductors of the distribution system.

1.2.DEO(Works) recommend that whenever practicable pressure testing be carried out using double isolations between any energised conductors and conductors under test.

1.2.1. This is always possible at a withdrawable circuit breaker position.

1.2.2. Requires two switches to be isolated at Distribution sub-stations.

1.2.3. When double isolation is used, any conductors between the isolation points should be earthed.

2. Test Voltages

2.1 Distribution cable should be tested to BSrecommended values (seeTable 1).

2.2 When testing cables terminated to switchgear the test voltage may be limited by the manufacturers recommended test values for the switchgear. Most modern switchgear, however, is so designed that those parts which cannot be readily disconnected are capable of withstanding the cable test voltage. The following switchgear meets these requirements:

2.2.1 Circuit Breaker fixed portions complying with BS5227.

2.2.2 Oil Switches on RMU's complying with ESIS41-12.

2.2.3 Switchgear should be pressure tested to BSrecommended values (seeTable 2). The test voltage can be either ACor DCand is applied both between conductors and earth.

2.2.4 When testing older types of switchgear, it may be necessary to de-rate the test voltages. Switchgear manufacturers should be consulted (giving age, type, serial number etc) for maximum recommended test pressures.

2.2.5 An insulation resistance tester (1000V) should always be applied prior to a pressure test.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

1. 新建／變更迴路試運轉。
1.1 配電系統高壓導體擴建、改動或修理後，應耐壓測試。
1.2 DEO (Works) 建議可行時，帶電導體與被測導體間使用雙重隔離。
1.2.1 可抽出式斷路器位置可達成；1.2.2 配電站需隔離兩個開關；1.2.3 兩隔離點中間導體應接地。
2. 試驗電壓。
2.1 配電電纜按 BS 建議值（表 1）。
2.2 電纜連著開關裝置測試時，電壓可能受製造商開關裝置額定限制。教材稱多數現代裝置不易斷開的部分可承受電纜試驗電壓，包括：2.2.1 符合 BS 5227 的斷路器固定部；2.2.2 符合 ESI 41-12 的 RMU 油開關。
2.2.3 開關裝置按 BS 建議值（表 2）耐壓，可用 AC／DC，施於導體間及導體對地。
2.2.4 舊裝置可能須降額，應提供年齡、型式、序號等詢問製造商最高建議試驗電壓。
2.2.5 耐壓前先以 1000 V 絕緣電阻測試器檢查。

> 本章引用歷史標準與機關名稱，試驗值與方法須另核對當前適用版本及裝置條件。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p137。

3. Phase Polarity Tests (Phasing Out) On HV Circuits

3.1 Ring Circuits

Phasing out can be carried out in two ways:

3.1.1 3.11 LIVE - Using a potential indicator and phasing rod, at a withdrawable circuit breaker position.

3.1.2 3.12 DEAD - Under a "SANCTION TO TEST", using a continuity tester. This method should be used if testing from an oil switch.

Note: It will be necessary to check the continuity before commencing any work, in order to

ascertain initial conditions, and again on completion.

3.2 Radial Circuits

For radial feeders, the phase rotation can be checked on the LV side of the transformer.

## PDF p143｜操作程式表常用動詞

### English OCR

Abbreviations Description

Inform Inform shift controller/Process Controller of proposed work or status of system Switch to on Operate circuit breaker/Contactor/Switch to energise circuit Switch to Off Operate circuit breaker/Contactor/switch to de-energise circuit Switch to Earth Operate switch to earth cable Rack Down Rack down circuit breaker and remove truck

Rack Up

Rack up circuit breaker but must state whether in: Service, bus bar earth or circuit earth positions Lock/Label Fix safety locks and danger and/or caution signs Spike Cable Spiking cable to confirm dead

Update Mimic diagram or switch status board Prove Dead Test with potential indicator Isolate Switch to off and secure

c

### 繁體中文學習譯文

Inform：通知輪班／製程控制員預定工作或系統狀態。
Switch to ON：操作斷路器、接觸器或開關使迴路通電。
Switch to OFF：操作裝置使迴路斷電。
Switch to EARTH：操作開關使電纜接地。
Rack down：退出斷路器並移出臺車。
Rack up：推入斷路器，必須寫明運轉、匯流排接地或迴路接地位置。
Lock／Label：裝安全鎖及危險／警示牌。
Spike cable：刺穿電纜確認無電。
Issue：發出安全檔案。
Key safe：將鑰匙、連片及安全檔案副本放入鑰匙保管箱。
Cancel：取消安全檔案。
Update：更新模擬圖或開關狀態板。
Prove dead：用驗電器測試。
Isolate：切至 OFF 並確保其狀態。

> 簡表用語不能替代完整隔離定義；須連同所有電源、鎖定及證據要求閱讀。

VT Voltage Transformer (see also PT)

XLPE Cross Linked Polyethylene Insulated Cable

### 繁體中文學習譯文

AE｜Authorising Engineer｜授權工程師
SAP｜Senior Authorised Person｜資深授權人員
CP｜Competent Person｜具能力人員
CT｜Current Transformer｜電流互感器
DSS｜Distribution Substation｜配電變電站
DNO｜Distribution Network Operator｜配電網營運商
HRC｜High Rupturing Capacity｜高遮斷容量
Hz｜Hertz｜赫茲，每秒週期數
IDMT｜Inverse Definite Minimum Time｜反時限、定最小時間
ISS｜Intake Substation｜進線變電站
kA｜Kiloamps｜千安培，例如 4000 A = 4 kA
kV｜Kilovolts｜千伏特，例如 6600 V = 6.6 kV
MCCB｜Moulded Case Circuit Breaker｜模殼斷路器
MVA｜Megavolt-Amps｜百萬伏安
OCB｜Oil Circuit Breaker｜油斷路器
PME｜Protective Multiple Earthing｜保護性多點接地
PIC｜Person in charge｜負責人
PSM｜Plug Setting Multiplier｜插頭設定倍數（IDMT）
PS%｜Plug Setting Percentage｜插頭設定百分比（IDMT）
RMU｜Ring Main Unit｜環網單元
SBSS｜Standby Substation｜備用電源變電站
TLF｜Time Limit Fuse｜時限熔絲
TMS｜Time Multiplier Setting｜時間倍率設定（IDMT）
UPS｜原文 Uninterrupted Power Supplies｜不斷電電源
VCB｜Vacuum Circuit Breaker｜真空斷路器
VT｜Voltage Transformer（另見 PT）｜電壓互感器
XLPE｜Cross Linked Polyethylene Insulated Cable｜交連聚乙烯絕緣電纜



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p144。

### English OCR

### 繁體中文學習譯文

本頁為隔離與接地圖（Isolation and Earthing Diagram）的標準符號，形狀及位置請對照原始掃描。
斷路器（Circuit breaker）：合閘 ON、分閘 OFF、電纜／迴路已接地、母排已接地。
熔絲開關／開關熔絲組合（Fuse switch／Switch fuse）：ON、OFF、已接地。
油開關（Oil switch）：ON、OFF、已接地。
其他符號：變壓器、發電機、配電柱（Feeder pillar）一般符號、配電柱出線已隔離及接地、配電柱進線已隔離及接地。
這些符號表示的狀態不同，讀圖時須同時辨識裝置型式、開關位置、隔離點與接地位置。



來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p145。

TREAT ALLOTHER EQUIPMENT ASLIVE

### 繁體中文學習譯文

高壓測試許可檔案（Sanction-for-Test，SFT）。場址、編號。
第 1 部分：簽發。致收件人。下列高壓裝置已按配電安全規則處理，使其可安全進行測試。所有其他裝置均視為帶電。
隔離點：各點均施加安全鎖與警告牌。
危險告示：設於鄰近帶電裝置；不需要時填 NONE。
主接地（Circuit Main Earths，CME）：除為測試需拆除者外，均加安全鎖。
測試期間可拆除的接地：加工作鎖（Working locks）。
預定測試的簡述。
特殊指示：沒有時填 NONE。
SAP 姓名、簽名、時間、日期。
本頁為空白檔案範本，沒有指定實際裝置、隔離或接地位置。

第 3 部分：清場（Clearance）。本人及所負責的所有人員均已撤離，並已獲告知不可再安全地測試本 SFT 所述裝置。測試已完成／未完成；所有工具與裝置已／未移走。除下列例外外，裝置運轉狀態與本 SFT 簽發時相同。刪除不適用專案；沒有例外時填 NONE。
第 4 部分：登出（Cancellation）。本 SFT 已登出。SAP 姓名、簽名、時間、日期。

## PDF p150｜York MOD 高壓環網電纜改道情境題

### English OCR

York MOD HV Ring Diversion Exercise

The Scenario

You are the authorised person for the MOD High-voltage Open RingMain. It has been decided that a new building is to be erected on your site. The area that has been chosen for the new building will cross the route of your existing high-voltage cable.

You must now make the necessary preparations to divert the high-voltage cable so that it is out with the footprint of the new building

NOTE:

1. The cable that must be diverted is from the intake substation to distribution substation A.

2. There is a single line diagram attached to this paperwork.

The Task

Complete the following documentation as per Develop's High-voltage Safety Rules and Procedures:

a) TheSwitchingSchedule and Isolation and Earthing Diagram for the activities/operations you perform to enable a Permit to Work to be issued for the cable diversion work to be carried out.

b) TheSite Logfor the activities/operations you should perform up to and including the issue of the Permit to Work.

c) Parts1 and 2 of the Permit to Work.

d) TheSwitching Schedule and Isolation and Earthing Diagram for the activities/operations you perform to enable a Sanction to Test to be issued for the cable testing to be carried

out.

e) TheSite Logfor the activities/operations you should perform up to and including the issue of the Sanction to Test.

f) TheSwitching Schedule for the activities/operations you perform to carry out live phasingand restore the system to normal to be carried out.

g) TheSite Logfor the activities/operations you should perform up to and including the restoration of the system to normal.

### 繁體中文學習譯文

你是 MOD 開環高壓配電系統的 Authorised Person。新建築範圍跨越既有電纜，因此需將電纜改道至建築範圍以外。需改道的是 ISS 至 DSS A 的電纜；隨附單線圖。
準備下列檔案：
a）施工用切換程式及隔離與接地圖。
b）截至簽發 PTW 為止的場址操作紀錄（Site log）。
c）PTW 的第 1、2 部分。
d）測試用切換程式及隔離與接地圖。
e）截至簽發 SFT 為止的場址操作紀錄。
f）帶電對相與恢復系統所需的切換程式。
g）截至系統恢復為止的場址操作紀錄。
以下各頁是此教材情境的檔案示例；練習時應交叉核對裝置名稱、檔案編號、接地狀態及先後順序。

> 教材案例；學習用途，不可直接作為現場操作指令。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p150。

BURN HALL HV OPEN RING SYSTEM

電源由 DNO 接入 ISS；環路依序連線 ISS—DSS A—DSS B—DSS C—DSS D—DSS E—DSS F—ISS。DSS C 朝 DSS D 的開關標示為正常開點（Normal open point）。
ISS 至 DSS A 的既有電纜穿越新建築範圍（New build）；虛線顯示繞過新建築的電纜改道路徑（Cable diversion）。
圖中另示各站變壓器、高壓保護／開關及低壓側連線；精確符號與各位置以原圖為準。

### 繁體中文學習譯文

MOD RING，11 kV；程式 267487，第 1／2 張。裝置：ISS 與 DSS A 之間的高壓電纜。目的：插入新電纜及兩個直通接頭，繞過新建築。SAP A PERSON；覆核者 C A PERSON；日期為 XX 佔位。
1. SAP 辦公室：簽到接任，核對模擬圖。
2. DSS C、通往 DSS D 的 SF₆ 開關：合閘 ON，關閉正常開點；需鑰匙。
3. DSS A、通往 ISS 的油開關：分閘 OFF，加安全鎖及警告牌，開始隔離電纜；需鑰匙、鎖及告示。
4. ISS、通往 DSS A 的油斷路器：分閘，降低／抽出斷路器車體；母排遮板加安全鎖與警告牌，完成隔離；需操作鑰匙、鎖及告示。
5. ISS 的迴路插孔：以高壓驗電器確認電纜無電；需驗電器、驗證單元及監護人。
6. ISS：將斷路器移至迴路接地位置並合閘，跳脫手柄加安全鎖，使電纜接地；需鑰匙、手柄及鎖。

> 教材切換示例，須連同下一頁及隔離接地圖閱讀；學習用途，不可直接作為現場操作指令。

### 繁體中文學習譯文

MOD RING，11 kV；程式 26749，第 1／1 張。擬開始／完成日期 XX。SAP A PERSON；覆核 SAP C A PERSON。
本程式將使其可安全施工／測試的裝置：不適用。工作描述：在 ISS 通往 DSS A 的油斷路器進行帶電對相，並恢復 DSS C 通往 DSS D 的正常開點。
1. ISS、通往 DSS A 的油斷路器：分閘，解除電纜接地；需接地工作鎖鑰匙。
2. DSS A、通往 ISS 的油開關：分閘，解除另一端接地；需接地工作鎖鑰匙。
3. DSS A 同一開關：合閘，對通往 ISS 的電纜送電；需操作鑰匙。
4. ISS 同一斷路器：降低／抽出並移離櫃體，在迴路側與母排側間帶電對相；需對相棒及監護人。
5. ISS：斷路器移入運轉位置並合閘，閉合環網；需操作鑰匙。
6. DSS C、通往 DSS D 的 SF₆ 開關：分閘，恢復開環與正常開點；需操作鑰匙。

> 工作描述將 DSS C 裝置稱為 Oil switch，第 6 步稱為 SF₆ switch；此名稱差異保留供核對。學習用途，不可直接作為現場操作指令。

## PDF p180｜IDMT 測試情境練習

### English OCR

IDMT Test Exercise

The Scenario

You are the authorised person for the HV Distribution Systems shown in Figure1.

I.S.S.

E.A.S. S.B.S.S.

Ring Ring

FEEDER

PILLAR

Figure 1- HV DistributionSystem

It is necessary to carry out the routine testing of the protection fitted to the incoming supply oil circuit breaker. Details of the protection are given on a Switchgear and Transformer Schedule,and details of the tests are typically given in a JobSheet, for example PSA job sheet 22.103.

Will this testing interrupt the supply to the client, if so, what arrangements would you tak prior to the commencement of the test?

2. If you are carrying out the work yourself, what paperwork you would issuefor the job, and what safety measures you would take?

3. What would be different if a contractor was carrying out this testing on your behalf?

### 繁體中文學習譯文

你是圖 1 所示高壓配電系統的 Authorised Person。圖中有 ISS、EAS、SBSS、環網（Ring）及配電柱（Feeder pillar）；完整接線看原圖。
現在必須對進線油斷路器的保護做例行測試。保護資料載於《Switchgear and Transformer Schedule》（開關裝置及變壓器清冊）；試驗細節通常載於工作單，例如 PSA 工作單 22.103。
1. 這項測試會中斷客戶供電嗎？如果會，測試開始前你會做什麼安排？
2. 若由你本人執行，會為工作簽發什麼檔案，並採取哪些安全措施？
3. 若由承包商代表你進行測試，做法有何不同？

## PDF p181｜IDMT 測試記錄表與接線示意

### English — OCR / scan transcription

IDMT test record

Columns in reading order:
Phase | Test Amps | Creep Test | Test Amps | Plug Setting | PSM | TMS | Time (Graph) | Time 1 (Relay) | Time 2 (Relay) | Time 3 (Relay) | Time (Avg) | S/NS.
Rows: Red; Yellow; Blue; one unlabelled row.
Circuit Breaker Final Trip Test.

Diagram labels:
Plug bridge shorted to highest setting when plug is withdrawn.
Tap currents: 2.5, 3.75, 5, 6.25, 7.5, 8.75, 10 A.
Settings: 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, 175, 200%.
TRIP COIL; BATTERY; CTs; Small wiring panel; Relays; OC; EF; R; Y; B.
[Consult original image for wiring and handwritten test values.]

### 繁體中文學習譯文

測試記錄表的欄位依序為：相別（Phase）、測試電流 A（供爬行測試）、爬行測試（Creep Test）、測試電流 A、插頭設定（Plug Setting）、插頭設定倍數（PSM）、時間倍率設定（TMS）、曲線預估時間、繼電器時間 1／2／3、平均時間、S／NS。
相別列：紅相 Red、黃相 Yellow、藍相 Blue，以及另一空白列。另有斷路器最終跳脫測試（Circuit Breaker Final Trip Test）欄。
圖中插頭抽出時，插頭橋會短接至最高設定。抽頭標示：2.5、3.75、5、6.25、7.5、8.75、10 A，對應 50、75、100、125、150、175、200%。
左圖示示跳脫線圈（Trip coil）及電池（Battery）；右圖為 CT、二次小配線端子板（Small wiring panel）、過電流繼電器 OC、接地故障繼電器 EF，以及 R／Y／B 三相。
接線關係及原件手寫測試值請放大掃描檢視。

> 已檢視原始掃描核對欄序與圖中文字。手寫設定及秒數未當成核定測試資料；S／NS 的完整定義本頁未展開，保留原縮寫。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p181。


## PDF p182｜IDMT 標準反時限曲線

### English — OCR / scan transcription

I.D.M.T. RELAY STANDARD INVERSE CURVE
Drawn for T.M.S. of 1.

Vertical axis: TIME (seconds).
Horizontal axis: PLUG SETTING MULTIPLIER (I_test / I_plug setting).
[The plotted curve and scales are preserved in the original page image.]

### 繁體中文學習譯文

IDMT 繼電器標準反時限曲線（Standard Inverse Curve）。本圖依 TMS = 1 繪製。
縱軸：時間，單位秒（TIME, seconds）。
橫軸：插頭設定倍數（Plug Setting Multiplier），即測試電流 I_test 除以插頭設定電流 I_plug setting。
圖中可見：倍數增大時，曲線對應的動作時間縮短。讀取具體時間須使用原圖刻度；本閱讀器未以 OCR 或自行推算值取代曲線。

> 已核對原始掃描的 TMS、軸標與比值；橫向圖請用『原始掃描』放大閱讀。

來源：Textbook_OCR.pdf／Textbook.PDF，PDF p182。
